### [1] [EmbodiedSmith: Scaling Embodied Data through Recursive Self-Improvement Flywheel in Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07969)
*Yikai Qin,Yifei Deng,Mingjian Liang,Wenxuan Song,Zepeng Lin,Zhiyi Jiang,Jiajun Fu,Qiao Sun,Huashuo Lei,Xicheng Gong,Jiayi Chen,Han Zhao,Shuanghao Bai,Pengxiang Ding,Pengwei Wang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EmbodiedSmith 是一个通过递归自我改进实现可扩展具身数据生成的框架，统一资产、场景与任务生成，支持自主创建和语言驱动定制，并提升任务生成成功率与下游策略泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型的扩展需要多样训练数据和可靠评估环境；仿真虽可扩展，但现有生成流程受限于预定义资产与技能、场景生成与任务生成脱节，以及对复杂本体和物理的支持不足。

Method: 提出 EmbodiedSmith，将资产生成、场景生成和任务生成统一到一条支持自主创建与语言驱动定制的流程中。其核心是智能体式精炼循环：场景生成预判下游任务需求，任务生成引导针对性场景编辑，使场景与任务相互迭代改进；同时支持移动机械臂、人形机器人、灵巧手，以及可变形物体和流体交互。

Result: 联合精炼提高了任务生成成功率，包括长时程任务；大量实验验证了生成数据的质量、多样性和生成效率；下游策略实验表明，数据多样性增加可提升泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架为机器人预训练和评估提供了一个灵活的仿真引擎，可生成涵盖更广泛行为与物理现象的数据。

Abstract: Scaling robotic foundation models requires diverse training data and reliable evaluation environments. Simulation offers a scalable solution, yet existing generation pipelines remain constrained by predefined assets and skills, a disconnect between scene generation and task generation, and limited support for complex embodiments and physics. We introduce EmbodiedSmith, a framework for scalable embodied data generation through recursive self-improvement (RSI). EmbodiedSmith unifies asset, scene, and task generation in a pipeline that supports autonomous creation and language-driven customization. Its core is an agentic refinement loop: scene generation anticipates downstream task requirements, while task generation guides targeted scene edits, allowing scenes and tasks to iteratively improve one another. This joint refinement improves task generation success, including for long-horizon tasks. The framework further supports mobile manipulators, humanoids, and dexterous hands, as well as interactions involving deformable objects and fluids, broadening the range of behaviors and physical phenomena represented in generated data. Together, these capabilities provide a flexible simulation engine for both robot pretraining and evaluation. Extensive experiments validate the quality, diversity, and generation efficiency of the resulting data, while downstream policy experiments demonstrate that increased data diversity improves generalization.

</details>


### [2] [R2RI: A Multi-View Event and RGB Dataset for Robot-to-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07117)
*Gabriele Magrini,Riccardo Catalini,Federico Becattini,Guido Borghi,Pietro Pala,Roberto Vezzani,Lorenzo Seidenari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出首个面向机器人-机器人交互（RRI）的大规模数据集R2RI，包含多类人形机器人、基于真实人类社会行为建模的交互，以及自我中心和外中心互补视角；数据集超650万帧、约5000段120fps视频，涵盖事件与RGB模态，并公开数据与标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解与建模自主智能体之间的交互是机器人学的基础挑战，对协作系统、社交机器人和人机共处具有重要意义。然而，机器人交互研究因缺乏大规模基准而进展严重受阻。

Method: 构建Robot-to-Robot Interaction（R2RI）数据集：采用不同人形机器人和真实人类社交行为建模的交互；提供每个机器人机载传感器的自我中心视角和外部固定相机的外中心视角，以支持丰富的空间与上下文理解；数据包含Event和RGB域；针对多项关键感知与交互任务比较当前最先进方法。

Result: R2RI包含超过650万帧、约5000段120fps视频，涵盖Event和RGB域；论文分析了各域优缺点，并在若干感知和交互任务上对比了SOTA方法；数据集及其所有任务和模态的标注已公开。

Conclusion: R2RI填补了RRI任务缺乏大规模基准的空白，为机器人交互、协作系统、社交机器人及人机共处研究提供了重要数据资源与评测基础。

Abstract: Understanding and modeling interactions between autonomous agents is a fundamental challenge in robotics, with broad implications for collaborative systems, social robotics, and human-robot coexistence. Although the study of robot interactions has emerged as a compelling research direction, progress has been severely hampered by the absence of large-scale benchmarks. In this paper, we introduce Robot-to-Robot Interaction (R2RI), the first dataset specifically designed to address the Robot-Robot Interaction (RRI) task. R2RI consists of different humanoid robots and realistic interactions modeled on real human social behaviors. Complementary viewpoints are available, \textit{i.e.}, an egocentric perspective from each robot's onboard sensors, and an exocentric perspective from external fixed cameras, thus enabling rich spatial and contextual understanding of the interaction dynamics. The dataset comprises more than $6.5$M frames and $\approx5000$ videos at $120$ fps, including Event and RGB domains. We investigate pros and cons of each domain, comparing state-of-the-art approaches for a number of key sensing and interaction based tasks. We publicly release the dataset and its annotations for all tasks and modalities at this https URL .

</details>


### [3] [Hierarchy-GBP: Accelerating Factor Graph Inference via Abstraction and Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06978)
*Yuzhou Cheng,Tom Yates,Ignacio Alzugaray,Danyal Akarca,Pedro A. M. Mediano,Andrew J. Davison*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Hierarchy-GBP（H-GBP），一种两阶段迭代框架：先用粗图抽象求解 GBP 的全局误差，再回投到原图，并用标准 GBP 精修局部误差；理论证明收敛，并在 PGO/BA 上显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gaussian Belief Propagation（GBP）可分布式、可扩展，适合空间智能，但其局部修正高效，全局误差需沿长程消息传播逐步修正，导致跨远距离图区域收敛慢。

Method: 利用 GBP 误差的局部与全局特性，H-GBP 每轮先通过粗图近似（abstraction）求解全局误差，再通过 recovery 投影回原图，随后用 GBP 细化剩余局部误差；通过推导抽象与恢复的组合矩阵算子并分析其谱半径，证明 H-GBP 收敛到最优解。

Result: 在线性稀疏图上，H-GBP 收敛速度本质上快于标准 GBP；在 Pose Graph Optimization（PGO）上大幅加速大规模问题，在 Bundle Adjustment（BA）的所有测试尺度上达到 state-of-the-art 运行时间。

Conclusion: H-GBP 通过层次化抽象-恢复与 GBP 精修，有效解决 GBP 全局误差传播慢的问题，兼具理论收敛保证与实际空间优化任务中的显著加速效果。

Abstract: Gaussian Belief Propagation (GBP) is a distributed inference algorithm that passes messages in graphical models, making it attractive for scalable spatial intelligence. However, we find GBP most effective locally: it rapidly smooths message errors that vary sharply between neighbor variables, but corrects global errors across distant graph regions incrementally through long-range message propagations. We propose Hierarchy-GBP (H-GBP), an iterative, two-stage framework that accelerates GBP by first solving these global errors with a coarse graph approximation (abstraction) and projecting the results back to the original graph (recovery), then refining the remaining local errors with GBP. We prove H-GBP convergence to the optimum by deriving the combined matrix operator of our abstraction and recovery steps and analyzing its spectral radius. Experiments on linear sparse graphs show that H-GBP converges fundamentally faster than standard GBP. Moreover, we validate H-GBP on two important spatial problems: Pose Graph Optimization (PGO) and Bundle Adjustment (BA). H-GBP markedly accelerates large-scale PGO and achieves state-of-the-art runtime across all tested BA scales.

</details>

### [4] [Statistical Turbulence and High-Fidelity Disturbance Fields for Quadrotor Flight Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06874)
*Xun Huang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文系统评估风场保真度对四旋翼强化学习控制器鲁棒性的影响，在五种扰动保真度下对PPO进行全交叉训练-测试，发现最廉价的离散阵风域随机化已足够，鲁棒性主要由控制权限而非风场真实性决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习四旋翼控制器通常在简化风模型下训练，但风场保真度（而非仅风速大小）对策略鲁棒性的影响尚未量化，因此需要明确高保真风场建模是否值得投入。

Method: 在悬停的竞速级四旋翼上，比较五种扰动保真度：无风、离散1-cosine阵风、统计湍流、合成相干结构以及大气边界层大涡模拟（LES）场；对PPO智能体进行全交叉保真度训练-测试，以级联PID和几何SE(3)控制器作为免训练参考，并在0–12 m/s风速范围内扫描；在控制器比较前，先定量验证所有扰动数据：合成生成器对照解析或认证标准，LES场对照 imposed log law。

Result: 训练-测试矩阵非常平坦，最便宜的结构化训练风（离散阵风域随机化）在所有测试列中排名第一，并在风敏感的27 g平台上复现；一次调参的几何控制器以零坠毁率包络学习到的PPO策略；机理诊断表明，限制鲁棒性的是控制权限而非风场真实性。

Conclusion: 风场保真度的投入应与平台风敏感性相匹配；当控制权限足够时，高保真风场并非必要，低成本结构化域随机化即可获得稳健的四旋翼强化学习控制策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning quadrotor controllers are usually trained under simplified wind models, yet the impact of wind-field fidelity, as opposed to magnitude, on policy robustness remains unquantified. This paper compares five disturbance-fidelity levels, from wind-free flight and discrete 1-cosine gusts through statistical turbulence and synthetic coherent structures to large-eddy-simulation fields of the atmospheric boundary layer, in a full cross-fidelity train test evaluation of proximal policy optimization (PPO) agents, with cascaded PID and geometric SE(3) controllers as training-free references, over a 0-12 m/s wind sweep. Before any controller comparison is made, all disturbance data are validated: every synthetic generator is checked quantitatively against its analytical or certification-standard reference, and the large-eddy-simulation fields against the imposed log law. On a racing-class quadrotor in hover, the train test matrix is remarkably flat, and the cheapest structured training wind, which is discrete-gust domain randomization, ranks first in every test column, a ranking replicated on a wind-sensitive 27 g platform; a once-tuned geometric controller brackets the learned PPO policies at zero crash rate. Mechanism diagnostics show that control authority, not wind realism, bounds robustness, so wind-fidelity investment should scale with platform wind sensitivity.

</details>

### [5] [Revisiting Temporal Regularization for Smooth Control in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07910)
*SungJae Ahn,Jeong Woon Lee,Kyoleen Kwak,Hyoseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 深度强化学习策略会产生动作振荡，影响实机部署。作者证明时间平滑惩罚可带来空间平滑效应，并提出CATS：仅用时间平滑正则并结合线性递增权重，在不牺牲任务性能的情况下显著减少动作振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有架构设计与惩罚方法通过直接降低策略对状态输入的敏感性来追求空间平滑，但过强约束会损害任务性能；而时间正则化虽约束沿观测转移的动作差异，却被认为在观测噪声下无法提供所需空间平滑。本文重新审视并挑战这一假设。

Method: 作者证明时间惩罚能够约束具有同一下一状态的两个当前状态之间的期望动作差，从而揭示其空间平滑效应，并在经验上扩展到空间平滑。基于此提出CATS（Conditioning for Action using only Temporal Smoothness），将时间惩罚与线性ramp-up结合：先让策略学习高回报行为，再逐步增强平滑约束。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，CATS能大幅减少动作振荡且不降低任务性能，计算开销很小；线性ramp-up还改善了回报保持以及时间与空间平滑性。

Conclusion: 时间正则化可有效提供空间平滑，并比显式空间正则化更好地保持任务性能；CATS是一种简单、低开销且适用于实机部署的策略平滑方法。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning policies can produce nonsmooth action oscillations that hinder deployment on physical robots. Existing architectural and penalty-based approaches seek spatial smoothness by directly reducing sensitivity to changes in state inputs, but their broad constraints can degrade task performance as stronger smoothing is pursued. Temporal regularization instead constrains action differences along observed transitions, but has been considered unable to provide the spatial smoothness needed under observation noise. We revisit this assumption by proving that the temporal penalty bounds the expected action differences between current states sharing a next state, revealing a spatial effect that empirically extends to spatial smoothness. Building on this finding, we propose Conditioning for Action using only Temporal Smoothness (CATS), which combines a temporal penalty with linear ramp-up. We highlight temporal regularization's ability to provide spatial smoothness while better preserving task performance than explicit spatial regularization. Through linear ramp-up, CATS allows the policy to learn rewarding behavior before progressively smoothing its actions, improving return preservation and both temporal and spatial smoothness. Experiments in both simulation and the real world show that CATS substantially reduces action oscillation without degrading task performance, with little computational overhead.

</details>


### [6] [Preserving Unstable Modes Through Inverse Dynamics in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07540)
*Leonardo F. Toso,Yann LeCun,James Anderson,Oumayma Bounou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出仅用下一步预测与防坍缩正则的JEPA可能丢失可控不稳定模态，提出加入动作重建/逆向动力学损失以学习控制感知表示，并证明其保证有限时域可达子空间上的编码器单射性，实验在视觉控制任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统常存在不稳定模态，微小扰动可能无界增长，需反馈控制；从高维视觉观测控制此类系统要求表示保留这些模态。现有JEPA式世界模型训练虽能学习表示与动力学，但下一步预测加防坍缩正则并不保证保留可控不稳定模态，甚至可在训练损失最小时将其坍缩，导致无法基于学习表示进行稳定控制。

Method: 在world-model训练中引入动作重建目标，即逆向动力学损失，鼓励学习控制感知的视觉表示，保留对控制关键的特征。理论分析针对线性系统：证明精确动作重建使编码器在有限时域可达子空间上单射，因此编码器不能丢弃H步内可由动作序列到达的任一状态方向；并证明随H增大，有限时域可控性Gramian的主导特征空间收敛到可控不稳定子空间。

Result: 在线性系统上建立了上述理论结果；在非线性视觉控制任务CartPole、Walker2D和PointMaze上进行实证，表明发现可推广到非线性视觉控制场景，并凸显控制感知表示学习的收益。

Conclusion: 控制感知表示学习，尤其是结合逆动力学/动作重建目标，可避免JEPA坍缩可控不稳定模态，从而提升视觉机器人控制中不稳定系统的可稳定性和表示质量。

Abstract: Robotic systems often exhibit unstable modes, along which small perturbations and disturbances can cause unbounded growth unless corrected through feedback. Controlling such systems from high-dimensional visual observations requires representations that preserve these modes. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) provide a natural framework for learning such representations and their dynamics from visual data. However, we demonstrate that next step prediction combined with anti-collapse regularization does not guarantee that controllable unstable modes are preserved: the training loss can be minimized while these modes are collapsed, making stabilization from the learned representation impossible. To address this, we augment world-model training with an action reconstruction objective (i.e., an inverse dynamics loss) that encourages control-aware representations, namely, visual representations that preserve crucial features for control. We prove that exact action reconstruction makes the encoder injective on the finite-horizon reachable subspace. Thus, the encoder cannot discard any state direction reachable by an action sequence within $H$ steps. Moreover, we show that, as $H$ grows, the dominant eigenspace of the finite-horizon controllability Gramian converges to the controllable unstable subspace. We establish our theoretical results for linear systems and demonstrate empirically that our findings extend to nonlinear visual control tasks (CartPole, Walker2D, and PointMaze), highlighting the benefits of control-aware representation learning.

</details>

### [7] [Demo: Closed-Loop Sionna-Isaac Sim Co-Simulation Framework for Wireless-Aware Robot Navigation over ROS 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08618)
*Yi Shen Lim,Seungeun Oh,Jihong Park*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于ROS 2的实时联合仿真框架，将NVIDIA Isaac Sim与NVIDIA Sionna耦合，打通感知-动作-通信（PAC）闭环，使机器人控制回路可感知并规避通信中断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人若将控制回路卸载到网络，其链路质量会随移动位置变化；但现有仿真器缺乏同时统一物理引擎与站点特定传播模型的能力，已有耦合也多为离线分析，难以支持实时闭环研究。

Method: 该框架通过ROS 2连接Isaac Sim与Sionna：Isaac Sim提供精确物理仿真与几何场景，Sionna在相同几何上对基站到机器人的信道进行GPU光线追踪，而非随机建模，并将信道状态实时反馈至控制回路；GPU光追覆盖图约每16 ms刷新一次（约60 Hz），满足实时控制需求。

Result: 在基于OpenStreetMap构建的SUTD校园数字孪生中，使用两台Nova Carter机器人实现无线感知导航：闭环规划器消除了通信中断，相比最短路径基线仅增加7.4%的通行时间；而基线在81.2 s运行中有7.9 s处于断连状态。

Conclusion: 该工作表明物理仿真与站点特定信道传播的实时联合仿真可行，并可用于验证通信感知的机器人导航策略，在几乎不显著牺牲任务效率的情况下避免通信中断。

Abstract: A robot that offloads its control loop to the network carries the receiver with it, so link quality is decided by where it goes. Simulating this requires both a physics engine and a site-specific propagation model at once; to our knowledge no simulator natively unifies both, with existing couplings of the two limited to offline analyses. We demonstrate a real-time co-simulation framework coupling NVIDIA Isaac Sim and NVIDIA Sionna over ROS 2 that closes the perception-action-communication (PAC) loop between them. Sionna ray-traces the base-station-to-robot channel over the exact geometry Isaac Sim simulates on, rather than modeling it stochastically, and feeds channel states back into the control loop in real time. Ray-traced on GPU, the coverage map is refreshed in ~16ms (~60 Hz), fast enough for real-time control. To showcase the framework's utility, we implement a wireless-aware navigation application in an OpenStreetMap(OSM)-derived SUTD campus twin with two Nova Carter robots: the closed-loop planner eliminates communication outage at only +7.4% traversal time over the shortest-path baseline (which spends 7.9 s of its 81.2 s run disconnected).

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [8] [Context-Conditioned Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability for Adaptive Safety Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08115)
*Ali Fuat Sahin,Yunus Yazoglu,John Talbot,Zeyuan Feng,James Dallas,Somil Bansal*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该工作学习一个以局部边界几何、摩擦系数和对抗扰动尺度为条件的 Hamilton-Jacobi 后向可达管安全证书，使同一值函数无需重新合成即可跨部署条件查询，并在仿真与实车近操控极限场景中验证其安全性与性能代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Hamilton-Jacobi 可达性安全证书通常针对特定动力学、安全约束和部署场景合成，场景变化后需重新合成，难以复用。作者希望探究：单一证书能否表示一族上下文相关的安全问题，并在不同部署条件下直接查询，而无需重新合成。

Method: 针对八状态车辆模型学习条件化后向可达管：局部边界几何通过 ego 坐标系下的边界观测进入，定义局部包含约束；摩擦系数与对抗扰动尺度作为显式运行条件变量。同一值函数可在摩擦系数 0.4 至 2.0 以及未参与合成的几何场景中查询。随后将证书作为采样离散时间控制障碍函数（CBF）滤波器，部署于接近操控极限的全尺寸车辆；评估包含 11 个留出几何场景、对抗驾驶和自动驾驶赛车，并与已知测试几何重新合成的证书对比干预率和速度。

Result: 在 11 个留出评估几何上，证书在完整测试摩擦范围内保持包含性，包括几何与抓地力同时变化；与知晓测试几何后重新合成的证书相比，干预率差异在 1.2 个百分点以内，速度差异在 0.09 m/s 以内。实车部署中，对抗驾驶与自动驾驶赛车下每次硬件会话均保持横向包含，99 百分位加速度幅值达 0.99 g；在固定自动驾驶赛车控制器下评估三个证书，圈速仅变化 3.1%。

Conclusion: 上下文条件化的可达性证书能够迁移到未参与合成的部署几何，并以适度性能代价实现跨摩擦与几何条件查询，表明单一安全证书可覆盖一族上下文相关安全问题而无需逐场景重新合成。

Abstract: Hamilton-Jacobi reachability constructs safety certificates for specified dynamics and safety constraints, tying each certificate to the deployment context for which it is synthesized. We ask whether a single certificate can instead represent a family of context-dependent safety problems and be queried across deployment conditions without re-synthesis. We learn a backward reachable tube for an eight-state vehicle model conditioned on local boundary geometry, friction coefficient, and adversarial disturbance scale. Geometry enters through an ego-frame boundary observation that defines the local containment constraint, while friction and disturbance scale enter as explicit operating-condition variables. This allows the same value function to be queried across friction coefficients from 0.4 to 2.0 and on geometries absent from synthesis. On 11 held-out evaluation geometries, the certificate maintains containment across the full tested friction range, including simultaneous geometry and grip shifts, while remaining within 1.2 percentage points in intervention rate and 0.09 m/s in speed of certificates re-synthesized with knowledge of the test geometry. We then deploy the certificate as a sampled discrete-time control barrier function filter on a full-scale vehicle near the handling limit. Lateral containment holds in every hardware session under both adversarial driving and autonomous racing, with 99th-percentile acceleration magnitude reaching 0.99 g. Across three certificates evaluated under a fixed autonomous racing controller, lap time varies by only 3.1%, demonstrating that a context-conditioned reachability certificate can transfer to deployment geometries absent from synthesis with modest performance cost.

</details>


### [9] [Belief-Informed Hybrid Control with Almost-Sure Target-Set Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07674)
*Clinton Enwerem,Saleh Kemal,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种基于信念的双控制算法，通过两步前瞻与累计松弛约束，在参数不确定下用信息性动作使混合系统几乎必然收敛到目标集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将混合系统控制到目标集时，参数不确定性可能需要暂时偏离目标集的探索性动作；短视控制容易在零输入后失去可接受性，因此需要兼顾信息获取与目标收敛。

Method: 算法结合信念空间滚动时域选择与非负目标集进展函数的期望下降约束，并用标量控制器状态限制约束的累计松弛。两步前瞻先用预测观测更新参数信念，再评估后续动作可接受性；在距离比较界、正确条件预测和递归可行性下证明几乎必然收敛，并用上置信界扩展到有界模型样本。

Result: 平面调节中，未知控制方向下两步选择在11次决策内将欧氏状态范数降至0.01以下，短视一步选择在零输入后立即失去可接受性。双臂装配仿真中，一步实现利用间隙反馈在名义摩擦下完成装配，相对位置无穷范数误差比参考跟踪基线低96.4%；低摩擦时后验条件成功检测空可接受集，而固定先验替代方案允许违反条件下降约束的动作。

Conclusion: 该方法通过信念更新和累计松弛约束实现递归可行性与目标集收敛，能有效处理参数不确定性下的探索性偏离，并优于短视或固定先验基线。

Abstract: Controlling a hybrid system to a target set under parameter uncertainty can require informative actions that temporarily drive the system state away from the specified set. We propose a belief-informed dual-control algorithm that combines belief-space receding-horizon selection with an expected-decrease constraint on a nonnegative target-set progress function. To accommodate exploratory deviations, a scalar controller state bounds the constraint's cumulative slack. Our algorithm's two-step lookahead selection uses predicted observations to update the parameter belief before evaluating the subsequent action's admissibility. Under distance-comparison bounds, correct conditional prediction, and recursive feasibility, we prove almost-sure target-set convergence at decision times and bound both the sum of expected progress-function values and the expected neighborhood-entry time. Upper confidence bounds extend these convergence guarantees to bounded model samples with summable error probabilities. In planar regulation with an unknown control direction, we verify recursive feasibility: the proposed two-step selection reduces the Euclidean state norm below 0.01 within 11 decisions for either sign, whereas a myopic one-step selection loses admissibility immediately after zero input. In a simulated bimanual assembly task, our algorithm's one-step implementation uses clearance feedback to complete the assembly under nominal friction, yielding a 96.4% lower infinity-norm relative-position error than the reference-tracking baseline at the method's completion time. At lower friction, its posterior conditioning successfully detects an empty admissible set, whereas a fixed-prior alternative admits an action that violates the conditional decrease constraint. Project page: this https URL .

</details>

### [10] [Mission-Aware Attestation Envelopes for Time-Critical Autonomous Action: A Hardware-in-the-Loop V2I Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08771)
*Dimitrios Nikou,Nikolaos Kekatos,Sophia Petridou,Stylianos Basagiannis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 将面向任务的远程证明形式化为运行时保证合约，联合考虑完整性有效性、证据新鲜度与决策时延，并在硬件在环V2I平台上验证其可区分篡改、过期与迟到三类拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统证明门控被当作二值谓词，忽略证据时效、决策延迟和物理系统截止期，无法区分篡改导致的拒绝、证据过期导致的拒绝与决策迟到导致的拒绝，难以满足有物理截止期的自主系统需求。

Method: 把mission-aware attestation建模为运行时保证合约：仅当完整性有效、证据足够新鲜、且决策在当前物理状态导出的授权预算内完成时合约成立；由此产生四种运行结果而非两种。在硬件在环车路协同平台上评估：驾驶模拟器提供物理状态与授权截止期，MCU车载单元与TPM支持、运行Linux完整性测量的路侧单元提供保证证据；比较security-blind模型与hardware-informed模型。

Result: security-blind模型允许整个操作空间，hardware-informed模型允许约四分之三；其拒绝点均因新鲜度裕度失败，而非响应裕度失败。将证明间隔在验证器允许范围内调整，其代价约相当于时延分布放大五倍；证明间隔可直接配置，因而是最直接可操作的部署参数。若新鲜度上界不超过授权预算，则所有迟到决策同时过期，迟到不可观测。

Conclusion: 证明间隔与新鲜度上界不能仅由安全需求单独确定，必须与物理任务截止期和运行时保证合约联合设计；部署时应将证明间隔作为关键可配置参数，并注意新鲜度与授权预算的耦合会使迟到不可观测。

Abstract: An autonomous system that asks for a privileged physical action is usually gated on integrity evidence: a platform proves what it is running, and the request is granted or refused on that basis. Such a gate is normally treated as a predicate, yet the evidence behind it has an age, the decision that consumes it has a latency, and the physical system that waits for it has a deadline. We formulate mission-aware attestation as a runtime assurance contract that holds only when integrity is valid, the evidence is fresh enough, and the decision completes inside a budget derived from the current physical state. The contract yields four operational outcomes where a binary gate yields two, separating a refusal caused by tampering from one caused by stale evidence and from one caused by a late decision. We evaluate it on a hardware-in-the-loop vehicle-to-infrastructure platform: a driving simulator supplies the physical state and the authorisation deadline, while a microcontroller on-board unit and a TPM-backed roadside unit running Linux integrity measurement supply the assurance evidence. A security-blind model admits the whole operating space and a hardware-informed one three quarters of it, and every point it refuses fails the freshness margin rather than the response margin. Moving the attestation interval across the range the verifier permits costs about as much as a fivefold scaling of the latency distribution, and the interval is directly configurable, which makes it the immediately actionable deployment parameter. If the freshness bound does not exceed the authorisation budget, every late decision is also stale and lateness becomes unobservable, so the attestation interval and the freshness bound cannot be chosen from security requirements alone.

</details>


### [11] [Federated Bayesian Surveillance of Mechanical Thrombectomy Adverse Events: A Population Risk Layer for Surgical Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08464)
*Damini Rijhwani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该文将人群级不良事件监测视为手术数字孪生的独立信念层，并提出在正式隐私保证下学习的联邦贝叶斯协议，用于介入器械并发症相对发生率排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有学习型手术模拟器和世界模型能生成合理的手术未来推演，但无法提供介入器械实际伤害患者频率的可靠估计；因此需要将人群级不良事件监测作为手术数字孪生的独立信念层。

Method: 各站点维护不良事件发生率的逐类Gamma-Poisson后验，仅交换满足Rényi差分隐私的自然参数更新。在FDA MAUDE的完整血栓取出导管队列（产品代码NRY，8,617份报告，其中6,491份经透明关键词规则归入五类血栓切除并发症，并划分到K=8家厂商站点）上评估联邦贝叶斯协议。

Result: 在匹配隐私预算ε≈2.09、δ=1e-5下，共轭协议在留出测试事件上的Poisson得分为-5.78/事件，优于带差分隐私的FedAvg（-26.58）。非隐私联邦模型也优于集中式池化（+3.19 vs +2.93），表明厂商特异性并发症谱真实存在且联邦学习可保留该结构。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为手术数字孪生中不良事件监测的隐私保护信念层，支持厂商间相对风险排序；但因MAUDE缺少操作分母，输出仅为相对发生率排序而非绝对风险，且需报告全部隐私-效用工作点。

Abstract: Learned surgical simulators and world models can roll out plausible procedural futures, but they carry no grounded estimate of how often interventional devices actually harm patients. We propose treating population-scale adverse-event surveillance as a distinct belief layer of the surgical digital twin, and we evaluate a federated Bayesian protocol for learning it under formal privacy guarantees. Each site holds per-class Gamma-Poisson posteriors over adverse-event rates and exchanges only Rényi-differentially-private natural-parameter updates. We benchmark on the complete FDA MAUDE cohort for thrombus-retrieval catheters (product code NRY): 8,617 reports, of which 6,491 are classified by transparent keyword rules into five thrombectomy complication classes and partitioned across $K=8$ manufacturer sites. At a matched privacy budget of $(\\varepsilon \\approx 2.09, \\delta = 10^{-5})$, the conjugate protocol attains a held-out Poisson score of -5.78 per test event versus -26.58 for FedAvg with differential privacy. The non-private federated model also outperforms centralized pooling (+3.19 vs +2.93), evidence that manufacturer-specific complication profiles are real and that federation preserves them. Because MAUDE lacks procedure denominators, outputs are relative rate orderings rather than absolute risks, and we report all privacy-utility operating points.

</details>
