### [12] [Generalizable Robustness Testing of DNN-Based Robotic Navigation Systems via XAI-Guided Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06862)
*Khizra Sohail,Miren Illarramendi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种可解释性引导的多目标进化方法，为DNN控制的CPS生成跨观测有效的稀疏鲁棒性测试，并在仿真与实物机器人上验证失败迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN increasingly control CPS，但微小输入扰动可导致系统级不安全行为；现有方法多针对单张图像优化扰动且仅在仿真中评估，泛化性与实际有效性不足。

Method: 提出可解释性引导的多目标进化方法：通过视觉与行为聚类选取代表性图像，在其上生成稀疏扰动；利用聚合Integrated Gradients引导变异朝向关键图像区域；在Gazebo中面向DNN控制的LeoRover评估，进行消融实验，并在实物机器人上验证分层子集扰动。

Result: 方法成功率中位数为70.0%，无引导搜索为53.85%；中位hypervolume从0.65提升至0.73。多图像优化将成功率从50.0%提升至57.5%，XAI引导进一步提升至70.0%。sim-to-real评估中，仿真达到0.95精确率和0.67召回率，仿真与实物失败时间呈显著正相关（0.617）。

Conclusion: 多图像优化与可解释性引导搜索结合可提升DNN控制机器人系统的鲁棒性测试；仿真能有效识别并优先选择可迁移失败，但部分真实失败无法在仿真中复现，因此物理验证仍然必要。

Abstract: **Context:** Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) increasingly control Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs), yet small input perturbations can cause unsafe system-level behavior. Existing approaches often optimize perturbations for individual images and evaluate them only in simulation, limiting their generalizability and practical validity. **Objectives:** This work aims to generate robustness tests that remain effective across operational observations and to evaluate whether the resulting failures transfer from simulation to a physical robot. **Methods:** We propose an explainability-guided multi-objective evolutionary approach that generates sparse perturbations over representative images selected through visual and behavioral clustering. Aggregated Integrated Gradients guide mutations toward influential image regions. We evaluate the approach on a DNN-controlled LeoRover in Gazebo, conduct an ablation study, and validate a stratified subset of perturbations on the physical robot. **Results:** The approach achieved a median success rate of 70.0%, compared with 53.85% for unguided search, and increased median hypervolume from 0.65 to 0.73. Multi-image optimization improved the success rate from 50.0% to 57.5%, while XAI guidance further increased it to 70.0%. In the sim-to-real evaluation, simulation achieved 0.95 precision and 0.67 recall, and simulated and physical failure times showed a significant positive correlation of 0.617. **Conclusion:** Combining multi-image optimization with explainability-guided search improves robustness testing for DNN-controlled robotic systems. Simulation effectively identifies and prioritizes transferable failures, but physical validation remains necessary because some real-world failures are not reproduced in simulation.

</details>


### [13] [Commit While Futures Agree: Consequence-Aware Adaptive Action Chunking for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07949)
*Yuyan Li,Yujia Wang,Yusong Huang,Junjie Yang,Yanggang Sheng,Ziyi Shi,Wenpeng Xu,Xiaoyang Zhou,Haoang Li,Hongliang Lu,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CA$^3$C：一种推理时自适应动作分块框架，基于“想象未来一致则继续执行，分歧则重规划”的原则，无需修改或重训练基策略，在多基准和真实机器人任务中显著降低失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块策略可预测多步控制序列，但固定长度执行前缀隐含假设该执行视界在不同状态下都可靠。现有自适应方法多基于动作相似性或稳定性估计视界，然而不同动作可能通向相同成功结果，相似动作也可能导致不同未来，因此承诺长度应由想象未来的一致性决定，而非动作空间相似性。

Method: CA$^3$C在推理阶段使用动作条件世界模型，在同一采样噪声下想象多个候选动作块的未来后果；将执行视界估计建模为贝叶斯变点推断问题，并依据想象未来之间的共识选择要执行的候选动作块，同时不修改或重训练基础策略。

Result: 在多个仿真基准和真实机器人操作任务中，CA$^3$C持续提升多种动作分块策略，相对相应基策略最高实现71.8%的失败率相对降低。

Conclusion: 执行动作块时，承诺长度应依据想象未来是否一致自适应确定；CA$^3$C通过未来共识与贝叶斯变点推断有效改进了动作分块策略的鲁棒性和成功率。

Abstract: Action-chunking policies predict multi-step control sequences, but a fundamental question remains: how much of a predicted action chunk should be committed before replanning? Existing systems typically execute a fixed-length prefix, implicitly assuming that the same execution horizon remains trustworthy across states. Some adaptive methods estimate this horizon from the similarity or stability of predicted actions. However, different actions may lead to the same successful outcome, whereas similar actions can produce different futures, suggesting that commitment should be determined by agreement among imagined futures rather than by similarity in action space. To this end, we propose Consequence-Aware Adaptive Action Chunking (CA$^3$C), an inference-time framework built on a simple principle: commit while imagined futures agree, and replan when they diverge. Without modifying or retraining the base policy, CA$^3$C uses an action-conditioned world model to imagine the future consequences of multiple candidate action chunks under the same sampling noise. Using these imagined consequences, we formulate execution-horizon estimation as a Bayesian change-point inference problem and select the execution candidate through future consensus. Across multiple simulation benchmarks and real-world robot manipulation tasks, CA$^3$C consistently improves diverse action-chunking policies, achieving up to a 71.8% relative reduction in failure rate over the corresponding base policies.

</details>


### [14] [Adapting Vision-Language-Action Models to Unknown Visual Disruptions During Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07946)
*Ahin Lee,Jinwoo Seo,Youngsoo Jang,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SALT，一种利用上一动作块未执行部分作为自监督信号的测试时自适应方法，使VLA策略在未知视觉干扰下继续执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人执行任务时可能遭遇视觉扰动，且干扰类型和发生时刻未知；VLA策略需要在线适应，但通常缺少标注、专家动作或目标域演示。

Method: SALT将上一动作块中未执行的剩余轨迹作为当前预测的时序对齐目标，因为相邻动作块在时间上重叠。视觉偏移初现时，剩余轨迹可保留损坏前形成的计划，并以过渡锚定将适应锚定到该计划；随后保留适应后策略并重生成当前块，其剩余部分成为下次重规划目标，实现顺序校正传播。监督完全来自策略自身预测，无需干扰标注、专家动作或目标域演示；仅用正常轨迹校准的轻量自适应门控决定何时开始更新。

Result: 在LIBERO-10上，五种持续视觉损坏下，SmolVLA平均成功率从43.9%提升至53.2%，GR00T N1.7从58.7%提升至66.0%，且基本保持正常性能；真实机器人上，数字与物理干扰平均任务进度从0.49提升至0.61。

Conclusion: SALT通过剩余轨迹自监督实现无需额外监督的测试时适应，可提升VLA策略在视觉扰动下的鲁棒性，同时基本保持正常条件性能。

Abstract: Visual disruptions can arise while a robot is executing a task, leaving a vision-language-action (VLA) policy to respond without knowing the disruption type or timing. We introduce Self-supervised Adaptation from Leftover Trajectories (SALT), which uses the leftover trajectory, the unexecuted part of the previous action chunk, as self-supervision for test-time adaptation. Because consecutive chunks overlap in time, the leftover provides a temporally aligned target for the current prediction over the same future control interval. At the onset of a visual shift, the leftover can retain a plan formed before the corruption, so updating the policy toward it anchors the adaptation across the shift (Transition Anchoring). SALT keeps the adapted policy and regenerates the current chunk, whose leftover becomes the target at the next replan, carrying the correction forward along the execution trajectory (Sequential Correction Propagation). Supervision comes entirely from the policy's own predictions, requiring no disruption annotations, expert actions, or target-domain demonstrations, and a lightweight adaptation gate calibrated only on nominal trajectories decides when updates begin. On LIBERO-10, SALT increases average success across five persistent visual corruptions from 43.9% to 53.2% with SmolVLA and from 58.7% to 66.0% with GR00T N1.7, while largely preserving nominal performance. On a real robot, it raises task progress averaged over digital and physical disruptions from 0.49 to 0.61.

</details>


### [15] [OpenWAM: An Open Framework for Composable World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07922)
*Heng Yu,David D. Yuan,Juze Zhang,Changan Chen,Yao Feng,Michelle Baldonado,Steve Cousins,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OPENWAM 提出统一且可配置的世界-动作建模框架，基于共享因果机器人视频基础和 Mixture-of-Transformers 动作专家，系统比较不同视频-动作交互设计，并扩展到逆动力学与正动力学中的反事实学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型常同时改变视频骨干、交互结构、监督方式和推理流程，导致设计选择难以公平比较；同时动力学学习多局限于成功演示。

Method: 基于 Wan2.2-5B，在超过10,000小时机器人视频上进行因果机器人视频预训练，再通过共享 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构集成动作专家，支持联合生成、先视频后动作、先动作后视频和解耦生成；同一架构扩展到逆动力学和正动力学，并引入反事实转移监督。

Result: 在四个 LIBERO 套件和真实双臂任务上取得高成功率；因果适配的机器人视频训练将 LIBERO-Long 的 VTA 成功率从68.4%提升到97.8%。仅适配视频预测器时，冻结的局部上下文逆动力学模型结合反事实数据与演示，在四个留出 LIBERO-90 任务上平均成功率为84.0%，优于全上下文逆模型47.0%和仅用演示训练的局部上下文模型21.5%；在正动力学中，反事实监督将 RGB 预测误差降低34.5%，并把16个同状态结果中的结果识别率从21.1%提高到71.3%。

Conclusion: OPENWAM 为比较世界-动作模型的交互设计提供了统一测试平台，并表明反事实视频监督能显著提升逆动力学和正动力学学习，突破仅依赖成功演示的局限。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) couple future prediction with robot control, yet existing systems often vary the video backbone, interaction structure, supervision, and inference procedure simultaneously, making their design choices difficult to compare. We introduce OPENWAM, an open world-action modeling framework built around a common causal robot-video foundation and configurable video-action interaction. Starting from Wan2.2-5B, we perform causal robot-video pretraining on over 10,000 hours of video, then integrate an action expert through a shared Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that supports joint, video-then-action, action-then-video, and decoupled generation. OPENWAM achieves high success rates on four LIBERO suites and real-world bimanual tasks; robot-video training with causal adaptation improves VTA success on LIBERO-Long from 68.4% to 97.8%. The same configurable architecture naturally extends to inverse and forward dynamics, allowing us to study how counterfactual transitions improve independently trained dynamics models beyond demonstrations alone. When only the video predictor is adapted to a new task, a frozen local-context inverse dynamics model trained on counterfactual data and demonstrations achieves 84.0% mean success across four held-out LIBERO-90 tasks, compared with 47.0% for a full-context inverse model and 21.5% for a local-context model trained only on demonstrations. For forward dynamics, counterfactual supervision reduces RGB prediction error by 34.5% and raises outcome identification from 21.1% to 71.3% among 16 same-state outcomes. OPENWAM provides a common testbed for comparing WAM interaction designs and for studying dynamics learning from video data beyond successful demonstrations.

</details>


### [16] [PACE: Stage-Consistent Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation via Progress-Aligned Context for Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07917)
*Yenan Chen,Junjie Shi,Lu Chen,Zhongxiang Zhou,Rong Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACE（Progress-Aligned Context for Execution）是一种状态化方法，按实际执行进度持续重解释完整演示，以缓解长时程机器人操作中的阶段混淆，并在多个任务上提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 演示条件策略是自然的行为接口，但长时程操作中，不同阶段可能出现视觉相似状态，且演示与执行速度不一致，导致策略产生阶段混淆（stage confusion）。

Method: PACE 将演示压缩为有序多模态提示 token，并用仅训练时的双边缘注意力监督暴露潜在阶段结构；执行时，回合局部 fast-weight 记忆因果编码已实现的动作-观测转移并调制提示交叉注意力，为统一扩散动作专家生成进度对齐上下文，且无需测试时阶段标签或阶段专用策略。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Gen Goal Chain 上成功率由 88.9% 提升至 94.0%，Spatial Combination 由 79.1% 提升至 83.3%，两步 Block Routing 由 33.3% 提升至 73.3%。

Conclusion: 失败分析表明，结构化演示对齐与因果执行记忆共同缓解阶段混淆，验证了 PACE 对长时程操作中进度对齐与状态记忆的有效性。

Abstract: Demonstration-conditioned policies provide a natural interface for specifying robot behavior, yet long-horizon manipulation remains difficult when visually similar states recur across different stages or when demonstrations and executions proceed at different speeds. We identify the resulting failure mode as stage confusion and introduce Progress-Aligned Context for Execution (PACE), a stateful method that continually reinterprets a complete demonstration according to realized execution progress. PACE compresses the demonstration into ordered multimodal prompt tokens and uses training-only dual-edge attention supervision to expose its latent stage structure. During execution, an episode-local fast-weight memory causally encodes realized action-observation transitions and modulates prompt cross-attention, producing a progress-aligned context for a unified diffusion action expert without test-time stage labels or stage-specific policies. PACE improves success from 88.9% to 94.0% on LIBERO-Gen Goal Chain, from 79.1% to 83.3% on Spatial Combination, and from 33.3% to 73.3% on the two-step Block Routing tasks. Failure analysis further indicates that structured demonstration alignment and causal execution memory jointly mitigate stage confusion.

</details>


### [17] [From Model to Prototype: Design and Motor-Flap Propulsion Control of a Twin-Wing Metamorphic UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07896)
*Anja Bosak,Dorian Erić,Ana Milas,Stjepan Bogdan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaMorpher变形无人机的悬停控制策略，比较电机推进与襟翼推进两种相位同步方法，仿真和实验验证了模型，表明襟翼推进是轻量且解耦的悬停控制方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巡航配置的控制已较为成熟，因此本文聚焦于悬停模式的控制，基于先前工作验证的飞行动力学模型和概念设计。

Method: 控制算法采用两种相位同步策略：电机推进（与飞行器旋转同步脉冲电机推力）和襟翼推进（一种新策略，脉冲翼上升降副翼的偏转）。通过不同飞行实验在仿真中评估两种策略。

Result: 仿真结果证实了数学模型，并展示了稳定的参考跟踪，表明襟翼推进是一种轻量、解耦的悬停控制替代方案。实验测试针对物理原型验证了垂直动力学推进模型，不同配置下稳态一致性良好。

Conclusion: 襟翼推进可作为悬停控制的轻量解耦方案，且垂直动力学模型得到实验验证。

Abstract: This paper presents the design and control strategy for MetaMorpher, a metamorphic Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV), capable of both spinning-wing hover and fixed flying-wing cruise flight. Since control of the cruise configuration is comparatively well established in the literature, this paper focuses on control of the MetaMorpher in hover mode, building on the flight dynamics model and conceptual design validated in previous work. The control algorithm is implemented using one of two phase-synchronized strategies: motor propulsion, which pulses motor thrust in synchrony with the vehicle's rotation, or flap propulsion, a novel strategy that pulses the deflection of the wing-mounted elevons. We evaluate both strategies in simulation through different flight experiments. Simulation results confirm the mathematical model and show stable reference tracking, demonstrating flap propulsion as a lightweight, decoupled alternative for hover control. Experimental testing validated the vertical-dynamics propulsion model against the physical prototype, demonstrating very good steady-state agreement across different configurations.

</details>


### [18] [Beyond Retargeting: Low-Latency and Robust Humanoid Whole-Body Teleoperation with Learned Atomic Motion Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07891)
*Xiayan Xu,Jiyu Yu,Xingzhou Chen,Siyi Qian,Zongyu Ma,Lilu Liu,Ling Shi,Haodong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种免重定向的人形全身遥操作策略，将原始人体运动通过单次前向传播直接映射为机器人关节命令，并利用全身运动基元码本提升对分布外观测与部分输入的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统通常依赖在线运动重定向来弥合人-机器人形态差异，但会引入延迟，并可能生成物理不可行的目标；同时，多样、含噪、部分可观测的人体运动常位于训练分布之外，可能导致机器人行为不稳定。

Method: 设计免重定向策略，单次前向将原始人体运动直接映射为机器人关节命令，从而消除在线运动学适配；学习全身运动基元码本，将分布外观测投影到合理的运动原型上，并从部分输入中恢复全身运动。

Result: 在Unitree G1的仿真与硬件实验中，使用VR、光学动捕、文本到运动生成和单目视频输入，所提方法在延迟和鲁棒性上优于基线。

Conclusion: 结合免重定向策略与全身运动基元码本，可实现低延迟、鲁棒的人形全身遥操作，并有效应对形态差异、分布外观测及部分输入等挑战。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body teleoperation translates human motion into stable robot behavior in real time. Existing systems typically rely on online motion retargeting to bridge human--robot morphological differences, but this process adds latency and can produce physically infeasible targets. Meanwhile, diverse, noisy, and partial human-motion observations often fall outside the training distribution, potentially causing unstable robot behavior. We propose a retargeting-free policy that maps raw human motion directly to robot joint commands in a single forward pass, eliminating online kinematic adaptation. To improve robustness, we learn a codebook of full-body motion primitives that projects out-of-distribution observations onto plausible motion prototypes and recovers full-body motion from partial inputs. Experiments on a Unitree~G1 in simulation and on hardware, using virtual reality, optical mocap, text-to-motion generation, and monocular video inputs, show that our method outperforms baselines in latency and robustness.

</details>


### [19] [CUSP: CUSUM-Governed Survival Hazard Alarms at the Perception Onset for Off-Road Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07882)
*Inuk Kang,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CUSP：一种模型无关的运行时危险警报器，从有人工干预终止的野外日志中学习“感知起始点”，即安全驾驶在人类判断中转为不安全的时刻；用离散时间生存目标训练视觉危险头，再用 CUSUM 累积风险触发警报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野导航中，学习型导航虽用安全监督选路径，但机器人沿路径驶向危险时没有运行时警报。此类警报需从野外日志学习，而人类干预会抢先阻止失败，导致真实失败从未被观测；且人类往往在危险临近时才干预，因此干预时刻晚于其早期“不安全”判断。现有干预监督方法未针对这一更早判断时刻。

Method: CUSP（CUSUM-governed Survival model of the Perception onset）将目标时刻称为 perception onset，并在日志中与干预分开标注。训练一个视觉危险头，以标注的 onset 为监督，采用离散时间生存目标，使有 onset 和无 onset 的驾驶片段都参与监督；推理时用 CUSUM 累积预测的 onset 风险，形成运行时警报。

Result: 在 5 个未见过的地点（2 个自动驾驶、3 个遥操作）和 142 个事件上评估，所有方法调至相同误报率（每小时 10 次）。CUSP 检测到 85 个事件，而 9 个适配基线中最佳者仅检测到 26 个；优势主要来自导航模型所有信号均“看不见”的危险。

Conclusion: CUSP 能从干预终止日志中有效学习并检测人类早期判断的危险起始点，在相同误报率下显著优于现有基线，尤其适用于导航模型信号盲区中的危险。

Abstract: Off-road navigation exposes a robot to potentially hazardous terrain en route. Although learning-based navigation uses safety supervision to choose which path to drive, it provides no runtime alarm when the robot following that path is heading into danger. Such an alarm must be learned from field logs, where human intervention preempts the failure and the failure itself is therefore never observed. The human judges driving unsafe early but typically intervenes only once failure is clearly near, so the intervention marks that judgment late. That earlier judgment is what a runtime alarm must detect, yet no prior intervention-supervised method has targeted it. To address this problem, we introduce CUSP (CUSUM-governed Survival model of the Perception onset), a model-agnostic runtime hazard alarm that learns this moment from intervention-terminated logs. "Cusp" is a word for the point at which one state is about to turn into another, and the moment we target is exactly such a cusp: the point at which safe driving turns unsafe in a human's judgment. We call this point the perception onset and annotate it separately from the intervention. A visual hazard head is trained on the annotated onset with a discrete-time survival objective so that driving with and without an onset both supervise the head, and a CUSUM accumulates the predicted onset risk into alarms. We evaluate CUSP at five unseen sites, two autonomous and three teleoperated, with 142 events and every method tuned to the same rate of ten false alarms per hour. CUSP detected 85 events compared to 26 for the best of nine adapted baselines, and the margin comes from hazards to which every signal in the navigation model is blind.

</details>


### [20] [Multi-Robot Multi-Goal Motion Planning with Stochastic Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07784)
*William Schnyder,Valentin N. Hartmann,Stelian Coros*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将随机技能集成到基于采样的多机器人规划中，在多模态复合路线图上构建MDP并求解反应式策略，使机器人能动态协调应对技能执行的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人共享工作空间执行复杂操作技能时，技能具有连续物理执行和随机性，导致执行时间可变、轨迹不确定。现有规划器多限于单机器人、依赖开环路径或采用事后调度，难以实现动态协调。

Method: 把随机技能纳入基于采样的多机器人规划框架，构建多模态复合路线图，并将问题形式化为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）。求解该MDP可得到反应式策略，使可控机器人根据其他机器人执行操作技能的情况动态调整自身运动，从而在规划阶段直接处理技能不确定性。

Result: 摘要称该方法避免了保守基线的悲观性，实现了稳健、动态的多机器人协调，并提供了规划器代码。摘要未给出具体定量实验结果。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了随机操作技能与多机器人采样规划之间的空白，通过在规划时处理技能不确定性，支持更动态、鲁棒的多机器人协同运动。

Abstract: As robots are increasingly deployed in groups and share workspaces to execute real-world tasks, planning their concurrent motions around complex manipulation skills becomes essential. These skills involve continuous physical execution and may exhibit stochastic behavior, resulting in variable execution times and uncertain continuous trajectories. Existing planners either limit execution to single-robot scenarios, rely on open-loop paths, or use post-hoc scheduling that prevents dynamic coordination. In this paper, we address this gap by integrating stochastic skills into sampling-based multi-robot planning by formulating the problem as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) over a multi-modal composite roadmap. For stochastic skills, solving the MDP yields a reactive policy that allows controllable robots to dynamically adapt their motions in response to other robots' execution of manipulation skills. By resolving skill uncertainty directly at planning time, this approach avoids the pessimism of conservative baselines and unlocks robust, dynamic multi-robot coordination. Code for the planners is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [21] [Model-Based Geometry-Aware Generative Optimization for Constrained Locomotion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07772)
*Zhilin He,Xinyuan Wang,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出2GO，将主动约束几何转化为可执行去噪算子并自适应解耦生成传输与反向随机性，用于四足与人形机器人的约束运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足与人形机器人的约束运动规划需同时满足碰撞避免、接触一致、运动学可行与支撑约束，在高层动力学和非凸环境中极具挑战。已有基于模型扩散（MBD）方法虽可无演示地利用动力学与蒙特卡洛 rollout 估计去噪得分，但约束变体仍缺乏任务调制的主动约束几何来引导得分方向与反向随机性，且采用确定性 DDPM 式反向传输，缺少跨生成传输的自适应调度。

Method: 提出基于模型几何感知的生成优化（2GO）。通过法向诱导度量塑形、切空间随机滤波和基于 CFS 的收缩，将主动约束几何转化为可执行去噪算子；并通过自适应扩散与类流调度，将生成传输与反向随机性解耦。

Result: 在约束四足与人形运动任务上，2GO 在离散落脚点选择与连续姿态规划中表现优异，成功率更高、约束违规更少，并提升了执行兼容性。

Conclusion: 2GO 通过几何感知去噪与自适应生成调度，有效提升高维非凸约束运动规划的可行性与执行性能，为四足和人形机器人约束运动提供新思路。

Abstract: Constrained Locomotion Planning (CLP) for quadrupeds and humanoids, where robots must satisfy collision avoidance, contact consistency, kinematic feasibility, and support constraints, is challenging under high-dimensional dynamics and highly non-convex environments. Recent Model-Based Diffusion (MBD) approaches recast trajectory optimization as posterior sampling over trajectories, using known dynamics and Monte Carlo rollouts to analytically estimate the denoising score function without demonstration learning. While constrained variants further incorporate feasibility into model-based score rollouts and show promising performance, they are still limited by (1) lacking a task-modulated active constraint geometry that shapes the score direction and reverse stochasticity, and (2) using deterministic DDPM-style reverse transport without adaptive scheduling across different generative transports. Therefore, we introduce Model-Based Geometry-Aware Generative Optimization (2GO) for constrained locomotion, which turns active constraint geometry into executable denoising operators through normal- induced metric shaping, tangent-space stochastic filtering, and CFS-based retraction. 2GO further decouples generative transport from reverse stochasticity through an adaptive diffusion and flow-like schedule. Experiments on constrained quadruped and humanoid locomotion demonstrate strong performance in discrete foothold selection and continuous posture planning, with higher success rates, fewer violations, and improved execution compatibility.

</details>


### [22] [StairVLA: Stage-Aware Hierarchical Action Generation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07756)
*Shangyuan Yuan,Xinda Qi,Yujiang Pu,Wenliang Guo,Xiaobo Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StairVLA 是一种阶段感知的分层 VLA 动作生成框架，利用部分去噪动作作为高层长时域粗生成与轻量局部精炼之间的接口，在保持闭环修正的同时显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散或流匹配的 VLA 动作头通常对去噪轨迹进行均匀处理，但作者观察到去噪阶段的条件关注会自然转移：早期更依赖语言指令与视觉观测建立粗动作轨迹，后期更强调当前视觉观测以进行动作对齐。

Method: StairVLA 将动作生成解耦为高层 VLA 与轻量 refiner：高层 VLA 仅执行早期去噪，输出可复用的长时域部分去噪动作轨迹；轻量 refiner 以更高频率利用最新观测精炼局部动作块。部分去噪动作作为粗长时域生成与局部精炼的自然接口，从而摊销高层 VLA 计算并保留频繁闭环校正。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上，GR00T-style 实例将平均成功率从 96.5% 提升至 97.8%，并将每个动作块的摊销推理延迟从 115.0 ms 降至 44.2 ms。跨两种 VLA 骨干、仿真基准与真实机器人任务，StairVLA 均持续降低推理成本并保持强任务性能。

Conclusion: StairVLA 通过利用去噪阶段条件关注的转移，实现高效且保留闭环修正的 VLA 动作生成，在任务性能与推理成本之间取得更优权衡。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models increasingly rely on diffusion- or flow-matching-based action heads to generate continuous robot actions. These action heads typically process the denoising trajectory in a largely uniform manner. However, we observe that the conditioning focus naturally shifts across denoising stages: early stages combine language instructions and visual observations to establish a coarse action trajectory, whereas later stages place greater emphasis on current visual observations for action alignment. Based on this insight, we introduce StairVLA, a stage-aware hierarchical action generation framework that uses partially denoised actions as a natural interface between coarse long-horizon action generation and local refinement. A high-level VLA performs early denoising to produce a reusable long-horizon partially denoised action trajectory, while a lightweight refiner operates at a higher frequency to refine local action chunks using the latest observations. This design amortizes expensive high-level VLA computation while preserving frequent closed-loop correction. On LIBERO, our GR00T-style instantiation improves average success from 96.5% to 97.8% while reducing amortized inference latency from 115.0 ms to 44.2 ms per action chunk. More broadly, across two VLA backbones, simulation benchmarks, and real-robot tasks, StairVLA consistently reduces inference cost while maintaining strong task performance.

</details>


### [23] [CoRE: Learning Collaboration-Role Experts for Decentralized Collaborative Manipulation with One Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07752)
*Yanan Zhou,Zhaoyan Qian,Zihao Li,Mingyuan Ba,Ranpeng Qiu,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoRE：在单策略去中心化协作设定下，每个机器人仅凭视觉与本体感受运行同一策略，无需任务提示、身份标签或机器人间通信，从多任务、多机器人演示中学习协作角色专家，实现互补团队行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作操作要求机器人随交互进展执行互补动作；难点在于共享参数下学习互补团队行为，并仅依据各机器人局部观测选择恰当动作，同时避免依赖任务提示、身份标签与通信。

Method: CoRE融合外观与几何特征作为局部交互证据；采用查询条件交叉注意力专家提供可适应的预测路径，由局部路由器在每个动作块位置进行组合；训练时引入动作-专家对齐损失，利用固定输入下相对于演示的强制路由预测误差来监督专家选择，无需角色标签。

Result: 在多个仿真基准中，CoRE在评估的去中心化方法里取得最高平均性能；物理实验表明其可在多样操作任务中有效协作，并对伙伴延迟与减速具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过隐式协作角色专家与局部路由，CoRE可在无标签、无通信、无任务提示条件下学习单策略去中心化协作，兼具仿真性能与真实环境鲁棒性。

Abstract: Collaborative manipulation requires robots to perform complementary actions as interactions unfold. We study single-policy decentralized collaboration: every robot runs the same policy from its visual observations and proprioception, without task prompts, identity labels, or inter-robot messages. The challenge is to learn complementary team behaviors within shared parameters and select appropriate actions from each robot's local observations. We introduce CoRE, which learns Collaboration-Role Experts from pooled multi-task, multi-robot demonstrations. Fused appearance and geometry provide local interaction evidence. Query-conditioned cross-attention experts provide adaptable prediction paths, which a local router combines at each action-chunk position. During training, an action-expert alignment loss supervises expert selection using relative forced-route prediction errors against demonstrations under fixed inputs, without role labels. Across simulation benchmarks, CoRE achieves the highest average performance among evaluated decentralized methods. Physical experiments demonstrate effective collaboration across diverse manipulation tasks and robustness to partner delays and slowdowns. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [24] [Seeing Through the Displaced Frame: Privileged Noise Distillation for Vision-Force Precision Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07745)
*Ching-Hsiang Chang,Tzu-Yu Chuang,Yi-Hsiu Lee,Yi-Ting Chen,Yuan-Fu Yang,Min Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出精度装配中的位姿误差会同时污染机器人观测与动作坐标系，使偏移无法仅凭本体状态在接触前辨识；通过在训练中引入特权教师或闭式重标注来提供补偿监督，学生策略在FORGE基准和真机上显著提升抗位姿噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在FORGE基准上，官方基于状态的策略在真实位姿下成功率为97%至99%，但在σ=5 mm位姿噪声设置下仅32%至60%。核心问题是同一估计位姿既进入观测又锚定动作坐标系，接触前仅靠本体感知无法辨识该偏移，而部署时可用的传感信息本身不足以解决该问题。

Method: 训练阶段用两种方式提供缺失信息：一是特权教师在仿真中观测位姿偏移；二是将干净演示闭式重标注到位移后的坐标系。部署学生策略采用行为克隆并加一轮DAgger训练，测试时仅接收带噪状态、原始力/力矩窗口和两个RGB相机。

Result: 在未修改的FORGE任务上，教师路线学生在σ=0至5 mm下保持92%至99%成功率，而六个非特权基线降至2%至80%。匹配行为克隆实验显示：相同学生架构、数据预算和训练流程下，无法访问偏移的演示在σ=5 mm时仅31.5%成功率，特权演示为88.7%，重标注演示为95.8%。在Franka上零样本部署，学生策略在σ=5 mm下汇总成功率为83.3%，最强基于状态的策略仅34.4%。

Conclusion: 仅靠可部署传感不足以获得鲁棒性；鲁棒性要求监督信号能够编码对潜在坐标系偏移的补偿。

Abstract: Pose error in precision assembly can corrupt not only what a robot observes but also the coordinate frame in which it acts. On the FORGE benchmark, the official state-based policy succeeds in 97% to 99% of episodes with the true pose but only 32% to 60% at the benchmark's $\sigma=5$ mm pose-noise setting. The same estimated pose enters the observation and anchors the action frame, making the offset unidentifiable from proprioceptive state alone before contact. We supply this missing information during training in two ways. A privileged teacher observes the offset in simulation, while clean demonstrations can instead be relabelled into the displaced frame in closed form. The deployed student is trained with behaviour cloning followed by one DAgger round and receives only noisy state, a raw wrench window, and two RGB cameras at test time. On the unmodified FORGE tasks, the teacher-route student maintains 92% to 99% success across $\sigma=0$ to 5 mm, while six non-privileged baselines fall to 2% to 80%. A matched behaviour-cloning experiment isolates the source of this robustness. With the same student architecture, data budget, and training procedure, demonstrations generated without offset access yield only 31.5% success at $\sigma=5$ mm, whereas privileged and relabelled demonstrations reach 88.7% and 95.8%. Deployed zero-shot on a Franka, the student reaches 83.3% pooled success at $\sigma=5$ mm against 34.4% for the strongest state-based policy. Deployable sensing alone is insufficient. Robustness requires supervision that encodes compensation for the latent frame this http URL page: this https URL

</details>


### [25] [ESP: Energy-Score Policy for One-Step Multimodal Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07696)
*Lilika Makabe,Heecheol Kim,Yasuyuki Matsushita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ESP（Energy-Score Policy），一种无需教师模型、单次网络评估即可将策略上下文与噪声直接映射为动作块的VLA策略；使用能量分数而非均方误差训练动作头，避免迭代采样，在仿真与真实操作任务中取得有竞争力的成功率并显著降低动作生成延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散和流匹配的生成式动作模型能捕获多样行为，包括同一观测与指令下的多个有效动作序列，但其迭代采样过程需重复网络评估，增加闭环控制中的推理延迟。因此需要一种无需迭代采样、可直接学习多模态动作分布的高效策略。

Method: ESP将策略上下文和噪声直接映射为动作块，仅需一次网络评估。其动作头以能量分数而非均方误差进行训练：平方误差回归面向条件均值，而能量分数是严格适当评分规则，其期望值唯一由目标分布最小化。该目标为学习多模态动作分布提供了有原则的监督信号，无需迭代采样；当模型能够表示目标分布时，可在总体最优处精确恢复目标分布。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作任务中，ESP取得了与流匹配基线相当的任务成功率，同时动作生成延迟显著降低。

Conclusion: 结果表明，直接分布学习可作为迭代生成式机器人策略的一种高效替代方案。

Abstract: Generative action models based on diffusion and flow matching have been increasingly adopted in vision-language-action (VLA) policies for their ability to capture diverse behaviors, including multiple valid action sequences under the same observation and instruction. Their iterative sampling procedures, however, require repeated network evaluations to generate each action chunk, increasing inference latency in closed-loop control. We propose ESP (Energy-Score Policy), a teacher-free approach that maps policy context and noise directly to an action chunk in a single network evaluation. ESP trains the action head with the energy score rather than mean squared error. Whereas squared-error regression targets the conditional mean, the energy score is strictly proper: its expected value is uniquely minimized by the target distribution. This provides a principled objective for learning multimodal action distributions without iterative sampling, with exact recovery at the population optimum when the model can represent the target distribution. Experiments on both simulation and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate competitive task success with substantially lower action-generation latency than the flow matching baseline. These results support direct distributional learning as an efficient alternative to iterative generative robot policies.

</details>


### [26] [ExoBridge: Learning a Bare Hand to Hand-Worn Exoskeleton Mapping through Human Limb Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07692)
*Ruitong Tian,Xianyao Li,Noah B. Wilson,Fang Xu,Eric Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ExoBridge 框架，利用人类双臂耦合，从裸手视频学习到传感外骨骼运动与触觉状态的桥接函数，从而将人类视频转化为可迁移至灵巧机器人手的监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频是可扩展的机器人学习经验来源，但在保持裸手交互的同时获取运动与触觉监督仍很困难；外骨骼可提供同步运动与触觉测量，但穿戴外骨骼会限制裸手演示。

Method: 采集时一手保持裸手并提供视觉观测，另一手佩戴外骨骼并提供同步运动与触觉测量；利用配对演示训练时序视觉模型，从裸手视频预测指尖接触、连续触觉强度和相对编码器运动。外骨骼定义中间状态空间，其运动坐标通过已有标定与灵巧机器人手关联。

Result: 在 4 个操作任务、1215 个演示上，使用留出采集会话评估，合并任何接触 AUROC 为 0.916，触觉强度 Pearson 相关系数为 0.790；学习到的桥接还能从裸手视频预测外骨骼配置的相对变化。

Conclusion: 人类肢体耦合可将外骨骼测量转化为裸手视频的监督信号，建立人类视觉演示与面向机器人的操作接口之间的学习桥接。

Abstract: Human video offers a scalable source of experience for dexterous robot learning, but obtaining motion and tactile supervision while preserving bare hand interaction remains challenging. We present ExoBridge, a framework that leverages human limb coupling to learn a bridging function from bare hand video to the motion and tactile state of a sensorized exoskeleton. Our central idea is to use coordinated bimanual behavior to connect an uninstrumented visual source with a measured manipulation interface. During collection, one hand remains bare and provides visual observations, while the opposite hand wears the exoskeleton and supplies synchronized motion and tactile measurements. These paired demonstrations train a temporal visual model to predict fingertip contact, continuous tactile intensity, and relative encoder motion from bare hand video alone. The exoskeleton defines an intermediate state space whose motion coordinates are linked to a dexterous robot hand through existing calibration. Evaluation on 1,215 demonstrations across four manipulation tasks uses held out collection sessions and yields a pooled any contact AUROC of 0.916 and a Pearson correlation of 0.790 for tactile intensity. The learned bridge also predicts relative changes in exoskeleton configuration from bare hand video. These results demonstrate that human limb coupling can turn exoskeleton measurements into supervision for bare hand video, establishing a learned bridge between human visual demonstrations and a robot oriented manipulation interface.

</details>


### [27] [EigenDEXplore: Structured Exploration for Dexterous Manipulation with Human Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07681)
*Harsh Gupta,Tyler Ga Wei Lum,Changhao Wang,Chuer Pan,C. Karen Liu,Jeannette Bohg,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究灵巧操作中人类运动先验的作用方式，提出EigenDEXplore：不改动动作空间，而是在独立关节噪声上叠加沿人类数据特征向量的相关扰动，从而提升探索效率与操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作是高维优化问题，需要多关节协调运动；但RL和采样轨迹优化常采用独立关节扰动探索，难以发现协调行为。已有方法用人类手部数据学习低维协调动作空间以降低搜索难度，却限制表达性；将学习动作与关节空间动作结合虽恢复表达性，却增加维度和冗余。因此作者希望系统研究动作维度、探索策略和人类数据来源的影响。

Method: 作者在多种操作任务中比较不同动作表示与探索策略，并发现人类运动先验更应被用于结构化探索，而非改变动作表示。据此提出EigenDEXplore：保留原始关节空间动作不变，将人类数据导出的特征向量方向上的扰动加入独立关节空间噪声中，以诱导相关探索。

Result: 在多种灵巧手上，EigenDEXplore在抓取、手内重定向和接触丰富操作中持续优于关节空间和学得动作空间基线。收益覆盖无结构RL、参考引导RL、轨迹优化和sim-to-real部署，且在奖励塑形和课程设计较少时提升最大。

Conclusion: 人类运动先验用于结构化探索比改变动作表示更有效；EigenDEXplore通过保持动作空间不变并注入相关探索扰动，为灵巧操作提供了一种通用且有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation poses a challenging high-dimensional optimization problem, as useful behaviors require coordinated motion across many hand joints. In reinforcement learning (RL) and sampling-based trajectory optimization, exploration commonly relies on independent robot joint perturbations, making coordinated behaviors difficult to discover. Prior work reduces this search space for grasp learning using low-dimensional spaces of coordinated joint motions learned from human hand data, but this restricts the expressivity required for general manipulation. Some combine learned and joint-space actions to restore expressivity, but this increases dimensionality and introduces redundancy. We study these effects across diverse manipulation settings, varying action dimensionality, exploration strategy, and the source of human data. Our experiments suggest that human-motion priors are most effective when used to structure exploration rather than change the action representation. Motivated by this finding, we propose EigenDEXplore, which induces correlated exploration by adding perturbations along human-derived eigenvectors to independent joint-space noise, leaving the action space unchanged. Across multiple dexterous hands, EigenDEXplore consistently outperforms joint-space and learned action-space baselines in grasping, in-hand reorientation, and contact-rich manipulation. These gains span unstructured and reference-guided RL, trajectory optimization, and sim-to-real deployment, and are largest in settings with less reward shaping and curriculum design.

</details>


### [28] [QF3: Fast Flow RL with Filtered Q-Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08789)
*Chung Min Kim,Brent Yi,David McAllister,Hongsuk Choi,Himanshu Gaurav Singh,Jinkun Cao,Ken Goldberg,Pieter Abbeel,Carmelo Sferrazza,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QF3 是一种在线离策略强化学习算法，通过流匹配与经滤波的 critic 动作梯度训练流策略，可从头学习并微调机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流策略已成为从演示中学习机器人行为的常用策略类，但强化学习对于改进预训练流策略或通过交互从头学习仍至关重要；现有流 RL 方法在离策略效率与可扩展性上仍有不足。

Method: 提出 QF3：在线离策略 RL，结合流匹配与 critic 的动作梯度，并通过流输出的一步预测反传；为只在 critic 与预测可靠的区域更新，仅对接近回放动作的动作维度施加 critic 梯度；配合高吞吐离策略训练配方。

Result: 据称是首个从头训练人形机器人运动策略并零样本迁移到硬件的离策略流 RL 方法；在人形运动与动作跟踪任务上较近期在策略流 RL 方法 FPO++ 取得 10 倍墙钟加速；并在 ABC-Sim 与 Robomimic 上微调预训练流式操作策略。

Conclusion: QF3 既能从头学习机器人策略，也能细化从演示中获得的策略，表明其在流策略强化学习中的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Flow policies have become a standard policy class for learning robot behaviors from demonstrations, but reinforcement learning is still critical for improving pre-trained flow policies or learning them from scratch through interaction. We introduce QF3 (Fast Flow RL with Filtered Q-Gradients), an online off-policy RL algorithm that trains a flow policy with flow matching plus the critic's action gradient, backpropagated through a one-step prediction of the flow's output. To keep updates where the critic and this prediction are reliable, QF3 applies the critic gradient only to action dimensions that stay near the replay action. To our knowledge, QF3 is the first off-policy flow RL method to train humanoid locomotion policies from scratch and transfer them zero-shot to hardware. Paired with a high-throughput off-policy training recipe, it trains humanoid locomotion and motion-tracking policies with a 10x wall-clock speedup over FPO++, a recent on-policy flow RL method. We further apply QF3 to fine-tune pretrained flow-based manipulation policies on both ABC-Sim and Robomimic tasks. These results suggest that QF3 can both learn robot policies from scratch and refine those acquired from demonstrations. Website: this https URL

</details>


### [29] [PEARS: Physical-Prior-Guided Efficient Adaptation via Failure Reasoning and Diffusion Steering for Tactile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08784)
*Kun Song,Yiming Wang,Yilin Chen,Tianyi Ding,Jiaxin Tian,Tianqi Gong,Daolin Ma,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PEARS：一种物理先验引导的混合强化学习框架，用于带触觉反馈的预训练策略样本高效在线自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略在部署时遇到分布外（OOD）条件会显著性能下降，需要真实交互后训练；但基于RL的后训练通常需大量环境交互，在操作任务中每次尝试慢、贵或具破坏性。

Method: PEARS每回合后由物理引导力推理（PFR）模块利用VLM中的物理先验，结合视觉结果与触觉交互历史诊断失败并更新任务相关接触力边界；高频混合力-位置控制器在接触时执行这些边界；同时，触觉条件扩散引导强化学习调整冻结流匹配策略的潜在噪声，以纠正自由空间运动和接触时机错误，且不更新基座模型。

Result: 仿真中，PEARS相比最强逐任务基线将成功率提高12.4-37.4个百分点；达到特定成功率阈值所需交互回合数最多减少53.2%。真实实验中，白板擦除成功率达95%，移液管液体吸取成功率达90%。

Conclusion: 将PFR模块与策略引导结合，可在减少昂贵交互的同时加速策略自适应。

Abstract: Pretrained robotic policies can suffer substantial performance degradation under out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions encountered during deployment, motivating post-training through real-world interaction. However, reinforcement-learning (RL)-based post-training typically requires substantial environment interactions, a burden that is especially significant in manipulation, where each trial can be slow, costly, or destructive. Therefore, we present PEARS, a physics-prior-guided hybrid RL framework for sample-efficient online adaptation of pretrained policies with tactile feedback. After each episode, its physics-guided force reasoning (PFR) module uses physical priors encoded in a vision-language model (VLM) to diagnose failures from the visual outcome and tactile interaction history and update task-appropriate contact-force bounds. A high-frequency hybrid force-position controller then enforces these bounds during contact. Complementarily, tactile-conditioned diffusion steering reinforcement learning adjusts the latent noise of the frozen flow-matching policy to correct errors in free-space motion and contact timing without updating the base model. In simulation, PEARS improves success rates by 12.4-37.4 percentage points over the strongest per-task baselines. PEARS also reduces the number of interaction episodes required for a certain success threshold by up to 53.2% relative to the fastest baseline. In real-world experiments, PEARS achieves success rates of 95% on Whiteboard Erasing and 90% on Pipette Liquid Aspiration. These results show that combining the PFR module with policy steering can accelerate adaptation while reducing costly interactions. The project website is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [30] [DepthWorld: 3D World Model for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08780)
*Jai Bardhan,Josef Sivic,Vladimir Petrik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对视频世界模型缺乏一致3D几何的问题，提出DROID-3D校准数据集与DepthWorld模型：通过立体深度和联合因子图校准，联合预测多视角RGB与深度，在提升RGB预测的同时输出度量深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人世界模型可用于策略评估、改进和规划，但这些应用依赖忠实的3D几何。现有基于RGB的视频世界模型逐帧看似正确，却无法组成一致的3D世界。弥合差距需要大规模3D监督，以及不破坏强预训练视频先验的架构。

Method: 提出校准流水线，结合学习式立体深度与联合因子图，汇集同一物理机器人所有episode，恢复共享运动学参数与每场景外参；应用于DROID得到DROID-3D，提供密集度量深度和重校准多视角外参。随后训练DepthWorld，基于Stable Video Diffusion，通过空间潜在分块联合预测多视角RGB和深度，并保持预训练VAE不变。

Result: DROID-3D在90%外部相机episode上达到<0.7 px重投影误差。DepthWorld在相同训练预算下，深度监督使RGB预测相比仅RGB基线提升+1.48 dB PSNR，同时产生准确度量深度以支持下游几何推理。

Conclusion: 大规模3D校准监督与兼容预训练视频先验的架构可同时提升视频世界模型的RGB质量与3D几何一致性，为机器人世界模型提供更可靠的几何基础。

Abstract: World models offer a data-driven alternative to traditional simulators for robotics, with applications spanning policy evaluation, improvement, and planning. All of these uses depend on faithful 3D geometry, yet current video-based world models are trained on RGB alone and produce rollouts that look correct frame-by-frame but do not compose into a consistent 3D world. Closing this gap requires progress on two fronts: large-scale 3D supervision for manipulation, and an architecture that can absorb it without disturbing strong pretrained video priors. We introduce a calibration pipeline that combines learned stereo depth with a joint factor graph, pooling all episodes collected from the same physical robot to recover its shared kinematic parameters alongside per-scene extrinsics. Applied to the DROID dataset, this yields DROID-3D, a calibrated 3D dataset providing dense metric depth and recalibrated multi-view extrinsics (achieving <0.7 px reprojection error on 90% of episodes for external cameras). We then train DepthWorld, a Stable Video Diffusion-based world model that jointly predicts multi-view RGB and depth via spatial latent tiling, leaving the pretrained Variational Autoencoder (VAE) unchanged. Depth supervision improves RGB prediction itself by +1.48 dB PSNR over an identical RGB-only baseline at equal training budget, while simultaneously yielding accurate metric depth for downstream geometric reasoning.

</details>


### [31] [LBA-CBF: Rapidly Adaptive Safety Filters via Parallel Dynamics Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08765)
*Maitham F. AL-Sunni,Timeea-Andreea Radu,Hassan Almubarak,Henry Z. Liao,Michael Görner,Francesco Maurelli,John M. Dolan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Look-Back Adaptive Control Barrier Functions（LBA-CBF）：用短回看窗口内的最近预测误差对有限候选动力学库排序，并对与最优模型误差在容差内的所有模型施加高阶 CBF 条件，从而实现无需切换模型或连续参数估计器的自适应安全控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 CBF 基于假设动力学模型颁发安全证书；当系统发生突发且未测量的工况变化时，模型失配会使证书失效，而这正是安全最关键的时刻。

Method: 构建有限候选动力学库；以短回看窗口的预测误差对候选模型排序；对误差在最优模型容差范围内的每个模型执行高阶 CBF 条件，覆盖从最佳拟合自适应到全库鲁棒滤波的谱系；允许动力学非线性依赖未知参数，且不需要切换模型或连续参数化估计器。

Result: 证明只要保留一个具有安全代表性的候选模型，任何可行的滤波输入都满足真实 CBF 条件。四旋翼仿真中面对突变风反向与未知载荷，LBA-CBF 从所有随机初始条件均安全到达目标并匹配 oracle，而自适应与鲁棒基线成功率为 0–88%。最多 250,000 个模型的库可在控制环内运行；Crazyflie 2.1 与 F1TENTH 实验验证了对风、载荷释放及变化轮胎-路面摩擦的适应能力。

Conclusion: LBA-CBF 为存在快速未建模动态变化的安全关键控制提供了一种可扩展、实时可行的模型库自适应安全滤波框架，兼具理论保证与仿真/实物验证。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) certify commands through an assumed dynamics model, so an abrupt, unmeasured regime change can undermine the certificate exactly when safety matters most. We present Look-Back Adaptive Control Barrier Functions (LBA-CBF), which rank a finite bank of candidate dynamics by recent prediction error over a short look-back window and enforce the high-order CBF condition against every model within a tolerance of the best, spanning best-fit adaptation to full-bank robust filtering. The dynamics may depend nonlinearly on the unknown parameters, and no switching model or continuously parameterized estimator is required. We prove that any feasible filtered input satisfies the true CBF condition whenever a safety-representative candidate is retained. In quadrotor simulation with abrupt wind reversals and an unknown payload, LBA-CBF is safe and reaches the goal from all random initial conditions, matching an oracle, while adaptive and robust baselines achieve 0-88% success. Banks of up to 250,000 models run inside the control loop, and Crazyflie 2.1 and F1TENTH experiments demonstrate adaptation to wind, payload release, and varying tire-road friction. Code, videos, and project details are available at: this https URL

</details>


### [32] [PhoneBot: A Low-Cost Open Humanoid Robot Platform Reusing Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08737)
*Ruochen Hou,Quanyou Wang,Daniel Koh,Dennis W. Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhoneBot 是一个低成本、开源的人形机器人平台，利用现成智能手机作为主要感知与计算单元，以降低硬件成本并简化系统架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在教育科研中的推广受限于高硬件成本、复杂传感系统和较高计算需求。

Method: 将智能手机的 IMU、摄像头、无线连接和板载处理能力作为机器人核心感知与计算模块；采用模块化下肢结构，由 13 个低成本执行器驱动，并在躯干安装智能手机以支持感知、控制计算和人机交互；同时设计机械结构、软件架构和实时通信框架，实现稳定行走以及基于视觉的人体跟随、对话交互、拍摄和移动临场等功能。

Result: 实验评估表明，PhoneBot 能实现可靠行走、感知驱动交互，并可直接使用现成消费级智能手机部署；硬件和软件设计完全开源。

Conclusion: PhoneBot 为教育、研究和快速原型开发提供了一个经济、可复现的人形机器人平台。

Abstract: The adoption of humanoid robots in education and research remains limited by high hardware costs, complex sensing systems, and substantial computational requirements. This paper presents PhoneBot, a low-cost, open-source humanoid robot platform that repurposes commodity smartphones as its primary sensing and computing unit. By using a smartphone's integrated inertial measurement unit (IMU), camera, wireless connectivity, and onboard processing capabilities, PhoneBot reduces hardware costs and simplifies the system architecture. The robot combines a modular lower-body structure driven by 13 low-cost actuators with a torso-mounted smartphone that supports perception, control computation, and user interaction. We describe the mechanical design, software architecture, and real-time communication framework that support stable locomotion and capabilities including vision-based human following, conversational interaction, filming, and mobile telepresence. Experimental evaluations demonstrate reliable walking, perception-driven interaction, and straightforward deployment using off-the-shelf consumer smartphones. With fully open-source hardware and software designs, PhoneBot provides an affordable, reproducible platform for education, research, and rapid prototyping. More details are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [33] [Towards an Extensible Benchmark for Spoken Dialogue with Social Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08733)
*Casey Kennington,Ross Mead,Saad Elbeleidy,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向人机口语对话的基准，覆盖澄清请求、打断、具身信号与时间约束等交互现象，并引入Retico实时通信框架以支持机器人具备口语对话能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型可作为人与机器人之间的即插即用接口，但在语音、对话、快速交互与协作场景中仍存在重要挑战。

Method: 提出社区可用的基准，用于探索机器人与人类之间常见的口语对话现象（如澄清请求、打断、具身信号和时间约束），并计划扩展至其他人机交互重要方面；同时提出Retico实时通信框架以满足关键技术需求。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验结果，主要贡献为基准提案与Retico实时通信框架介绍。

Conclusion: 该基准与Retico有望促进人机口语对话研究，并支持更广泛的人机交互研究。 

Abstract: Language models provide a plug-and-play interface between humans and robots, but important challenges remain when speech, dialogue, fast interaction, and collaboration are required. We propose a benchmark for the community to use as a way to explore common spoken dialogue artifacts between robots and humans, including requests for clarification, interruptions, embodied signals (e.g., head nods or facial cues), and time constraints. We also explain our vision to extend the benchmark for other aspects of human-robot interaction that are important to the larger research community. To facilitate the benchmark, we further propose using \textit{Retico}, a real-time communication framework that fulfills important technical requirements to enable robots to have spoken dialogue capabilities.

</details>


### [34] [EgoLAP: Learning from Egocentric Human Data through Language-Action Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08726)
*Lihan Zha,Shresth Grover,Tenny Yin,Samuel M. Bateman,Hengkai Pan,Mengchao Zhang,Aykut Onol,Allen Z. Ren,Dhruv Shah,Anirudha Majumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoLAP 通过共享的语言化动作思维链联合预训练人类与机器人轨迹，将人类运动意图迁移至机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称人类数据可突破昂贵的机器人演示限制，但人机具身差异使原始人类轨迹难以直接作为控制监督目标。核心洞察是：底层动作虽具具身特异性，其运动意图却可捕获跨人机可迁移的任务相关结构。

Method: 提出 EgoLAP，一种 VLA 预训练框架，通过共享的、基于语言的动作思维链（action chain-of-thought）同时学习人类与机器人轨迹；将运动意图表达为结构化、时间抽象的语言动作，并结合基于场景几何、物理和物体可供性的运动级推理。

Result: 在大量真实世界与仿真实验中，EgoLAP 比替代动作表示更有效地将人类经验迁移至机器人控制，达到 80.1% 的平均真实世界任务进度，较替代动作表示提升 2.3 倍；运动级推理也优于结合子任务、物体框和视觉轨迹推理的复合推理格式。

Conclusion: 以语言化运动意图作为跨具身监督目标，可有效弥合人机具身差距，实现从人类经验到机器人控制的高效迁移。

Abstract: Egocentric human data offer a path to scaling robot learning beyond costly robot demonstrations, yet the embodiment gap makes raw human trajectories a poor supervisory target for control. Our key insight is that, although low-level actions are embodiment-specific, their underlying motion intent can capture task-relevant structure that transfers across humans and robots. We introduce EgoLAP, a VLA pre-training framework that jointly learns from human and robot trajectories through a shared language-based action chain-of-thought. EgoLAP expresses motion intent as structured, temporally abstracted language actions and pairs them with motion-level reasoning grounded in scene geometry, physics, and object affordances. Across extensive real-world and simulated experiments, EgoLAP transfers human experience to robot control more effectively than alternative action representations and reaches 80.1% mean real-world task progress, a 2.3x performance gain over alternative action representations. Motion-level reasoning also outperforms a composite reasoning format that combines subtask, object-box, and visual-trace reasoning.

</details>


### [35] [NMPP: Nonlinear Model Predictive Planning for Agile UAV Flight in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08695)
*Ondřej Procházka,Juraj Pauko,Robert Pěnička,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出非线性模型预测规划（NMPP），将感知障碍作为硬几何约束，并将全状态参考交给无视障碍的SE(3)控制器，显著提升四旋翼在杂乱环境中的跟踪精度与高速飞行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼在杂乱环境中飞行需要既无碰撞又动态可行、满足执行器限制的参考轨迹，以便控制器精确跟踪。现有方法要么在凸走廊内优化平滑多项式，限制敏捷性；要么将障碍视为软代价，与跟踪性能权衡。

Method: 提出NMPP，将感知障碍建模为硬几何约束，规划全状态参考轨迹，并交由无视障碍的SE(3)控制器跟踪。

Result: 位置RMSE比线性MPC轨迹规划器低58-67%，比多项式轨迹规划器低41-70%；在森林飞行中完成所有最高9.5 m/s的无碰撞飞行，在更激进速度下成功率达86%，而最先进规划器仅26%；实际部署在未知杂乱环境中可靠飞行至5.5 m/s。

Conclusion: NMPP通过硬几何约束与SE(3)控制器结合，提高了轨迹跟踪精度和敏捷性，实现了杂乱环境中的高速可靠飞行。

Abstract: Flying a quadrotor through a cluttered environment requires not only planning a collision-free reference trajectory based on perceived obstacles, but the reference also needs to be dynamically feasible and within the actuation limits of the vehicle, so that the controller can track it precisely. Existing methods either optimize a smooth polynomial inside a convex corridor, which limits agility, or treat obstacles as soft costs traded against tracking performance. We propose a Nonlinear Model Predictive Planning (NMPP) that imposes perceived obstacles as hard geometric constraints and hands a full-state reference to an obstacle-blind SE(3) controller. Our planner achieves a 58-67 % lower position RMSE than a linear Model Predictive Control trajectory planner and a 41-70 % lower RMSE than a polynomial trajectory planner. It also completes all forest flights with up to 9.5 m/s speed without collisions, and achieves 86 % flight success rate under a more aggressive speed profile where a state-of-the-art planner has only 26 % success rate. The real-world deployment showed reliable execution flying up to 5.5 m/s in an unknown cluttered environment.

</details>


### [36] [Magnet-Aware Control of Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08653)
*J. Playan Garai,S. B. Djuve,C. McGreavy,M. Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向强磁场环境的腿足机器人控制框架，通过磁力建模、磁场估计与动态补偿，提高四足机器人在大科学设施中的鲁棒性与可操作范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人可用于大科学设施以提高运行时间并减少人员暴露，但强磁场会损坏传感器，并产生随位姿变化的机械力/力矩，导致机器人失稳，使传统反应式控制器难以应对。

Method: 在MuJoCo中开发自定义物理插件以建模刚体元素所受磁力；提出逆磁场估计框架，从四足机器人动态响应及任意数量传感器读数中推断潜在磁场；设计磁感知模型预测控制（MPC）与全身控制（WBC）架构，在运动中预测并抵消磁扰动。

Result: 通过仿真和真实硬件实验验证。相比未补偿系统，力空间中最大可抑制磁扰提高2.5倍，力矩空间中提高1.6–2倍；在给定磁场内，机器人可操作面积增加29.34%，其中10.84%的区域原本会使未补偿控制器立即崩溃。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效增强腿足机器人在强磁场中的动态稳定性与可操作范围，并为大科学设施中的自主机器人部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous robots can increase uptime and reduce human exposure in Big Science facilities, but strong magnetic fields needed for their operation corrupt sensors and induce pose-dependent mechanical wrenches that destabilize robots and challenge conventional reactive controllers. This paper presents a control framework for modeling, estimating, and dynamically compensating for spatially varying magnetic wrenches acting on legged robots to improve robustness in these fields. We introduce a custom physics plugin for the MuJoCo simulator to model magnetic forces on rigid-body elements, alongside an inverse field-estimation framework to infer the latent magnetic field directly from quadruped dynamic responses and any number of sensor readings. Furthermore, we develop a Magnet-Aware Model Predictive Control (MPC) and Whole-Body Control (WBC) architecture that predicts and counteracts magnetic perturbations during locomotion to increase the range of magnetic fields in which the robot can operate. The effectiveness of the framework is validated through both simulation and physical hardware experiments. We show our method increases the the maximum rejectable disturbances from magnetic field in the force space by a factor of 2.5 and and between 1.6-2 times in torque space compared to a non-compensated system. Within the proposed magnetic field, this constitutes an increase in the area in which the robot can operate by 29,34% of which 10,84% would have previously caused an immediate collapse to non-compensated controllers.

</details>


### [37] [Fast Non-Parametric Heteroscedastic Imitation Learning With Geometric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08650)
*Maximilian Mühlbauer,Arne Sachtler,Markus Knauer,Cem Küçükgenç,Yanlong Huang,Alin Albu-Schäffer,João Silvério*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种融合几何先验的非参数概率建模与策略学习方法，在数据稀缺和异方差不确定性下实现数据高效学习、毫秒级更新及对新场景的快速适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类演示中学习概率策略需要数据高效且能快速适应新场景。现有非参数/核方法要么未考虑机器人中常见的流形几何，导致数据效率受限；要么虽几何感知，但不确定性估计不可靠或适应时需重新训练。

Method: 提出一种利用几何先验的非参数方法，用于数据稀缺和异方差不确定性下的概率建模；据此构建基于时间或机器人状态的策略。采用不可分离对角核，在同维输入输出下捕捉各自由度之间的不确定性关系；通过优化公式实现快速更新。方法支持流形值输入和流形值输出，并能处理较大姿态变化；结合任务参数化可适应不同物体位姿。

Result: 在包含位置和姿态的轨迹上，更新耗时少于3 ms；在若干玩具示例以及真实机器人操作任务中进行了评估，涵盖自主执行和共享控制场景。

Conclusion: 该几何感知的非参数概率策略方法能够支持流形输入输出、大姿态变化和任务参数化，在数据稀缺条件下实现快速更新与对新物体位姿的适应，并在真实机器人操作中验证了可行性。

Abstract: When learning probabilistic policies from human demonstrations, data-efficient learning and fast adaptations to new scenarios are key requirements. One popular way to achieve intuitive and reliable adaptations is through non-parametric, typically kernel-based, methods. However, existing solutions either fail to account for the geometry of manifolds common in robotics, limiting data efficiency, or, when geometry-aware, provide unreliable uncertainty estimates or require retraining to adapt. We propose a non-parametric approach leveraging geometric priors in scenarios of data scarcity and heteroscedastic uncertainties for probabilistic modeling. We utilize the method to formulate policies based on time or robot state, where non-separable diagonal kernels allow capturing uncertainty relations between degrees of freedom for same-sized in- and outputs. Fast updates, requiring less than 3 ms for a trajectory involving both position and orientation are possible through an optimized formulation. Our approach supports both manifold-valued input and manifold-valued output with large orientation changes. Using task parameterization, adaptation to different object poses is easily possible. We evaluate the approach on a set of toy examples and on real robot manipulation tasks both in autonomous execution and in shared control scenarios.

</details>


### [38] [HygieneRoboBench: Benchmarking Hygiene-Aware Planning for Household Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08642)
*Yurun Chen,Josh Qixuan Sun,Jason Qin,Chengtai Li,Tianyi Wang,Mark Crowley,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 HygieneRoboBench 基准和 Hygiene-NSP 规划器，用于在家庭机器人接触污染传播场景下，依据执行历史与用户优先级安全完成任务并优化卫生处理与执行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法联合评估规划器如何从接触历史识别卫生风险，并在新接触事件后规划安全续行；同时规划器还需在时间和资源约束下兼顾用户优先级。

Method: 构建 HygieneRoboBench，含 624 个实例、134 个任务族，覆盖双夹爪与共享物体的污染、处理成本与用户优先级，并通过受控历史、画像与事件对比及独立计划评估进行测试；提出 Hygiene-NSP，结合 LLM 接地的语义解析、接触历史重建与 CP-SAT，联合规划卫生处理和任务执行。

Result: Hygiene-NSP 达到 94.4% 安全完成率和 90.4% 最优安全完成率，在完整数据集上均高于所评估的基线规划器；对 LLM 与符号规划器的评估表明，安全完成任务不保证在用户优先级下成本最低。

Conclusion: 该工作表明卫生安全与成本最优需联合建模，Hygiene-NSP 通过接触历史与约束优化可有效提升家庭机器人任务的安全性与用户偏好满足度。

Abstract: Contact with contaminated objects can spread hazards through a household robot's grippers, tools, and shared surfaces, while new contacts can make an existing plan unsafe. Existing benchmarks do not jointly assess how planners identify hygiene risks from contact history and plan safe continuations after new contact events. Planners must do so within time and resource limits while respecting user priorities. We introduce HygieneRoboBench, with 624 instances across 134 task families, to evaluate safe resolution of household tasks from a given execution history. Tasks capture contamination through two grippers and shared objects, treatment costs, and user priorities. We combine controlled history, profile, and event comparisons with independent plan evaluation. These assess safe resolution, cost efficiency under user priorities, and responses to contact events. Evaluation of LLM-based and symbolic planners shows that safely completing a task does not guarantee the lowest execution costs under the user's priorities. To address this problem, we introduce Hygiene-NSP. It combines LLM-based grounding, contact-history reconstruction, and CP-SAT to jointly plan hygiene treatment and task execution under user priorities. Hygiene-NSP achieves safe resolution and optimal safe resolution rates of 94.4% and 90.4%, respectively. Both rates are higher than those of the evaluated baseline planners on the full dataset. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [39] [RIWANav: Recursive World-Action Models with Self-Improvement for Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08640)
*Jing Xie,Shouwei Ruan,Yubin Wang,Yuxiang Zhang,Haitao Yang,Songchang Jin,Dianxi Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RIWANAV，一种后训练框架，通过世界模型与策略的递归自我改进循环，提升长时程城市导航，避免昂贵的真实试错并缓解冻结世界模型随策略演化失准问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程城市导航依赖序列化局部决策，误差会随时间累积；模仿学习（IL）难以从失败中学习，而物理试错强化学习（RL）代价高昂。动作条件世界模型可通过预测候选动作的视觉后果提供想象反馈，但冻结的世界模型可能随策略演化而可靠性下降。

Method: 将世界模型与动作模型（策略）的耦合自适应建模为任务特定递归自我改进（RSI）。每轮交替两步：世界模型通过想象结果评估策略动作，为组相对策略优化（GRPO）提供比较反馈；改进后的策略构建 grounded self-curriculum，按行为新颖性和预测误差选择专家一致的动作-视频对；精炼后的世界模型为下一轮策略更新提供反馈，闭合成RSI循环。

Result: 实验表明RIWANAV优于训练基线和先前方法，验证了策略与世界模型之间的递归自我改进循环；真实世界试验进一步证明其实际适用性。

Conclusion: RIWANAV通过策略与世界模型的递归协同进化，为长时程城市导航提供了有效的后训练范式，并具备现实部署潜力。

Abstract: Long-horizon urban navigation requires sequential local decisions whose errors can compound over time. Imitation learning (IL) rarely learns from failures, while physical trial-and-error reinforcement learning (RL) is costly. Action-conditioned world models can provide imagined feedback by predicting visual consequences for candidate actions. However, a frozen world model may become less reliable as the policy evolves. In this paper, we introduce RIWANAV, a post-training framework that casts the coupled adaptation of a world model and an action model (policy) as task-specific recursive self-improvement (RSI). Each cycle alternates two updates. The world model evaluates policy actions through imagined outcomes, providing comparative feedback for group-relative policy optimization (GRPO). The improved policy then constructs a grounded self-curriculum, selecting expert-consistent action-video pairs by behavioral novelty and prediction error. The refined world model supplies feedback for the next policy update, closing the recursive self-improvement loop. Experiments show that RIWANAV outperforms training baselines and prior methods, validating the proposed recursive self-improvement loop between the policy and world model. Real-world trials further demonstrate its practical applicability.

</details>


### [40] [Feeling Through the Load: Compliant Quadruped Locomotion under Payload Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08637)
*Shaunak A. Mehta,Mayank Mishra,Prajit KrisshnaKumar,Sebastian Scherer,Koichiro Niinuma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种力感知四足机器人负重运动框架，利用机载测量估计非约束负载上的人为交互力，并使机器人柔顺响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在人类环境中负重时，人可能直接推拉负载而非机器人；非约束负载使机器人必须区分有意交互与普通负载运动，同时保持负载平衡与稳定行走。

Method: 将负载交互视为塑造机器人-负载复合系统运动的指令；分离力感知运动学习与力估计；结合柔顺负重策略与因果力估计器，通过估计器在环数据聚合和微调训练，从机载机器人测量中预测交互。

Conclusion: 该方法使四足机器人能够仅凭机载感知理解并顺应负载上的人为交互，实现人-机器人-负载协同运动。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are increasingly expected to carry objects while moving through human environments. But what happens when a person interacts directly with the payload rather than with the robot? If the payload is unrestrained, the robot must distinguish intentional external interactions from ordinary payload motion, while still keeping the load balanced and maintaining stable locomotion. How can a quadruped infer and compliantly respond to such interactions using only onboard measurements? In this work, we develop a force-aware locomotion framework that treats payload interactions as commands that shape the motion of the combined robot-payload system. Our approach separates the learning of force-aware locomotion and force estimation on an unrestrained payload. We combine a compliant load-carrying policy with a causal force estimator, trained through estimator-in-the-loop data aggregation and finetuning, to predict interactions from onboard robot measurements. Our simulations and real-world experiments show that the resulting controller can maintain stable payload-carrying locomotion, yield compliantly to external interactions, and use the inferred force to support human-guided changes in the robot's trajectory.

</details>


### [41] [A Swarm-Coordinated Multi-Robot System for Early Stress Detection in Agricultural Rows Using Multimodal Leaf Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08603)
*Rishi Gupta,Astha Goyal,Vinay Vishwakarma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出低成本地面多机器人系统CropSentry，利用多模态叶片传感持续监测作物胁迫；63次观测中总体分类准确率84.12%，无线通信成功率100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期作物胁迫检测对提高效率、减少时间/资金/人力浪费至关重要，但高光谱成像和AI系统等现代技术成本高、复杂度大，中小农户难以部署。

Method: 系统由两个自主机器人组成，逐行检测叶片颜色和环境数据；观测结果进行空间映射并发送至主机器人，主机器人以颜色编码行段生成实时网页仪表盘，展示作物健康状况。

Abstract: Early stress detection in crops is a necessity today to improve efficiency and reduce waste of time, money, and effort. However, most modern techniques, such as hyperspectral imaging and AI-based systems, are too costly and complex for medium and small-scale farmers to implement. This paper showcases CropSentry, a low-cost, ground-based multi-robot system that uses multimodal leaf sensing to continuously monitor crop health by tracking stress levels. The system comprises two autonomous bots that continuously detect leaf color and environmental data row by row. The observations are spatially mapped and sent over to the master bot, which uses color-coded row segments to generate a real-time web-based dashboard displaying crop health. After 63 observations were collected during the experiments, the results showed an overall crop health classification accuracy of 84.12%, with 82.60% for healthy plants, 88% for nutrient-deficient plants, and 80% for diseased plants. Also, 100% wireless communication success rate across 10 slave observations was achieved. Close-range leaf inspection across multiple bots can detect early stress in crops while remaining affordable, accessible, and scalable. It provides farmers with timely information to improve resource utilization and crop management.

</details>


### [42] [One for All, All for One: Coordinated Multi-Agent Diffusion Steering via Stochastic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08595)
*Riccardo Barbano,Vincent Pauline,Runchang Li,George Webber,Alexander Denker,Željko Kereta,Stefan Bauer,Francisco Vargas,Esmeralda S. Whitammer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CMDS，将冻结的预训练扩散模型作为可复用生成基元，通过学习控制协调其反向过程，实现多组件结构化输出的模块化生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一生成模型需同时学习组件分布及其交互，成本高且难复用；作者希望复用独立训练的组件生成器，仅学习如何协调它们以产生一致的结构化输出。

Method: 将协调建模为随机最优控制问题，学习控制以平衡装配级奖励（指定组合输出目标属性）与偏离预训练动力学的惩罚；学习到的控制摊销该优化，可复用到新任务实例。

Result: CMDS可恢复已知目标分布，用同一训练控制满足不同空间约束，并从退化混合中恢复个体源；在多智能体迷宫导航、关节机器人规划和文本条件人体运动中，将冻结模型转化为协调的多智能体生成器。

Conclusion: CMDS提供了一种模块化框架，通过协调而非重训预训练扩散模型，实现多智能体与组合结构化生成。

Abstract: Deep generative models often produce structured outputs composed of interacting components. Modelling these outputs with a single model requires learning both the component distributions and their interactions. We pursue a modular alternative: reuse independently trained component generators and learn only how to coordinate them to produce coherent structured outputs. Our framework, Coordinated Multi-Agent Diffusion Steering (CMDS), treats frozen pretrained diffusion models as reusable generative primitives and coordinates their reverse processes through a learned control. We formulate coordination as a stochastic optimal control problem, balancing an assembly-level reward that specifies the desired properties of the combined output against deviations from the pretrained dynamics. The learned control amortises this optimisation, allowing reuse across new task instances. Experiments show that CMDS can recover a known target distribution, satisfy different spatial constraints with the same trained control, and recover individual sources from degraded mixtures. Across multi-agent maze navigation, articulated robot planning, and text-conditioned human motion, CMDS turns frozen models into coordinated multi-agent generators.

</details>


### [43] [Towards Efficient Robotic Manipulation Models with Self-Recursive Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08555)
*Zijia Chen,Yuenan Hou,Yu Li,Weijie Li,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LCAM剪枝，一种免训练的非结构化剪枝方法，用于预训练机器人操作策略，通过动作损失条件化的激活矩和由粗到细递归预算分配，在LIBERO、OpenVLA及真实机器人任务中保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用剪枝准则多面向图像识别，倾向于保留权重幅值、局部重建或语言模型似然，而非闭环动作行为；直接用于机器人策略效果不佳，因此需要针对动作预测与闭环控制行为设计剪枝准则。

Method: LCAM使用行归一化权重贡献、在校准演示上测得的激活矩，以及输出方向对动作预测损失的敏感度来排序连接；并采用自递归粗到细流程：每轮嵌套粗剪后重新校准重要性，在稀疏拐点后利用留出的离线动作失真指导细粒度预算分配；剪枝后无需昂贵恢复训练或仿真器rollout。

Result: 在三个LIBERO套件和OpenVLA上评估，LCAM在广泛剪枝率下表现有竞争力；在LIBERO-Object与OpenVLA上，50%非结构化剪枝达到84.0%成功率，保留密集策略超过90%的成功率；真实世界机器人乒乓球任务也展示出良好效果。

Conclusion: LCAM能有效剪枝机器人策略，缓解通用剪枝准则与闭环动作行为不匹配的问题，在免训练且无需剪枝后rollout的条件下保持较高任务成功率，具有实际机器人应用潜力。

Abstract: Network pruning can reduce parameter redundancy in robotic policies. However, generic pruning criteria are tailored for image recognition tasks and commonly designed to preserve weight magnitude, local reconstruction, or language-model likelihood rather than closed-loop action behavior. Directly applying these pruning algorithms to robotic tasks yields unsatisfactory performance. In this paper, we propose Loss-Conditioned Activation-Moment (LCAM) pruning, a training-free method for unstructured pruning of pre-trained robotic manipulation policies. Specifically, we first rank connections using row-normalized weight contribution, activation moments measured on calibration demonstrations, and the sensitivity of output directions to the action-prediction loss. We further design a self-recursive coarse-to-fine procedure: importance is recalibrated after each nested coarse pruning stage, while held-out offline action distortion guides fine-grained budget allocation after a sparsity knee. Our algorithm is free from costly recovery training and simulator rollouts after pruning. Experiments on three LIBERO suites with competitive robotic policies, together with evaluations on OpenVLA, show that LCAM attains competitive performance across a broad range of pruning ratios. Notably, on LIBERO-Object with OpenVLA, our LCAM achieves 84.0% success at 50% unstructured pruning, retaining over 90% of the dense policy's success rate. Promising results on real-world robotic ping pong further demonstrate the effectiveness of our pruning algorithm.

</details>


### [44] [Micro Neural Policies for Safe Real-Time Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08541)
*Hongpeng Cao,Riccardo Curcio,Daniele Ottaviano,Marco Caccamo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出微型神经网络策略（MNP），通过进化策略与统计模型检验验证相结合，实现资源受限嵌入式设备上的安全、鲁棒、实时机器人控制，并成功完成零样本仿真到现实迁移与微控制器部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算资源严重受限的嵌入式设备上，实现安全且鲁棒的实时机器人控制仍具挑战，传统神经网络策略往往因模型过大、推理开销高而难以部署。

Method: 将进化策略（ES）与基于统计模型检验（SMC）的验证集成到策略搜索中，以合成微型神经网络策略；在Cartpole和四旋翼控制任务上进行大规模训练与评估，变化控制频率和网络架构；先在仿真中验证策略，再通过零样本迁移部署到物理系统。

Result: MNP显著缩小神经网络规模且不牺牲安全性与鲁棒性；在仿真到现实零样本迁移中保持控制性能；策略内存占用仅0.5至7.5 kB，可在微控制器上部署，实时推理抖动低于25 ns，芯片空闲时间超过97%，可承载额外工作负载。

Conclusion: MNP为资源极度受限的机器人系统提供了一种高度实用的安全、鲁棒、实时控制方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the synthesis of Micro Neural Policies (MNP) to enable safe and robust real-time robotic control on computationally constrained embedded devices. We demonstrate that integrating Evolution Strategy (ES) and Statistical Model Checking (SMC)-based verification for policy search can drastically reduce neural network size without compromising safety and robustness. We conduct a large-scale training and evaluation of MNP on Cartpole and Quadrotor control tasks, varying control frequencies and network architectures. After validating these policies in simulation, we evaluate their deployability through zero-shot transfer to physical systems. Our experiments show that MNP can successfully achieve safe sim-to-real transfer without sacrificing control performance. We then show that the policies' memory footprint, ranging from 0.5 to 7.5 kB, allows deployment on microcontrollers, where they achieve real-time inference latency with under 25 ns of jitter while leaving the chip idle for over 97% of the time for additional workloads. This makes them a highly practical solution for severely resource-constrained robotic systems.

</details>


### [45] [Pareto-Optimal Entropy-Regularized Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08532)
*Dimitrios S. Georgiou,Augustinos D. Saravanos,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PER-DDP，一种基于自由能/相对熵不等式的熵正则化种群DDP方法，通过先验引导采样、扩展rollout评估与Pareto筛选，在保持二阶结构的同时扩大探索范围，显著提升非凸轨迹优化的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力学、执行器限制与避碰约束下的轨迹优化是机器人学基础问题，但高度非凸。DDP作为高效二阶shooting方法易陷入次优盆地；现有采样增强变体仅在少量保留轨迹附近采样且仅依据代价选择，探索范围受限。

Method: 从自由能/相对熵不等式推导出熵正则化种群框架PER-DDP，包含三部分：先验引导采样围绕每条保留轨迹塑造探索；扩展rollout评估使采样策略在少量保留候选之外进行探测；Pareto筛选跨迭代保留满足任务约束的备选解。该方法将采样开销与优化种群规模解耦，在不牺牲DDP二阶结构的前提下拓宽探索。

Result: 在多个系统和数百个环境中，PER-DDP的成功率高于当前最优的采样增强轨迹优化方法，并能找到所有基线均无法到达环境中的可靠解。

Conclusion: PER-DDP通过熵正则化种群框架与Pareto筛选有效缓解DDP的局部最优问题，显著提升复杂约束下轨迹优化的成功率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Trajectory optimization (TO) under nonlinear dynamics, actuation limits and collision avoidance constraints is a fundamental problem in robotics, albeit especially challenging due to its highly non-convex nature. For this setting, Differential Dynamic Programming (DDP) is an efficient second-order shooting method, yet its local structure renders it vulnerable to suboptimal basins. Sampling-augmented variants mitigate this susceptibility through stochastic exploration, but often sample only around the few trajectories they retain for reoptimization, based solely on their cost which restricts exploration breadth. We introduce Pareto-Optimal Entropy-Regularized DDP (PER-DDP), an entropy-regularized population framework derived from the free-energy/relative-entropy inequality. Our method combines prior-guided sampling that shapes exploration around each retained trajectory, with expanded rollout evaluations that probe these sampling policies beyond the few retained candidates, and Pareto filtering for preserving task-constraint alternatives across iterations. This decouples sampling effort from the optimization population size and broadens exploration without sacrificing the second-order structure that makes DDP effective. Across multiple systems and hundreds of environments, PER-DDP achieves higher success rates than state-of-the-art sampling-augmented TO methods and finds reliable solutions in environments beyond the reach of all baselines.

</details>


### [46] [WareFly-VLA: A Vision-Language-Action Framework for UAV Navigation and Human Tracking in Smart Warehouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08526)
*Thinh D. Le,Son T. Nguyen,Duong Q. Nguyen,Dung D. Le,Ngo Anh Vien,H. Nguyen-Xuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出WareFly-VLA，一个面向智能仓库中语言引导无人机人体搜索、定位与跟踪的光真实感UAV VLA框架和数据集，包含507条人工遥操作飞行episode与8,504个高分辨率RGB transition，配对目标工人外观描述和同步4自由度控制命令；覆盖目标接近与人员跟随任务，并建立四个开源VLA模型基准。结果表明语言条件空中控制仍远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作和地面移动导航中表现突出，但智能仓库中语言条件无人机控制仍缺乏探索，主要受限于缺少同时提供连续低级飞行动作、细粒度自然语言目标描述和真实工业环境的基准。

Method: 构建WareFly-VLA：在NVIDIA Isaac Sim中采集507条人工遥操作飞行轨迹和8,504个高分辨率RGB transition；每个样本配对人工撰写的目标工人外观描述与同步4DoF控制命令。任务包括目标接近和人员跟随，涵盖遮挡、远距离搜索、高度变化和杂乱场景。在无泄漏的episode级协议下，以两种控制率统一评测SmolVLA、GR00T N1.7、pi_0和OpenVLA四个开源VLA架构，并提供同步视频、语言、动作、位姿与难度标注。

Result: 语言条件仓库空中控制远未解决：严格泛化设置下性能显著下降；连续动作建模持续优于离散动作token化；单帧输入下仅前向通道可可靠学习；当前基础模型接口从地面和人形平台迁移到空中平台效果较差。

Conclusion: 论文发布数据集、基线和评估协议，以支持智能仓库中语言接地的空中自主性；同步的多模态标注还可支持世界模型研究。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved impressive results in robotic manipulation and ground-mobile navigation, yet language-conditioned control of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) in smart warehouses remains largely unexplored, hindered by the lack of benchmarks that jointly provide continuous low-level flight actions, fine-grained natural-language target descriptions, and realistic industrial environments. This paper introduces WareFly-VLA, a photorealistic UAV VLA framework and dataset for language-guided human search, localization, and tracking in warehouse environments. It contains 507 human-teleoperated flight episodes and 8,504 high-resolution RGB transitions collected in NVIDIA Isaac Sim, each paired with a human-written appearance description of the target worker and a synchronized four-degree-of-freedom control command. Two aerial tasks are covered: target approach and person following, under occlusion, long-range search, altitude variation, and clutter. A unified benchmark of four open-source VLA architectures (SmolVLA, GR00T N1.7, pi_0 and OpenVLA) is established under a leakage-free episode-level protocol at two control rates. The results show that language-conditioned aerial control in warehouses is far from solved: performance drops substantially under strict generalization settings, continuous action modeling consistently outperforms discrete action tokenization, only the forward channel is reliably learnable from a single frame, and current foundation-model interfaces transfer poorly from ground and humanoid embodiments to aerial platforms. The synchronized video, language, action, pose, and difficulty annotations further support world-model research. The dataset, baselines, and evaluation protocol are released to support language-grounded aerial autonomy in smart warehouses.

</details>


### [47] [No Need to Stop the Fleet: Localized Anomaly Isolation via Dead Zones in AGV Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08524)
*Natalia Ogorelysheva,Falk Howar,Jian-Jia Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出“死区”机制，对AGV车队中的局部异常区域进行形式化隔离，避免全车队系统级急停；给出基本停靠与绕行逃逸两种策略，证明逆时针轨迹逃逸的无碰撞性与有界到达延迟，并通过实验比较三种方法的吞吐权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGV车队在生产和物流中遇到局部异常（如故障、危险或污染区域）时，通常采用系统级紧急停止。虽然安全，但即使仅小范围受影响也会导致整个车队停机，造成不必要的中断。因此需要一种附加的fail-operational机制，在不必全局停机时仅隔离异常区域，使未受影响机器人继续运行。

Method: 论文提出并形式化定义“死区”作为局部隔离方法，并设计两种运行策略：基本死区处理，即受影响机器人进入死区并停止；带逃逸的死区处理，即距离死区足够远的机器人绕行。对于逆时针轨迹上的逃逸，在机器人之间保持最小成对间距的前提下，证明逃逸过程无碰撞且到达延迟有界。实验评估验证理论保证，并刻画全系统急停、基本死区处理和带逃逸死区处理三种方法的吞吐量权衡。

Result: 理论证明：在逆时针轨迹逃逸中，若保持最小成对间距，则所有逃逸机器人无碰撞且有界到达延迟。实验评估验证了理论保证，并表征了全系统急停、基本死区处理、带逃逸死区处理三种方法之间的吞吐量折衷。

Conclusion: 死区可作为系统级紧急停止的补充fail-operational机制，实现局部异常隔离并维持未受影响机器人运行。带逃逸策略在特定条件下具备安全性与有界延迟保证，三种策略在吞吐量上存在可评估的权衡，适用于不同异常场景与运行需求。

Abstract: Automated Guided Vehicle (AGV) fleets in production and logistics environments rely on a system-wide emergency stop to handle local anomalies such as malfunctions, hazards, or contaminated areas. While safe, this approach halts the entire fleet even when only a small area is affected, causing unnecessary downtime. This paper proposes dead zones --- a formally defined localized isolation approach that serves as an additional fail-operational mechanism for situations where a full system-wide halt is not strictly needed. Rather than shutting down the entire fleet, a dead zone isolates the anomalous area, allowing unaffected robots to continue operating normally. We introduce two operational strategies: basic dead zone handling, in which affected robots enter a dead zone and stop, and dead zone handling with escaping, in which robots that are sufficiently far from a dead zone reroute around it. For escaping on counterclockwise trajectories we prove collision-freedom with a bounded arrival delay for all escaping robots, provided a minimum pairwise separation is kept between robots. Experimental evaluation confirms the theoretical guarantees and characterizes the throughput tradeoffs between all three approaches: full emergency stop, basic dead zone handling, and dead zone handling with escaping.

</details>


### [48] [A Belief-State World Model for Catheter Navigation under Sparse Fluoroscopy: A Planar Proof of Concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08469)
*Damini Rijhwani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出并验证一种基于物理世界模型的稀疏X射线荧光导航框架：在简化合成血管体模中，利用Cosserat杆动力学、稀疏投影观测和粒子滤波维持器械尖端状态信念，并在显著降低成像频率时保持定位与不确定性校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血管内导管导航需持续荧光透视，使患者和医护人员全程暴露于电离辐射；作者关注能否在有意稀疏的X射线采集之间维持导航，并让模型在内部状态估计不可靠时发出信号。

Method: 将稀疏荧光导航建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程：Cosserat杆模拟器提供转移动力学，稀疏含噪投影作为观测，粒子滤波维护器械状态信念；当前实现将状态简化为带几何接触代理的尖端状态。评估限于刻意简化的设置：合成平面血管体模、准静态杆模型和模拟投影，不使用临床或动物数据。

Result: 在该设置中，信念状态模型以连续采集频率的十五分之一获取观测时，跟踪模拟尖端的RMSE为1.30 mm（每帧为0.97 mm，三十分之一为1.51 mm）；报告的90%可信区间达到0.92的经验覆盖率；基于信念的接触风险估计判别不安全接触事件的AUROC为0.68。

Conclusion: 结果仅为简化仿真中的概念验证，而非临床就绪证明；其意义在于表明稀疏成像世界模型必须提供校准良好的信念，而不仅是点估计精度。

Abstract: Endovascular catheter navigation relies on continuous fluoroscopy, exposing patients and clinical staff to ionizing radiation throughout the procedure. We investigate whether a physics-based world model can sustain the navigation task between deliberately sparse X-ray acquisitions, and whether the model can signal when its internal state estimate is no longer reliable.
