We formulate sparse-fluoroscopy navigation as a partially observable Markov decision process where a Cosserat rod simulator supplies transition dynamics, sparse noisy projections provide observations, and a particle filter maintains a belief over the device state, reduced in this implementation to a tip state with a geometric contact proxy. We evaluate this formulation in a deliberately simplified setting: a synthetic planar vessel phantom with a quasi-static rod model and simulated projections, without clinical or animal data. In this setting, the belief-state model tracks the simulated tip with a root mean square error of 1.30 mm while acquiring observations at one fifteenth of the continuous rate (0.97 mm at every frame, 1.51 mm at one thirtieth), reported 90 percent credible intervals achieve 0.92 empirical coverage, and the belief-derived contact risk estimate discriminates unsafe contact events with an AUROC of 0.68. These results constitute a proof of concept on a simplified simulation rather than a demonstration of clinical readiness; their purpose is to establish that calibrated belief, rather than point-estimate accuracy alone, is the essential property a sparse-imaging world model must deliver.

</details>


### [49] [Behavioral Safety Assessment towards Large-scale Deployment of Autonomous Vehicles, Part II: Assessment Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08458)
*Henry X. Liu,Tinghan Wang,Xintao Yan,Haowei Sun,Zhijie Qiao,Kenneth Boyd,Shuo Feng,Greg Stevens,Greg McGuire*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文验证了一套面向自动驾驶行为安全的第三方测试框架，通过在仿真环境和密歇根大学Mcity实体测试场对开源L4自动驾驶系统进行测试，发现其仅具备14项行为胜任力中的6项，碰撞率约为人类驾驶员平均水平的1000倍，并暴露出未知不安全场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方自动驾驶安全评估有助于提升公众接受度、增强消费者信心并建立有效安全标准；本文旨在验证此前提出的系统化行为安全测试倡议/框架。

Method: 使用所提出的第三方测试框架，对开源Level 4自动驾驶系统（文中以 this http URL 指代）进行仿真环境测试和密歇根大学Mcity测试场的实体测试，以评估其行为安全表现。

Result: 该系统具备14项行为胜任力中的6项；碰撞率为3.01x10^-3次/英里，约为人类驾驶员平均碰撞率的1000倍；测试中还发现了若干此前未知的不安全场景。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了在广泛公开部署前开展自动驾驶行为安全评估、以提升AV安全性能的必要性。

Abstract: Third-party evaluations of autonomous vehicle (AV) safety can play a vital role in improving public acceptance, building consumer confidence, and establishing effective safety standards. In Part I of this study, we propose a dedicated third-party testing initiative for systematically evaluating AV behavioral safety. In this paper, we validate our proposed framework using this http URL , an open-source Level 4 Automated Driving System (ADS), tested both in simulated environments and on the physical test track at the University of Michigan's Mcity Testing Facility. The results indicate that this http URL possesses 6 out of 14 behavioral competencies and exhibited a crash rate of 3.01x10^-3 crashes per mile, approximately 1,000 times higher than the average human driver crash rate. During the tests, we also uncovered a number of unknown unsafe scenarios for this http URL . These findings underscore the necessity of behavioral safety evaluations for improving AV safety performance prior to widespread public deployment.

</details>


### [50] [Behavioral Safety Assessment towards Large-scale Deployment of Autonomous Vehicles, Part I: Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08450)
*Henry X. Liu,Tinghan Wang,Xintao Yan,Haowei Sun,Zhijie Qiao,Kenneth Boyd,Shuo Feng,Greg Stevens,Greg McGuire*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向自动驾驶行为安全的第三方评估框架，包含行为胜任力测试与驾驶智能测试，并在 Part II 中用开源 L4 自动驾驶系统验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶落地进展显著，但安全仍是规模化部署的关键障碍。传统功能安全主要从车辆中心视角验证 AV 硬件与软件的可靠性、鲁棒性和充分性，无法充分覆盖 AV 与周边交通环境的交互及其行为影响。

Method: 提出向行为安全的范式转变，构建第三方 AV 安全评估框架，包含两个互补部分：行为胜任力测试在受控场景中评估 AV 的反应行为，确保基本行为胜任力；驾驶智能测试在自然交通条件下评估 AV 的交互行为，量化安全关键事件频率，以在大规模部署前提供具有统计意义的安全指标。

Result: 在 Part II 中，使用开源 Level 4 自动驾驶系统进行测试，以证明所提方法的有效性。摘要未给出具体量化结果。

Conclusion: 该框架将安全评估从车辆本体扩展到交通环境中的行为交互，可为 AV 部署前提供系统化且具有统计意义的安全评估；Part II 的开源 L4 ADS 测试用于验证方法有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) have significantly advanced in real-world deployment in recent years, yet safety continues to be a critical barrier to widespread adoption. Traditional functional safety approaches, which primarily verify the reliability, robustness, and adequacy of AV hardware and software systems from a vehicle-centric perspective, do not sufficiently address the AV's broader interactions and behavioral impact on the surrounding traffic environment. To overcome this limitation, we propose a paradigm shift toward behavioral safety, a comprehensive approach focused on evaluating AV responses and interactions within the traffic environment. To systematically assess behavioral safety, we introduce a third-party AV safety assessment framework comprising two complementary evaluation components: the Behavioral Competency Test and the Driving Intelligence Test. The Behavioral Competency Test evaluates the AV's reactive behaviors under controlled scenarios, ensuring basic behavioral competency. In contrast, the Driving Intelligence Test assesses the AV's interactive behaviors within naturalistic traffic conditions, quantifying the frequency of safety-critical events to deliver statistically meaningful safety metrics before large-scale deployment. In Part II of this study, an open-source Level 4 Automated Driving System (ADS) is tested to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [51] [ActTune: Action-Aware Precision and GPU Operating-Point Adaptation for Energy-Efficient Vision-Language-Action Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08444)
*Zou Qingyun,Bin Gao,Wenju Zhao,Weng-Fai Wong,Bingsheng He,Tulika Mitra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActTune 面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的推理能耗问题，以“每次成功任务的 GPU 能耗”为目标，在不显著增加推理延迟的前提下，通过动作感知的精度分配与 GPU 工作点选择，降低能耗并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略需反复调用推理来控制机器人，GPU 能耗成为任务执行的持续成本。仅降低单次推理能耗未必降低单次成功任务能耗，因为数值误差可能导致失败，或较慢推理延长执行时间。因此需在保持任务成功率且推理延迟增加不超过 10% 的约束下，优化每次成功任务的 GPU 能耗。

Method: ActTune 基于两点观察：量化敏感度因动作类别、模型层以及权重/激活而异；数值精度会改变工作负载并影响有利的 GPU 工作点。其框架将逐层精度分配与工作负载相关的 GPU 工作点选择相连接，后者作用于请求的频率-功耗上限对。轻量决策树直接从配置动作误差中学习分裂规则与叶节点精度配置，并在每次策略调用前选择精度。控制器预测下一工作负载，并依据延迟预算校准的查找表，以异步方式应用所选 GPU 工作点。共享常驻量化权重库支持配置切换，无需权重重构或额外策略评估。

Result: 在 LIBERO 终身机器人学习基准上，ActTune 相对当前最优方法将平均任务成功率最高提升 2.3%。相对原始 BF16 实现，其推理速度最高提升 2.02 倍；结合 GPU 工作点自适应后，每次成功任务的能耗最高降低 76.8%。

Conclusion: ActTune 表明，面向成功任务的能耗优化需联合考虑动作感知精度分配与 GPU 工作点自适应；该方法在保持或提升任务成功率的同时，显著提升推理速度并大幅降低每次成功任务的 GPU 能耗。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies repeatedly invoke inference to control robots, making graphics processing unit (GPU) energy a recurring cost of task execution. Reducing energy per inference call, however, may not reduce energy per successful task if numerical errors increase failures or slower inference prolongs execution. We therefore target GPU energy per successful task while preserving task success and keeping the inference-latency increase within 10\%. Our approach builds on two observations: quantization sensitivity varies across action classes, model layers, and weights versus activations; and numerical precision changes the workload, shifting favorable GPU operating points. We introduce ActTune, an action-aware framework that connects layer-wise precision allocation with workload-dependent GPU operating-point selection over requested frequency--power-cap pairs. A lightweight decision tree learns its splits and leaf precision configurations directly from configuration action errors, then selects precision before each policy call. The controller forecasts the next workload and applies the selected GPU operating point asynchronously using a lookup table calibrated under a latency budget. A shared resident quantized weight bank enables configuration switching without weight reconstruction or additional policy evaluations. On LIBERO, a benchmark for lifelong robot learning, ActTune improves mean task success by up to 2.3\% relative to state of the art. Relative to the original BF16 implementations, it delivers up to $2.02\times$ faster inference and, with GPU operating-point adaptation, reduces energy per successful task by up to 76.8\%.

</details>


### [52] [Safe Multi-Robot Collaborative Transport Using Density Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08441)
*Jagannath Prasad Sahoo,Sriram S. K. S. Narayanan,Umesh Vaidya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向多四足机器人安全协作操控的分层密度基模型预测控制框架，无需预计算参考轨迹即可将共享物体推至目标位姿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多四足机器人协作推物体时，需在狭窄通道等复杂环境中同时保证目标收敛、避障、机器人间安全以及全身接触动力学约束；且仅给定初始与目标位姿，难以依赖预计算参考轨迹。

Method: 采用分层密度基MPC：中心化box-level MPC优化接触力，并施加控制密度约束以实现目标收敛与避障；每台机器人再在共享信息假设下求解分布式robot-level全身MPC，跟踪移动接触位置，同时考虑静态障碍与邻机器人时变位置。

Result: 在MuJoCo中使用全身接触动力学，对两台和三台Unitree Go2协作推动刚性物体穿越狭窄通道进行验证；与匹配的Control Barrier Function及RRT*跟踪基线相比，所提密度基方法在仅推、力与力矩耦合的操控任务中表现有效。

Conclusion: 该分层密度基MPC框架能够在不依赖预计算参考轨迹的情况下，实现多四足机器人安全协作操控，并有效处理目标收敛、避障、机器人间协调与全身接触控制问题。

Abstract: This paper presents a hierarchical density-based model predictive control framework for safe collaborative manipulation by multiple quadrupedal robots. The framework enables a team of robots to push a shared object to a desired pose using only the initial and goal poses, without requiring a precomputed reference trajectory. A centralized box-level MPC optimizes contact forces while enforcing a control-density constraint for goal convergence and obstacle avoidance. Each robot then solves its own distributed robot-level whole-body MPC, under a stated shared-information assumption, to track its moving contact location while accounting for static obstacles and the time-varying positions of neighboring robots. The approach is evaluated in MuJoCo using whole-body contact dynamics for two and three Unitree Go2 quadrupeds collaboratively pushing rigid objects through narrow passages. Comparisons with matched Control Barrier Function and RRT* based tracking baselines demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed density-based formulation for push-only, force- and torque-coupled manipulation tasks. Implementation videos are available at this https URL

</details>


### [53] [MIM-VLA: Learning Physical Interaction Representations from Gripper Motor Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08425)
*Jaeyoung Lee,Jiyeon Koo,Taehwa Kim,Yerin Cha,Andrew Jaeyong Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIM-VLA 是一种基于电机反馈的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）架构：将夹爪近期电流、位置、速度及信号有效性编码为128维交互token，仅条件化SmolVLA的夹爪动作通路，从而在抓取中显式利用接触后物理响应，并支持交互选择与解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略主要依赖视觉观测和机器人状态推断抓取动作，未显式表征接触后的物理响应；而引入触觉阵列、力/力矩传感器或校准力估计又会增加硬件与标定成本。

Method: 方法将夹爪近期电流、位置、速度和信号有效性编码为128维交互token；仅用电机数据预训练Motor Interaction Module（MIM），使用人工审核的接触与交互阶段标签；该token仅条件化SmolVLA的夹爪动作通路，不改变机械臂动作与位置控制接口；同一token还用于MEM选择器VLM，对候选交互进行比较并输出证据条件化的选择与解释。

Result: 在三个真实场景中评估：比较视觉不同物体的交互阻力、通过主动探测区分视觉相似的真实与复制物体、轻柔抓取易碎物体（含未见实例）。在13对物体中，MIM-VLA以75.0%选择更高阻力物体，SmolVLA基线为48.8%。该方法使用夹爪已有的电机反馈，无需额外触觉阵列、力/力矩传感器、校准力估计或直接电流控制。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅利用夹爪电机反馈即可为VLA策略引入接触后物理交互表征，在不增加传感器硬件和改变底层控制接口的情况下提升抓取决策与交互选择能力，并提供可解释性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies infer grasp actions primarily from visual observations and robot state, but do not explicitly represent the physical response observed after contact. We present MIM-VLA, a motor-feedback-based architecture that encodes recent gripper current, position, velocity, and signal validity as a 128-dimensional interaction token. A motor-only Motor Interaction Module (MIM) is pretrained with human-reviewed contact and interaction-phase labels and then conditions only the gripper-action pathway of SmolVLA; arm actions and the position-control interface remain unchanged. The same token supports the MEM selector VLM that compares candidate interactions and produces evidence-conditioned selections and explanations. We evaluate MIM-VLA in three real-world settings: comparing the interaction resistance of visually different objects, disambiguating visually similar real and replica objects through active probing, and gently grasping fragile objects, including held-out instances. Across 13 object pairs, MIM-VLA selects the higher-resistance object in 75.0% of trials, compared with 48.8% for the SmolVLA baseline. For the evaluated tasks, the approach uses motor feedback already available from the gripper and does not require an additional tactile array, force-torque sensor, calibrated force estimate, or direct current control.

</details>


### [54] [Post-Grasp Kinematic Repair for Robotic Insertion via Object-in-Gripper Reorientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08421)
*Haegu Lee,Christoffer Sloth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出通过物体在夹爪内的小幅重定向来修复抓取后运动学可行性的方法：在固定插入路径下，以分支感知IK图最大化最小可行性裕度来评估候选，并利用任务条件学习先验加速搜索，最终通过触觉外部操作执行重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定抓取并不保证机器人插入运动学可行，因为物体-夹爪变换可能使机器人沿指定插入路径接近奇异位形或关节限位，因此需要在抓取后修复运动学可行性。

Method: 给定已实现的抓取和固定插入路径，搜索小幅物体在夹爪内的重定向。候选重定向由分支感知IK图评估，目标为最大化离散插入路径上的最小可行性裕度；同时使用任务条件学习先验改进查询排序。所选重定向通过基于触觉的外部操作执行。

Result: 在UR5e仿真中，无学习排序的规划器相比基于网格的序贯IK，在成功试验中将平均重定向量减少51.5%；加入学习排序后，该规划器平均规划时间进一步减少51.1%。真实机器人实验验证了完整流程。

Conclusion: 物体在夹爪内重定向可有效修复抓取后插入的运动学可行性；结合分支感知IK评估、学习排序与触觉外部操作，可减少重定向量和规划时间，并具备真机可行性。

Abstract: A stable grasp does not guarantee kinematically feasible robotic insertion because the object-in-gripper transform may force the robot towards singularities or joint limits along the prescribed insertion path. We study post-grasp kinematic feasibility repair through object-in-gripper reorientation. Given an achieved grasp and a fixed insertion path, we seek a small reorientation that restores kinematic feasibility. Sequential IK can miss such candidates by following an unfavorable joint-space path, while the nonsmooth feasibility landscape makes the search computationally expensive. We evaluate candidates using a branch-aware IK graph that maximizes the minimum feasibility margin over the discretized insertion path and use a learned task-conditioned prior to improve query ordering. The selected reorientation is executed through tactile-based extrinsic manipulation. In UR5e simulations, the planner without learned ranking reduces mean reorientation over successful trials by 51.5% compared with grid-based sequential IK. Adding learned ranking reduces this planner's mean planning time by an additional 51.1%. Real-robot experiments validate the complete pipeline.

</details>


### [55] [From Legs to Wheels: Embodiment-Aware Human Motion Retargeting for Mobile-Base Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08381)
*Jiyeon Koo,Eunseom Pyo,Jeonghee Seo,Taehwa Kim,Andrew Jaeyong Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出任务条件的人到移动底座人形机器人动作重定向框架，将人体运动分配给底盘、升降和双臂，并生成50 Hz可执行命令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频可提供可扩展的机器人演示，但现有人到人形机器人重定向方法通常假设机器人具有类人腿式运动学，不适用于带轮式底座、垂直升降和双臂的移动底座人形机器人。

Method: 该框架先按任务将重建的人体运动分配到base、lift和arm职责，再进行机器人特定实现。分配器保持人体路径、稳定朝向、必要时分离转向与平移、重定时命令以满足base限制，并修复lift和arm轨迹。部署适配器通过静止base检测、死区与变化率滤波、时间一致回放缩放以及独立线/角增益，将参考转换为50 Hz命令。

Result: 通过基于人体派生的任务空间比较、无策略仿真回放以及物理机器人上的定性执行进行评估。

Conclusion: 该框架针对轮式移动人形机器人的运动学不匹配，为从人类视频生成可部署控制参考提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Human video offers a scalable source of robot demonstrations, yet most human-to-humanoid retargeting methods assume a legged robot with human-like kinematics. This assumption does not hold for mobile-base humanoids equipped with a wheeled base, vertical lift, and two arms. Human walking must be expressed through base motion, while torso bending may require coordinated lift and arm motion. We address this mismatch with a task-conditioned framework that assigns reconstructed human motion to base, lift, and arm responsibilities before robot-specific realization. The allocator preserves the human-derived path, stabilizes heading, separates turn and translation when needed, retimes commands to satisfy base limits, and repairs lift and arm trajectories. A deployment adapter then converts the reference to 50 Hz commands using stationary-base detection, deadband and slew-rate filtering, time-consistent playback scaling, and separate linear and angular gains. We evaluate the resulting references with human-derived task-space comparisons, policy-free simulation replay, and a qualitative execution on a physical robot.

</details>


### [56] [How Much Planning Is Enough? Reducing Search and Computation in World-Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08350)
*Changbai Li,Sirui Li,Yichen Yang,Tongfei Chen,Zichao Feng,Shuwei Shao,Huobin Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SufficientPlan 部署框架，通过按模型-任务对认证“足够”的规划预算并复用静态上下文，在不修改世界模型或规划器的前提下，降低视觉世界模型决策时搜索预算与规划延迟，同时保持有竞争力的控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型可通过决策时动作搜索实现目标导向控制，但部署效率常受保守的大规划预算限制。作者发现：无需与 Full-budget 动作一致也能取得有竞争力性能；充分预算因模型-任务对而异；迭代规划器会重复编码与求解无关的上下文。

Method: SufficientPlan 无需修改预训练世界模型或更新规划器。其 PSBC 组件使用配对闭环证据，在预设 Full 性能容差内搜索并认证缩减后的模型-任务特定预算；其 SCR 组件跨搜索迭代缓存观测和目标表示，同时保留候选依赖的规划过程与最终所选动作。

Result: 在多个世界模型骨干和视觉控制任务上，SufficientPlan 显著降低搜索预算和规划延迟，并保持有竞争力的控制性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过充分预算认证与静态上下文复用，可提升视觉世界模型的部署效率，而无需牺牲控制性能或改动预训练模型与规划器。

Abstract: Visual world models enable goal-directed control through decision-time action search, but their deployment efficiency is often limited by conservatively large planning budgets. We show that competitive task performance can be achieved without agreement with the Full-budget action, that sufficient budgets vary across model--task pairs, and that iterative planners repeatedly encode solve-invariant context. To address these inefficiencies, we propose {SufficientPlan}, a simple deployment framework that requires no modification to pretrained world models or planner updates. Its {Paired Sequential Budget Certification (PSBC)} component uses paired closed-loop evidence to search for and certify a reduced model--task-specific budget within a predefined Full-performance tolerance. Its {Static-Context Reuse (SCR)} component caches observation and goal representations across search iterations while preserving candidate-dependent planning and selected actions. Experiments across multiple world-model backbones and visual-control tasks show that SufficientPlan substantially reduces search budgets and planning latency while maintaining competitive control performance.

</details>


### [57] [Event-Driven Proactive Robot Assistance through Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08344)
*Fengkai Liu,Hao Su,Haozhuang Chi,Rui Geng,Congzhi Ren,Xuqing Liu,Chenfei Xu,Yuichi Ohsita,Liyun Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出事件驱动的主动辅助框架：通过监测人-物交互结果触发推理，用冻结VLM推断任务上下文并生成可执行辅助动作；无需推理时用户任务说明，在三个真实桌面协作任务上达到与指令驱动变体相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作操作辅助通常依赖用户指令，属于请求驱动；而流畅的人类团队合作常根据已观察到的动作结果主动推断下一步帮助，而非等待指令。因此，本文研究事件驱动的主动辅助，使推理由人-物交互结果触发，且推理时无需用户提供任务规格。

Method: 框架包含事件监视器，持续监测工作区状态变化；事件完成后提取稳定的前/后快照以刻画状态转移。冻结的预训练视觉语言模型利用语义先验推断任务上下文，判断是否需要辅助，并在需要时基于观察到的状态转移生成辅助动作序列。为保证输出可执行和可验证，动作被限制为动作原语，对象通过整数ID引用。整个框架无需任务特定训练或微调。

Result: 在三个不同的真实世界桌面协作任务上评估同一框架，未进行任务特定训练或微调。事件驱动框架的性能与给定用户指令的变体相当。

Conclusion: 事件驱动式主动辅助可行：仅依据人-物交互结果即可触发高阶推理并生成可执行帮助，减少对显式用户指令的依赖，并能在多任务真实场景中达到接近指令驱动方法的性能。

Abstract: Assistance in collaborative manipulation is often initiated by user instructions, making high-level reasoning request-driven. In fluent human teamwork, however, partners often infer the next helpful step from the observed outcome of an action rather than waiting for instructions. Motivated by this, we investigate an event-driven formulation of proactive assistance, where human--object interaction outcomes initiate assistive reasoning without user-provided task specifications at inference time. To this end, we propose an event-driven framework that monitors workspace state changes with an event monitor and, upon event completion, extracts stabilized pre/post snapshots that characterize the resulting state transition. A frozen pretrained Vision-Language Model (VLM) then uses its semantic priors to infer the task context, decide whether assistance is appropriate, and, when needed, generate a sequence of assistive actions from the observed transition. To make outputs executable and verifiable, we restrict actions to a set of action primitives and reference objects via integer this http URL evaluate the same framework across three distinct real world tabletop collaboration tasks without task-specific training or fine-tuning. The event-driven framework achieves performance comparable to variants given user instructions.

</details>


### [58] [SC3BF: Shifted Collision Cone Control Barrier Function for Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08332)
*Amin Kashiri,Yasin Yazıcıoğlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出移位碰撞锥控制障碍函数（SC3BF），通过状态相关的容许量放宽传统碰撞锥的保守性，在保证安全的同时允许更高效的避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统速度空间控制障碍函数使用的碰撞锥过于保守：只要相对速度指向障碍物，无论速度多慢都会被拒绝，导致机器人难以靠近或绕过障碍物，灵活性不足。

Method: 在碰撞锥条件中引入状态相关的容许量，形成SC3BF，使机器人可以随距离和自身速度增大而提高接近障碍物的速率；该方法可通过普通二次规划（QP）实现，并在有界输入下保证安全集前向不变，无需最小前进速度或间隙裕度。作者证明总能找到保持安全的非零容许量，并给出闭式解。

Result: 在运动学自行车模型、最多100个移动障碍物的场景中，与三种速度空间基线方法相比，SC3BF到达目标更频繁，且对名义输入的修改量不到基线的一半。

Conclusion: SC3BF有效缓解了碰撞锥CBF的保守性，在无需最小前进速度和间隙裕度的情况下保持安全性，并减少对名义控制输入的干预，提升避障导航成功率。

Abstract: The collision cone used by velocity-space control barrier functions is conservative: it rejects every relative velocity aimed into an obstacle, however slow. We propose the \emph{shifted collision-cone CBF} (SC3BF), which adds a state-dependent \emph{allowance} to the cone condition, so the robot may approach the obstacle at a rate that grows with distance and with its own speed. SC3BF is enforced by an ordinary quadratic program, and its safe set is forward invariant under bounded inputs without a minimum forward speed or a clearance margin. We prove that a nonzero allowance preserving safety always exists, and derive one in closed form. Against three velocity-space baselines on a kinematic bicycle among up to $100$ moving obstacles, SC3BF reaches the goal more often and modifies the nominal input less than half as much.

</details>


### [59] [Communication-Free Obstacle Localization from Aggregate Wrench Measurements in Leader--Follower Cooperative Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08324)
*Amin Kashiri,Aditya Mohan,Yasin Yazıcıoğlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究多机器人协同搬运刚性负载时，在无机器人间显式通信条件下，利用领导者测得的跟随者总力旋量实现障碍物定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation:  leader 只能测量跟随者施加在负载上的合力与合力矩，无法直接区分各跟随者的个体反应；同时机器人间没有显式通信，因此需要仅凭聚合力旋量推断障碍物位置并识别响应的跟随者。

Method: 设计跟随者控制律，使每个跟随者在靠近障碍物时抵抗朝向障碍物的运动，从而在负载平移/角速度与聚合力旋量之间形成分段线性关系；相邻线性区域的变化用于揭示障碍物的方位与距离，并识别响应跟随者。进一步给出固定负载构型下精确恢复的充分条件，并提出自适应探测流程，由领导者对负载施加平移和旋转输入以获取所需测量。

Result: 理论上给出了精确恢复障碍物位置的充分条件，并设计了自适应探测程序；仿真实验验证了所提方法在障碍物定位与响应跟随者识别方面的性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在无机器人间通信、仅依赖聚合力旋量测量的情况下实现障碍物定位，为协同搬运中的分布式感知与避障提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We consider obstacle localization for a team of robots cooperatively transporting a rigid payload without explicit inter-robot communication. A leader robot directs the payload's motion, while follower robots assist and react to locally detected obstacles. The leader measures the followers' aggregate wrench, i.e., the combined force and torque they exert on the payload, but cannot directly distinguish their individual reactions. We design a follower control law that allows the leader to recover obstacle locations from these measurements. Each follower resists motion toward nearby obstacles, resulting in a piecewise-linear relationship between the payload's translational and angular velocity and the aggregate wrench. Changes between adjacent linear regions reveal an obstacle's bearing and distance and identify the responding follower. We give sufficient conditions for exact recovery at a fixed payload configuration and develop an adaptive probing procedure in which the leader applies translational and rotational inputs to the payload to obtain the required measurements. We demonstrate the performance of the proposed method in simulations.

</details>


### [60] [Humanoid Horizon: Extending Task Horizon in Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation via Parallel Training, Dynamic Starting, and Reward Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08320)
*Haozhuo Zhang,Qiang Zhang,Jian Tang,Mingzhe Ni,Michele Caprio,Angelo Cangelosi,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Humanoid Horizon统一策略框架，通过并行训练、动态起始与奖励门控，缓解长时程全身移动操作中的易奖励偏差与灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱室内环境中，人形机器人需在单个不间断回合内依次完成导航、抓取、运输与精确放置；现有方法存在易奖励偏差（过度优化早期运输阶段）和灾难性遗忘（关注后期阶段导致早期性能下降）两大问题。

Method: Humanoid Horizon包含三个机制：并行训练策略将N个场景组织为S个并发的阶段流并由共享策略控制，使各运输阶段持续获得梯度更新；动态起始机制用上游rollout的终止状态更新各环境初始状态，逐步扩大过渡覆盖并增强阶段边界鲁棒性；奖励门控在当前对象被移动超过阈值时，将后续阶段流中该回合剩余奖励置零，以学习不干扰刚放置对象并保持早期放置。

Result: 在LHM-Humanoid两对象基准（350个训练场景、66个留出场景）上，各阶段成功率超过80%。当顺序搬运对象超过两个时，成功率随任务时程增加而下降，但相比所有基线的急剧下降，其退化更为平缓。

Conclusion: 该工作通过统一策略框架有效缓解长时程人形机器人全身移动操作中的阶段偏置与遗忘问题，在短时程多对象任务上表现优异，并在更长时程下保持相对鲁棒的退化趋势。

Abstract: Cluttered indoor environments, where large and heavy objects are scattered across diverse surfaces, require humanoid robots to sequentially navigate, grasp, transport, and accurately place each item at its target location within a single uninterrupted episode. This long-horizon, whole-body loco-manipulation task remains a significant challenge for current methods. Previous approaches often suffer from two main issues: easy-reward bias, where training overemphasizes early transport stages at the expense of later ones, and catastrophic forgetting, where focusing on later stages leads to a decline in earlier-stage performance. In this work, we introduce Humanoid Horizon, a unified policy framework designed to overcome these limitations through three interrelated mechanisms. The Parallel Training Strategy organizes $N$ scenes into $S$ concurrent stage streams governed by a shared policy, ensuring all transport stages receive continuous gradient updates and removing the bottleneck of sequential optimization. The Dynamic Starting Mechanism updates each environment's initial state with terminal states from upstream rollouts, gradually broadening transition coverage and enhancing robustness at stage boundaries. Reward Gating sets the reward to zero for the rest of the episode in later-stage streams when the immediately preceding object is displaced beyond a set threshold, so the shared policy learns not to disturb a just-placed object and earlier placements are preserved throughout the episode. Collectively, these strategies achieve per-stage success rates exceeding 80\% on the two-object LHM-Humanoid benchmark (350 training scenes, 66 held-out scenes). As the number of sequentially transported objects grows beyond two, success declines with the horizon, but the degradation is graceful relative to the sharp drop seen in all baselines.

</details>


### [61] [Sensor-Layout-Agnostic Navigation via Geometric Observation Canonicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08306)
*Welf Rehberg,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种体现本体信息的导航策略，将任意深度传感器布局显式转换到机器人中心坐标系，形成统一球面距离图像和有效性掩码，实现跨相机配置的零样本泛化与真实飞行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航策略通常绑定固定相机配置，导致难以在具有异构传感器布局的不同机器人之间进行零样本部署。

Method: 不依赖隐式学习空间对齐，而是将来自任意深度传感器载荷的深度测量显式反投影到共享的机器人中心坐标系，其中传感器数量、外参和内参均可变化；随后拼接为统一球面距离图像和二元有效性掩码，使下游策略能区分已覆盖空间与未观测盲区；并通过强化学习结合激进的相机随机化进行训练。

Result: 策略可零样本泛化到最多七台相机的未见布局，成功率随总空间感知覆盖率增加从78%提升至95%；在物理四旋翼于障碍物走廊和户外森林中的真实飞行试验，验证了跨相机配置的零样本迁移能力以及对在线传感器突然丢失的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式本体信息化的深度传感器统一表示能够有效支持跨异构相机布局的导航策略泛化，并提升真实部署中的零样本迁移与传感器故障鲁棒性。

Abstract: Existing visual navigation policies are inherently bound to fixed camera configurations, creating a fundamental barrier to zero-shot deployment across heterogeneous robot sensor layouts. To overcome this limitation, we present an embodiment-informed navigation policy capable of generalizing across diverse depth sensor configurations on a specific aerial platform. Instead of implicitly learning spatial alignments, our approach explicitly unprojects depth measurements from arbitrary depth sensor payloads, varying in sensor count, mounting extrinsics, and intrinsics, into a shared robot-centric frame, stitching them into a unified spherical range image and a binary validity mask. This mask allows the downstream policy to explicitly distinguish covered space from unobserved blind spots. Trained via reinforcement learning with aggressive camera randomization, our policy generalizes zero-shot to unseen layouts featuring up to seven cameras, scaling success rates from 78% to 95% as total spatial sensing coverage increases. Finally, real-world flight trials on a physical quadrotor, conducted in an obstacle-filled corridor and an outdoor forest, validate the policy's zero-shot transfer across camera configurations and its resilience to sudden online sensor dropouts.

</details>


### [62] [Mitigating Concept Drift in QoS Prediction for Teleoperation of Autonomous Vehicles Using Historic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08297)
*Xiyan Su,Jianning Gao,Mahmoud Ashri,Frank Diermeyer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向遥操作，基于实测数据提出预测上行数据速率与往返时延的pQoS框架，并利用历史数据缓解概念漂移，提出关键场景检测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作是自动驾驶的兜底方案，但其可靠运行依赖移动网络资源，而网络资源无法始终得到保障，因此需要引入预测性服务质量（pQoS）来提升遥操作的韧性。

Method: 基于一次数据测量活动，构建预测框架以预测遥操作的两个关键网络KPI：上行数据速率和往返时延；将历史数据纳入预测流程，以缓解机器学习预测模型在未见数据上因概念漂移导致的性能下降；同时引入关键场景检测指标，专门评估遥操作场景下的预测性能。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数值；主要产出为遥操作网络KPI预测框架、抗概念漂移方法以及面向遥操作的关键场景检测评价指标。

Conclusion: 该工作为遥操作pQoS提供了可预测上行速率与往返时延的框架，并通过历史数据增强和关键场景检测来提升预测模型在动态网络环境下的鲁棒性与评价针对性。

Abstract: Teleoperation serves as the fallback solution to autonomous driving but reliable functions of the teleoperation require a certain amount of mobile network resources, which cannot be guaranteed at all times. Therefore, predictive quality of service (pQoS) is introduced as a concept to increase the resilience of the teleoperation. In this paper, based on a data measurement campaign, we propose a prediction framework to prediction two important network KPIs of teleoperation: uplink data-rate and round-trip latency. Furthermore, we introduce a method to alleviate the performance degradation of machine-learning-based prediction models on previously unseen data due to concept drift by incorporating historic data into the prediction pipeline. Additionally, we introduce the metric of critical scenario detection to evaluate the prediction performance specifically for teleoperation.

</details>


### [63] [HexaGripper: A Single-Actuator, Winch-Deployed Gripper for Autonomous Aerial Parcel Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08259)
*Nimantha Adikaram,Mahen Abeyratne,Lakmina Chandrajith,A. H. T. E. De Silva,Isira Naotunna,Asanka Perera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 HexaGripper：一种单驱动器、绞盘部署的六板夹爪，用于无人机自主采集长方体包裹，无需人工装载、降落或专用地面设施。系统融合视觉对准、测距、绞盘部署与状态控制，实现与拾取表面保持分离的自主抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空中包裹采集受限于包裹交接操作，通常需要人工装载、无人机降落或专用地面基础设施，限制了全自主性与效率。

Method: 设计单执行器驱动的六板夹爪，通过绞盘部署实现机械同步抓取，具有较大捕获区域和对位置偏差的容忍度；集成基于视觉的对准、距离感知、绞盘部署和状态机控制，使无人机在拾取过程中与拾取表面保持安全分离。

Result: 静态抓取实验在三种包裹几何形状下达到 100% 成功率（15/15）；捕获工作空间实验在 28 个测试位置全部成功，径向偏移可达 150 mm；端到端户外空中采集达到 80% 成功率（4/5）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，机械同步、绞盘部署的抓取方案用于免降落自主空中包裹采集具有可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous aerial parcel collection remains constrained by package-transfer operations that require manual loading, landing, or dedicated ground infrastructure. This letter presents HexaGripper, a single-actuator, winch-deployed six-plate gripper for autonomous collection of cuboid parcels. The proposed mechanism provides synchronized grasping with a wide capture region and tolerance to positional misalignment during pickup. The system integrates vision-based alignment, range sensing, winch deployment, and state-based control to enable autonomous collection while maintaining UAV separation from the pickup surface. Experimental evaluation covered static grasping, capture-workspace assessment, and end-to-end outdoor aerial collection. Static experiments achieved 100% pickup success (15/15 trials) across three parcel geometries, while workspace experiments achieved successful pickup at all 28 tested positions up to a 150 mm radial offset. End-to-end outdoor aerial collection achieved an 80% success rate (4/5 trials). These results demonstrate the feasibility of mechanically synchronized, winch-deployed grasping for landing-free autonomous aerial parcel collection.

</details>


### [64] [UWB Meets Crazyflow: Simulating Degraded Feedback at Scale for Aerial Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08225)
*Martin Schuck,Marcel P. Rath,Yufei Hua,Abhishek Goudar,SiQi Zhou,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Crazyflow 是一个基于 JAX 的精确可微仿真器，将物理与控制统一到单个可微计算图中，实现大规模并行与高速训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真器难以兼顾速度、建模精度与可扩展性，尤其难以在加速硬件上高效并行训练和评估空中机器人控制器。

Method: 基于 JAX 与 XLA 的 jit 编译，将物理和控制统一为单个可微计算图，从而在加速硬件上实现大规模并行且不牺牲建模精度；采用高度模块化设计，并集成完整高保真 UWB/IMU 仿真管线与全状态 EKF。

Result: 相比现有基线实现数量级加速，能在数秒内训练出可部署的强化学习智能体；支持在真实退化状态反馈下进行大规模并行控制器评估，且对 GPU 吞吐影响极小。

Conclusion: Crazyflow 兼具速度、精度与可扩展性，可作为下一代空中机器人研究的基础工具。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce Crazyflow, an accurate, differentiable simulator built on JAX. By leveraging jit compilation via XLA, Crazyflow unifies physics and control into a single differentiable computation graph, enabling massive parallelization on accelerated hardware without sacrificing modeling accuracy. This architecture achieves order-of-magnitude speedups over existing baselines, capable of training deployable reinforcement learning agents in seconds. To highlight its highly modular design, we demonstrate how easily Crazyflow can be extended by integrating a complete, high-fidelity Ultra-Wideband (UWB) and Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) simulation pipeline coupled with a full-state Extended Kalman Filter (EKF). This capability allows for massive parallel controller evaluation under realistic, degraded state feedback with minimal impact on GPU throughput. By combining speed, accuracy, and extensibility, Crazyflow serves as a foundational tool for the next generation of aerial robotics research.

</details>


### [65] [VOMMI: Collecting and Leveraging Portable Demonstrations for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08220)
*Yutian Zhang,Xingrui Xiong,Siyuan Ma,Yang Li,Jiawen Wen,Jiaqi Zhai,Liwen Yang,Ce Hao,Haozhen Chi,Yangkun Zhu,Yifan Zhu,Xiaowen Chu,Dong Wei,Qiaojun Yu,Dibo Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VOMMI 是一个便携式移动操作演示采集与学习框架，通过离线轨迹重建和在线视觉-运动条件化，将便携 RGB 演示连接到 VLA 后训练，从而在无需机器人遥操作或额外传感硬件的情况下提升运动监督与真实机器人任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 便携式移动操作演示有助于缓解具身智能的数据稀缺问题，但从 RGB 观测中获取可靠、低成本且无需机器人的运动监督仍很困难。现有方法通常依赖遥操作或配备额外传感器的专用设备，而直接使用估计的视觉里程计（VO）轨迹会因累积漂移和不完美运动监督导致不一致。

Method: 提出 VOMMI：同步身体视角与手部视角，以捕获导航上下文和局部物体交互，无需人-机器人运动学校准。R2-VO 利用稀疏几何锚点细化离线演示轨迹，并在多个预测时域上生成因果局部运动 token，用于在线策略条件化。动作组残差适配器仅将这些 token 注入基础分支。

Result: 每个任务使用 500 条便携式轨迹，其中 75 条留出用于 RGB-VO 评估，并以 200 条机器人演示作为参考。仅用便携式演示后训练的策略，其基座速度误差比用机器人采集演示训练的策略低 18.2%，同时末端执行器平移精度相当。离线重建使身体流和手部流的绝对轨迹误差相较各流最佳评估基线平均降低 24.6%。完整系统在三个真实机器人任务上，将平均成功率较 OpenPI 0.5 提升 8.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: VOMMI 能够从便携 RGB 演示中实现无需机器人的可靠运动监督与 VLA 后训练，在提升轨迹重建精度的同时改善真实机器人移动操作任务的成功率。

Abstract: Portable mobile-manipulation demonstrations can help alleviate data scarcity for embodied intelligence, but obtaining reliable, low-cost, and robot-free motion supervision from RGB observations remains challenging. Existing approaches often rely on teleoperation or specialized devices equipped with additional sensing hardware, while directly using estimated visual odometry (VO) trajectories can introduce inconsistencies due to accumulated drift and imperfect motion supervision. We present the Visual-Odometry-Conditioned Mobile Manipulation Interface (VOMMI), a portable demonstration collection and learning framework that connects portable RGB demonstrations to vision-language-action (VLA) post-training through offline trajectory reconstruction and online visual-motion conditioning. VOMMI synchronizes body and hand views to capture navigation context and local object interactions without requiring human-robot kinematic correspondence calibration. R2-VO refines offline demonstration trajectories using sparse geometric anchors and produces causal local-motion tokens over multiple prediction horizons for online policy conditioning. An action-group residual adapter incorporates these tokens only into the base branch. Experiments use a 500-trajectory portable for each task, with 75 trajectories held out for RGB-VO evaluation, and 200 robot demonstrations as references. Our policy, post-trained only on portable demonstrations, achieves 18.2% lower base-velocity error than a policy trained with robot-collected demonstrations, while maintaining comparable end-effector translation accuracy. Offline reconstruction reduces absolute trajectory errors for the body and hand streams by 24.6% on average relative to the best evaluated baseline for each stream. The complete system improves the mean success rate by 8.3 percentage points over OpenPI 0.5 across three real-robot tasks.

</details>


### [66] [Compact Robot Policies Need Fine-Grained Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08183)
*Nanhe Chen,Runqiu Yang,Jiawei Tang,Sichao Liu,Yuquan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 CoRP：一个仅 48.9M 参数、不含 VLM 和视频生成先验的紧凑多任务操作策略，在 LIBERO 达 97.0%、RoboTwin 2.0 Clean/Randomized 达 75.78%/73.36%，可匹配大 40.9-163.6 倍的系统；作者主张多任务操作策略的性能主要来自视觉表征，而非参数规模或生成先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多任务操作策略在架构、规模和预训练先验上同时不同，导致已发表比较无法将性能归因于单一组件。作者认为大部分性能来自视觉表征，参数规模和生成式先验在很大程度上只是附带因素。

Method: 构建 CoRP，将策略分解为视觉表征提取器和 flow-matching 动作生成器，且刻意保持紧凑。在固定动作生成器的条件下，逐项改变表征提取器属性，包括预训练初始化、是否冻结编码器、token 压缩方式、变分信息瓶颈与硬 token 预算，以及语言条件消融。

Result: CoRP 在 LIBERO 上达 97.0%，在 RoboTwin 2.0 Clean/Randomized 上达 75.78%/73.36%。随机 ViT-S/14 在 LIBERO 降至 78.1%，ImageNet ResNet-34 降至 74.5%；冻结编码器损失 19.8 分。每视图重采样 48 tokens 优于全部 patch tokens，为 97.0% vs 83.2%；变分信息瓶颈将 LIBERO-Goal 从 95.8% 降至 33.0%。语言条件仅在目标模糊时关键，LIBERO-Goal 从 9.2% 提升至 95.8%；在观测无歧义的 RoboTwin 2.0 上移除语言条件略有提升。

Conclusion: 紧凑策略有效的关键条件是表征需同时具备预训练、任务适配和压缩。多任务操作策略的性能主要来自视觉表征，而非参数规模或生成式先验。

Abstract: Multi-task manipulation policies differ in architecture, scale, and pretrained priors all at once, so published comparisons cannot attribute performance to any single component. We argue that most of it comes from the visual representation, and that parameter scale and generative priors are largely incidental. To test this, we build CoRP (Compressed Representation Policy), a deliberately compact policy (48.9M parameters, no vision-language model and no video-generative prior) that factorizes into a representation extractor and a flow-matching action generator. It reaches 97.0% on LIBERO and 75.78%/73.36% on RoboTwin 2.0 Clean/Randomized, matching systems 40.9-163.6x larger. Holding the action generator fixed, we then vary one extractor property at a time. Pretrained initialization is decisive: a random ViT-S/14 drops to 78.1% and an ImageNet ResNet-34 to 74.5% on LIBERO. Pretraining alone is not enough, as freezing the encoder costs 19.8 points. Compression matters as much: resampling each view to 48 tokens beats passing all patch tokens (97.0% vs 83.2%), and a variational information bottleneck over those tokens is worse than a hard token budget, cutting LIBERO-Goal from 95.8% to 33.0% by suppressing the instruction-dependent token selection the policy relies on. Language conditioning contributes only where the observation leaves the goal ambiguous (LIBERO-Goal: 9.2% to 95.8%), while on RoboTwin 2.0, where observations are unambiguous, removing it slightly improves success. Therefore, we argue that a compact policy works when its representation is pretrained, task-adapted, and compressed. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [67] [Nested Power Models for Multirotor Propulsion: From Aerodynamic Drag to Electrical Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08177)
*Antonio Franchi,Aaron Saini,Ahmed Ali,Chiara Gabellieri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作构建并实验验证了一类嵌套式多旋翼推进功率动态模型，表明仅靠转速/气动功率不足以预测动态功率，需引入可逆动能变化率、转矩平方相关机电损耗及速度比例损耗项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖转速的气动功率模型无法表征加速度相关效应，因此需要建立能准确预测动态推进功率的模型，并能区分可逆动能交换与不可逆损耗。

Method: 从气动功率耗散出发，逐步引入可逆动能变化率、转矩相关机电耗散和集总速度比例耗散，形成嵌套模型序列；在电机-驱动-螺旋桨单元上用部分实验辨识、其余实验验证预测，并借助独立估计的转动惯量和气动阻力检验功率归因是否正确。

Result: 可逆动能变化率项是必要的，但不足以实现准确的动态功率预测；与电机转矩平方成正比的耗散是主要额外改进项；速度比例耗散可进一步避免将不可逆损耗误归因于可逆动能交换。

Conclusion: 该建模与验证方法为多旋翼系统动态推进功率模型的开发与选择提供了可复用且可实验验证的基础。

Abstract: Speed-only aerodynamic power models for multirotor propulsion cannot represent acceleration-dependent effects. This work develops a nested sequence of propulsion-power models that starts from aerodynamic power dissipation and progressively introduces a reversible kinetic-energy rate, torque-dependent electromechanical dissipation, and lumped speed-proportional dissipation. The models are identified using one subset of experiments and validated using the other on a motor-drive-propeller unit. Independent estimates of rotational inertia and aerodynamic drag complement predictive validation by assessing whether the models correctly attribute the measured power to reversible kinetic-energy exchange and irreversible dissipation and, within the latter, to aerodynamic and electromechanical losses. The results show that the reversible kinetic-energy rate is necessary but insufficient for accurate dynamic power prediction. Dissipation proportional to the squared motor torque provides the main additional improvement, while speed-proportional dissipation further prevents irreversible losses from being attributed to reversible kinetic-energy exchange. The resulting methodology provides a reusable and experimentally verifiable basis for developing and selecting dynamic propulsion-power models for multirotor systems.

</details>


### [68] [ViDAL: A Visual Dynamics-Grounded Action Latent Space for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08150)
*Yuan Xu,Yixiang Chen,Qisen Ma,Jiabing Yang,Peiyan Li,Kai Wang,Jianhua Yang,Jianlou Si,Jun Huang,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViDAL 提出一种以未来视觉动态为锚点的连续动作潜空间，通过 Action VAE 重构动作块并将潜变量与未来场景动态对齐，作为可插拔动作接口提升多种 VLA 策略，并在仿真与真实机器人任务上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 动作表示（原始动作块、离散动作 token、连续动作潜变量）主要建模动作轨迹本身，较少考虑动作所引发的视觉动态。作者希望将动作潜空间与未来视觉动态进行 grounding，以增强动作表示对场景演变的建模能力。

Method: 提出 ViDAL（Visual Dynamics-grounded Action Latent Space）。具体训练一个 Action VAE 来重构动作块，同时将其连续动作潜变量与未来场景动态对齐。该 Action VAE 可作为 plug-in 动作接口兼容多种 VLA 架构，并可选地支持未来视频预测。下游机器人策略集成该动作接口进行学习与决策。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上达到 98.1% 平均成功率；在 RoboTwin 2.0 的 50 个双臂任务上，将多任务 π0.5 策略成功率从 54.3% 提升至 65.5%（Clean），从 33.2% 提升至 43.1%（Random）；在真实单臂 Franka 和双臂 ARX 平台上分别取得 20.0% 和 23.4% 的绝对成功率提升。

Conclusion: ViDAL 表明，将连续动作潜空间锚定于未来视觉动态是一种有效的动作表示学习方式。该方法作为通用可插拔动作接口，能显著提升多种 VLA 策略在仿真与真实机器人任务中的表现，并额外具备未来视频预测能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a central paradigm for robot policy learning, which predict actions in three forms: raw action chunks, discrete action tokens, or continuous action latents. However, existing action representations primarily model action trajectories, with limited consideration of the visual dynamics induced by these actions. We introduce ViDAL, a Visual Dynamics-grounded Action Latent Space that anchors continuous action latents in the future visual dynamics of the scene. Specifically, ViDAL learns action latent space by training an Action Variational Autoencoder (Action VAE) to reconstruct action chunks while aligning its latent with future scene dynamics. When integrated into downstream robot policies, the proposed Action VAE serves as a plug-in action interface compatible with multiple VLA architectures and enables optional future-video prediction as an additional capability. Empirically, ViDAL outperforms competitive baselines on LIBERO with 98.1% average success, improves a multi-task $\pi_{0.5}$ policy on RoboTwin 2.0 from 54.3% to 65.5% (Clean) and from 33.2% to 43.1% (Random) success rates over 50 dual-arm tasks, and yields 20.0% and 23.4% absolute success-rate gains on real-world single-arm Franka and dual-arm ARX robot platforms.

</details>


### [69] [VLA-ACL: Action-Consistent Visual Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08133)
*Owen Du,Yang Yue,Jie Zhang,Jiaqi Pi,Chi Bene Chen,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA模型操控性能强，但每个控制步处理长token序列计算开销大，限制实时部署。VLA-ACL提出在冻结基础VLA模型的前提下，通过动作级监督学习轻量视觉token剪枝策略，实现高剪枝率、低计算量与推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉patch在VLA输入序列中占主导且冗余高，视觉token剪枝是直接解决方案。但现有方法要么依赖注意力分数、运动阈值等间接的无训练启发式，要么需要对基础VLA模型进行昂贵的微调，难以兼顾性能与效率。

Method: 提出VLA-ACL（Action Consistency Learning）。该方法保持基础VLA模型完全冻结，学习一个轻量级视觉token剪枝策略。训练目标使基于剪枝后视觉上下文产生的动作与全上下文教师模型保持一致，并以真值动作作为辅助监督，从而将token选择直接与其对下游控制输出的影响关联起来。

Result: 在LIBERO和真实世界操控任务上，VLA-ACL最多剪枝87.5%的视觉token，仍保持有竞争力的性能；计算量最多降低75%，推理加速1.5倍。相比现有冻结VLA的剪枝方法，取得了更强的性能-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 结果表明，动作级监督对视觉token选择具有重要价值，VLA-ACL能在冻结基础VLA模型的情况下实现更优的性能-效率权衡，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong robotic manipulation performance but incur high computational costs from processing long token sequences at every control step, limiting real-time deployment. Visual token pruning offers a direct solution, as visual patches dominate the input sequence and contain considerable redundancy. Existing approaches, however, either rely on indirect training-free heuristics, such as attention scores and motion thresholds, or require costly fine-tuning of the base VLA model. We introduce VLA-ACL (Action Consistency Learning), which learns a lightweight visual token pruning policy through action-level supervision while keeping the base VLA model entirely frozen. The training objective encourages actions produced from pruned visual contexts to remain consistent with the full-context teacher, with ground-truth actions as auxiliary supervision. This directly ties token selection to its effect on the downstream control output. Experiments on LIBERO and real-world manipulation tasks show that VLA-ACL prunes up to 87.5% of visual tokens while retaining competitive performance, reduces computation by up to 75%, and achieves a 1.5x inference speedup. These results establish a stronger performance-efficiency trade-off than existing frozen-VLA pruning methods and demonstrate the value of action-level supervision for visual token selection. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [70] [Beyond Waypoint Regression: Query-Based Cost Learning over Reachable Ego Futures for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08123)
*Ahmed Abouelazm,Rupert Polley,Qingyuan Zhang,Yin Wu,Philip Schörner,Carl Esselborn,J. Marius Zöllner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于查询的成本学习规划框架，为动态可达的自车轨迹查询估计有界成本，并借助场景token、风险感知成本聚合与MPPI混合生成可行规划，在提升碰撞安全性的同时保持轨迹多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于waypoint回归的端到端规划器开环精度高，但主要模仿专家几何轨迹，难以适应部署时的安全约束；密集BEV成本或少量回归轨迹表示也存在局限。

Method: 采用查询式成本学习，对动态可达的自车轨迹查询估计有界成本；用紧凑联合场景token捕捉一致的多模态智能体未来；通过contingency-aware cost aggregation与cost-guided intra-cluster MPPI mixing，将学习到的成本拓扑转化为可行自车规划。

Result: 在nuScenes上优于ST-P3、NMP等成本估计规划器，碰撞率优于多数回归基线，L2保持竞争力，并保留可解释成本接口；在真实驾驶日志上无需微调即可较SparseDrive和Alpamayo降低碰撞率，同时维持多样化候选轨迹。

Conclusion: 该框架将可学习成本拓扑转化为安全可行的规划输出，兼顾安全性、开环性能、轨迹多样性与可解释性，展现出面向部署安全约束的适应潜力。

Abstract: End-to-end planners based on waypoint regression achieve strong open-loop accuracy, but they primarily learn to mimic expert geometry and remain difficult to adapt to deployment-time safety constraints. We propose a query-based cost-learning framework that estimates bounded costs for dynamically reachable ego trajectory queries, rather than dense BEV cells or a small regressed trajectory set. Compact joint scene tokens capture coherent multimodal agent futures, while contingency-aware cost aggregation and cost-guided intra-cluster MPPI mixing convert the learned cost topology into feasible ego plans. On nuScenes, our method improves over prior cost-estimation planners such as ST-P3 and NMP, outperforms most regression baselines in collision rate, while remaining competitive in L2, and retaining an interpretable cost interface. On real-world driving logs, the proposed planner reduces collision rates compared with SparseDrive and Alpamayo without fine-tuning, while maintaining a diverse set of candidate trajectories.

</details>


### [71] [iGPC: Generative Motion Priors for Object-Aware Humanoid Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08120)
*Anujith Muraleedharan,Abdul Ahad Butt,Nolan Fey,Yash Prabhu,Anamika J H,Sandor Felber,Maurice Rahme,Ivan Laptev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个将生成式预训练控制器（GPC）从通用人体运动扩展到全身人形-环境交互的框架，用于学习可部署的感知驱动交互策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在非结构化环境中需兼具鲁棒的全身控制与对物体的感知和物理交互能力；大规模人体运动数据虽能提供自然、多样的控制先验，但将其有效迁移到感知驱动的物体交互仍很困难。

Method: 首先，将GPC适配为交互专家，以场景可供性线索和特权状态信息为条件，利用预训练人体运动先验学习特定接触行为，如伸手够物、抓握环境支撑以稳定身体、推动可移动物体。其次，引入感知驱动的学生策略，保留预训练GPC策略，并通过机载传感器观测从专家中蒸馏交互技能。为弥合特权专家观测与感知输入之间的差距，提出两个互补训练目标，使预训练运动先验在蒸馏中有效适应。

Result: 在多个全身交互任务上的实验表明，大规模生成式人体运动先验可为学习人形机器人在接触丰富的真实世界环境中的可部署交互策略提供有效基础。

Conclusion: 扩展GPC至全身人形-环境交互是可行的，且大规模生成式人体运动先验能够作为感知驱动、接触丰富交互策略学习的有效基础。

Abstract: Humanoid robots operating in unstructured environments must combine robust whole-body control with the ability to perceive and physically interact with surrounding objects. While large-scale human motion data provides powerful priors for natural and versatile humanoid control, effectively transferring such priors to perception-driven object interaction remains challenging. To address this bottleneck, we propose a framework that extends the recently proposed Generative Pretrained Controller (GPC) from general human motion to full-body humanoid-environment interaction. First, we adapt GPC into interaction experts conditioned on scene affordance cues and privileged state information. These experts leverage the pretrained human motion prior while learning task-specific contact behaviors, including reaching toward objects, grasping environmental supports for stabilization, and pushing movable objects. Second, we introduce a perception-driven student that retains the pretrained GPC policy and distills interaction skills from the experts using onboard sensory observations. To bridge the gap between privileged expert observations and sensory inputs, we propose two complementary training objectives that enable effective adaptation of the pretrained motion prior during distillation. Notably, our experiments across multiple whole-body interaction tasks demonstrate that large-scale generative human motion priors provide an effective foundation for learning deployable policies for humanoid interactions in contact-rich real-world environments.

</details>


### [72] [AutodidactWAM: Cross-Modal Self-Distillation from Generated Video to Robot Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08119)
*Sergei Kurchev,Iaroslav Kolomiets,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Artem Lykov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究世界-动作模型（WAMs）在新机器人上微调时出现的“视频-动作不对称”：生成视频看似合理，但协同生成的动作系统性错误。作者提出 AutodidactWAM，通过无需遥操作的手部姿态估计与逆运动学从生成视频中恢复动作，并以此作为偏好目标，仅微调动作相关层，实现 embodiment 适配与自蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cosmos 3 等 WAMs 可联合生成未来视频与机器人动作，但用轻量 LoRA 适配到未见过的 Unitree G1 人形机器人（配五指 BrainCo 手）时，视频能渲染出合理执行过程，动作却系统性偏离目标。现有动作输出在预抓取、抓取、抓放三阶段成功率极低（约17%、10%、7%），在留出物体上更差，因此需要无需额外任务遥操作的动作校正与适配方法。

Method: 提出 AutodidactWAM：先用无遥操作方式训练手部姿态估计器，再通过逆运动学在模型生成的视频上恢复动作估计；将这些恢复动作与原生预测配对，作为偏好目标，仅微调动作相关层，同时教师强制生成视频。比较监督重标注与基于 rectified-flow 的 Diffusion-DPO 适配；进一步提出组合偏好监督、监督目标拟合和笛卡尔轨迹锚定的混合目标 DPO+SFT+DTW。一次 embodiment 适配后，自蒸馏无需额外任务特定遥操作。

Result: 恢复动作门控在预抓取、抓取、抓放分别约达 75%、47%、42%，显著高于原生动作的 17%、10%、7%。混合目标 DPO+SFT+DTW 最佳：在训练物体 Oreo 上达到 90% 预抓取与 20% 全任务成功；在留出物体上达到 80% 与 30%。纯 Flow-DPO 尽管验证偏好准确率为 1.000，但成功率为 0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，WAM 在新 embodiment 上的视频-动作不对称可通过从生成视频中恢复动作并自蒸馏来缓解；性能提升并非仅来自对比目标，而依赖偏好监督、监督拟合与轨迹锚定等多目标的组合。该方法减少了对任务特定遥操作数据的依赖，但仍需一次 embodiment 适配。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) such as Cosmos 3 jointly generate future video and robot actions from an observation and instruction. Adapting one such model with a lightweight LoRA fine-tune to a previously unseen robot, a Unitree G1 humanoid with five-fingered BrainCo hands, exposes a video-action asymmetry: the video renders plausible task executions, while the co-generated action is systematically mis-targeted. We evaluate closed-loop real-robot trials at three cumulative stages: pre-grasp, grasp, and pick-and-place. The native action succeeds only approximately 17%, 10%, and 7% of the time, respectively, and performs worse on held-out objects. We propose AutodidactWAM, a hand-pose estimator trained without teleoperation, followed by inverse kinematics, that runs on the model's generated video to recover action estimates. Paired with the native prediction, these recovered actions provide preferred targets for fine-tuning only the action-related layers, while the generated video is teacher-forced. We compare supervised relabeling with a rectified-flow adaptation of Diffusion-DPO. After one-time embodiment adaptation, self-distillation requires no additional task-specific teleoperation. The recovered-action gate reaches approximately 75%, 47%, and 42% pre-grasp, grasp, and pick-and-place success, compared with 17%, 10%, and 7% for the native action. A hybrid objective combining preference supervision, supervised target fitting, and Cartesian trajectory anchoring (DPO+SFT+DTW) performs best: on Oreo, the training object, it reaches 90% pre-grasp and 20% full-task success; on a held-out object, it reaches 80% and 30%. Plain Flow-DPO reaches 0% success despite 1.000 validation preference accuracy, indicating that the combination of training objectives, rather than the contrastive objective alone, drives the observed gains.

</details>


### [73] [Energy-Aware Path Following: Comparative Analysis of Reinforcement Learning and NMPC for Electric Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08112)
*Mohamed Sabaa,Mostafa Emam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在统一Frenet坐标系运动学车辆模型与含显式再生制动的VT-CPEM能量模型下，对比了NMPC、PPO、PID-SF和Stanley四种路径跟踪控制器，并验证PPO策略对未知道路与更复杂车辆模型的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径跟踪控制通常面临双目标优化困境：既要最小化与参考路径的偏差，又要保持平滑速度曲线。对电动汽车而言，平滑速度曲线尤为重要，因为通过再生制动回收能量可延长有限续航里程，而该问题在文献中尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 在统一的Frenet坐标系运动学车辆模型下，采用经验证的VT-CPEM能量模型并显式建模再生制动，对四种控制器进行对比分析：非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）、近端策略优化（PPO）、增益调度Ackermann状态反馈基线（PID-SF）和Stanley几何基线。为满足实时性，NMPC使用JIT编译的CasADi实现；PPO在传统直线和S形弯道赛道上训练。

Result: 训练后的PPO策略未经修改即成功迁移到未见赛道，包括ISO 3888-1换道、chicane、随机生成的参数化样条以及±3°坡道。此外，该策略零样本迁移到带线性轮胎的动态单轨车辆模型时具有可接受的初始性能，经简短微调后得到优化，表明PPO可 readily 迁移到更复杂的车辆模型。

Conclusion: 本文展示了所开发控制器的性能分析，并讨论了未来工作方向；重点在于PPO策略对未知道路和更复杂车辆模型具有良好的迁移能力。

Abstract: Path-following control strategies typically follow the bi-objective optimization dilemma: minimizing deviations from a reference path while maintaining smooth speed profiles. The latter objective is especially relevant for Electric Vehicles (EVs), since their limited driving range can be extended by recovering energy through regenerative braking, a feature that has not yet been sufficiently studied in the literature. In this work, we perform a comparative analysis of four controllers under one common Frenet frame-based kinematic vehicle model, utilizing a validated energy model (VT-CPEM) with explicit regenerative braking. Herein, we implement the following controllers: Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC), Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), gain-scheduled Ackermann state-feedback baseline (PID-SF), and a Stanley geometric baseline. To satisfy real-time requirements, we implement the NMPC using JIT-compiled CasADi. Moreover, we train the PPO using traditional straight and S-curve tracks, after which we successfully transfer the unmodified policy to unseen tracks, including: an ISO 3888-1 lane-change, a chicane, randomly-generated parameterized-splines, and a $\pm3^\circ$ graded road. In addition, the policy transfers to a dynamic single-track vehicle model with linear tires, zero-shot with an acceptable initial performance, which was optimized after brief fine-tuning. Thereby, we demonstrate that our PPO is readily transferable to more comprehensive vehicle models. We conclude with a performance analysis of developed controllers and discuss ideas for future work.

</details>


### [74] [Reactive Exploration of Unknown Environments for Redundant Robots using Virtual Model Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08110)
*Alessio Canzolino,Omar Faris,Alessandro De Blasi,Fulvio Forni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向冗余机械臂与眼在手相机的主动探索方法，结合体素评分和虚拟模型控制，在受限与遮挡空间中实时、安全地完成建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限、遮挡及部分已知空间中的机器人操作极具挑战，机械臂需在紧密几何约束下精确控制整体位姿，同时避开探索中新发现的障碍。

Method: 针对冗余机械臂和眼在手相机，提出主动探索策略：利用机器人当前状态与期望信息增益构造新评分，从待探索空间直接选择目标体素；采用虚拟模型控制驱动机器人安全移向目标体素，保证柔顺性并实现全身反应式避障，无需路径重规划。

Result: 在受限和开放环境中的仿真与真实机器人实验表明，所有测试环境的建图覆盖率均超过90%，耗时少于120秒，且未发生碰撞。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效、安全、高效地探索受限、遮挡和部分未知空间，验证了主动探索与虚拟模型控制相结合用于冗余机械臂操作的可行性。

Abstract: The exploration of confined, occluded, and partially known spaces poses significant challenges in robotic manipulation. The overall pose of the robotic arm must be carefully controlled to respect tight geometric constraints while avoiding newly discovered obstacles. We address this problem by proposing an active exploration approach for redundant robotic arms with an eye-in-hand camera configuration. Our approach navigates and acquires information in real-time based on a novel scoring method that directly selects a target voxel from the unexplored space using the robot's current state and expected information gain. To move the robot safely toward the target voxel, we utilize Virtual Model Control, which guarantees compliance and enables whole-body reactive obstacle avoidance without the need for path replanning. Simulated and real-robot experiments in both confined and open environments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, achieving over $90\%$ mapping coverage across all tested environments in under $120$ seconds without colliding with obstacles.

</details>


### [75] [Navigation with RF Cues: Embodied Perception Action under Multipath Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08105)
*Wenlihan Lu,Tianshun Li,Liuqing Yang,Shijian Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作构建了Habitat Sionna RT多模态导航基准，并提出不确定性感知的多模态导航框架，利用RF、视觉与位姿历史联合估计目标方向及不确定性，以提升无先验地图下智能工厂机器人巡检导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能工厂巡检中，机器人需在无先验地图、无目标坐标，且存在遮挡或光照不足时到达设备。RF信号可提供方向线索，但多径传播会扭曲瞬时测量，使目标真实方向难以推断。

Method: 基于详细场景几何与材质属性构建Habitat Sionna RT基准，生成与机器人动作对齐的视觉和RF观测；提出不确定性感知多模态导航框架，从RF、视觉和位姿观测历史中联合估计目标方向及其不确定性，并结合视觉上下文指导动作选择。

Result: 在未见场景中，相比最强基线，成功率（SR）相对提升18.2%，路径长度加权成功率（SPL）相对提升11.5%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将RF方向线索与不确定性建模融入多模态导航，可有效应对多径干扰和感知受限，提升未知环境下目标搜索与巡检导航的鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: Smart factory inspection requires robots to reach connected equipment without a prior map or known target coordinates. Radio frequency (RF) signals from the target can provide directional cues to complement visual observations when occlusion or poor lighting limits target detection. However, multipath propagation can distort these cues, making it difficult to infer the target's true direction from instantaneous RF measurements. To enable navigation research under these conditions, we first construct a Habitat Sionna RT benchmark that uses detailed scene geometry and assigned material properties to generate aligned visual and RF observations in response to robot actions. Building on this benchmark, we propose an uncertainty aware multimodal navigation framework that jointly estimates target direction and its uncertainty from a history of RF, visual, and pose observations. These estimates inform action selection alongside visual context. Experiments in unseen scenes show relative improvements of 18.2% in success rate (SR) and 11.5% in success weighted by path length (SPL) over the strongest evaluated baseline.

</details>


### [76] [Vector Map Quality Metrics for Contextual Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08043)
*Marie-Ngoïe Badibanga Kalenda,Philippe Bonnifait,Marie-Anne Mittet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并验证了面向自动驾驶矢量地图质量评估的新指标GOSPAM，可统一度量位置误差、存在性与完整性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶安全依赖持续的地图维护，需要可靠的质量指标来判断何时、何地触发地图更新（如众包数据）以及是否部署新编译的地图。

Method: 设计GOSPAM指标以度量矢量地图在位置误差、存在性和完整性方面的差异；在点要素和折线要素地图上进行详细仿真，分析其对偏置、假正例、假负例和坐标误差等常见地图退化的敏感性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，GOSPAM能够有效捕捉多种形式的地图偏差，提供统一且可解释的度量。

Conclusion: GOSPAM是汽车应用中地图质量评估的有力候选指标，可支持地图更新触发与新地图编译部署的决策。

Abstract: Ensuring safety in autonomous driving requires continuous map maintenance supported by reliable quality indicators. In this context, it is crucial to identify when and where map updates should be triggered, for instance through crowdsourced data, and under which conditions a new map compilation should be deployed. This paper focuses on effective metrics for assessing the quality of vector maps and guiding such decisions. We present a new metric called GOSPAM designed to measure map discrepancies in terms of location errors, existence, and completeness. Through detailed simulations on both point and polyline feature maps, we analyze its sensitivity to common map degradation such as bias, false positives, false negatives, and coordinate errors. The results demonstrate that GOSPAM offers a unified and interpretable measure that effectively captures various forms of map deviation, making it a strong candidate for map quality assessment in automotive applications.

</details>


### [77] [Reactive Task-Oriented Robot-Human Handovers via Generative Hypothesis Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08003)
*Carmen Scheidemann,Andreea Tulbure,Pascal Burkhardt,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GENESIS-Handover，利用VLM图像生成任务特定的手-物交互假设，并实时匹配观测人手姿态，从而推断合适的机器人-人递物配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类递物时会融合几何与语义信息，例如将刀柄朝向接收者以便利且安全。现有任务导向机器人-人递物方法虽从几何建模发展到物体可供性，但常忽略任务特定的显式手部姿态；许多物体存在多种交互模式，因此需建模这种可变性以实现鲁棒的任务导向递物。

Method: 提出GENESIS-Handover（GENErative HypotheSIS）：利用VLM图像生成产生多种任务特定的手-物交互假设；将这些假设实时与观测到的人手姿态匹配，以推断最合适的递物配置；以VLM作为合理手-物交互先验，生成面向任务条件的递物策略，并泛化到未见过的物体-任务对。先评估独立交互提议模块，再将完整系统部署到移动操作机器人上。

Result: 在12名参与者、5个任务-物体对的用户研究中，83.3%的参与者认为该方法比先前最先进方法具有更好的任务理解。

Conclusion: 基于VLM生成式手-物交互假设的方法能有效提升任务导向机器人-人递物的任务理解与泛化能力，并可在移动操作平台上实际部署验证。

Abstract: When humans hand each other objects, they incorporate both geometric and semantic information into this process. For example, passing a knife with the handle towards the recipient, rather than the blade, is both more ergonomic and safer. Recent state-of-the-art methods for task-oriented robot-human handovers have progressed from modeling object geometry to incorporating object affordances. However, they often forgo predicting the explicit, task-specific hand poses a human selects to utilize an object. Since many objects support multiple interaction modalities, e.g., a claw hammer used to strike or pull nails, this variability must be modeled to achieve robust task-oriented handovers. To tackle this, we propose a novel approach, GENESIS-Handover (GENErative HypotheSIS), which leverages VLM image generation to produce a variety of task-specific hand-object interaction hypotheses. These hypotheses are matched in real time to the observed human hand pose, enabling inference of the most suitable handover configuration. By leveraging VLMs as priors of plausible hand-object interactions, the method produces task-conditioned handover strategies for previously unseen object-task pairs. We evaluate the standalone interaction proposal module before deploying the full system on a mobile manipulator. In a user study with 12 participants across five task-object pairs, 83.3% perceived our method to have better task understanding than the previous state of the art.

</details>


### [78] [IronMan: Information-Constrained Video-Action Learning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07961)
*Yuanshuo Zhang,Wenzhe Zhao,Zixing Lei,Bin Chen,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 IronMan，一种基于信息瓶颈原理的视频-动作学习框架，通过抑制无关视觉信息、保留动作相关动态线索，提升机器人操作的分布内性能与分布外鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频动作模型（VAMs）将视觉动态建模与动作生成耦合，但视频表示并不天然适合动作生成；动作策略暴露于过多视觉细节会损害其泛化能力，因此需要约束信息，滤除无关视觉信息并保留动作相关动态线索。

Method: IronMan 基于信息瓶颈原理，采用信息约束的视频-动作学习框架，并引入动态感知瓶颈（dynamics-aware bottleneck），将含噪、纠缠的单步视频特征蒸馏为紧凑的世界表示。

Result: 大量仿真与真实世界实验表明，IronMan 具有强分布内性能和分布外鲁棒性，同时保持高效推理。在 LIBERO 上成功率达 99.0%，在 RoboTwin clean2clean 上达 79.4%，优于所有评估基线；在分布外偏移下，LIBERO-Plus 成功率达 79.1%，超过最强基线 10.4 个百分点。

Conclusion: IronMan 通过信息约束有效提升视频-动作学习的泛化与鲁棒性，在多种机器人操作任务和分布外场景中取得领先性能。

Abstract: Video Action Models (VAMs) couple visual dynamics modeling with action generation for robot manipulation. However, video representations are not naturally suited to action generation, as exposing the action policy to excessive visual detail can impair its generalization ability. Therefore, we introduce IronMan (Information-constRained videO-actioN learning for robot MANipulation), a robust video-action learning framework built on the information bottleneck principle. The core principle of this framework is to impose information constraints that suppress irrelevant visual information while preserving action-relevant dynamics cues. IronMan employs a dynamics-aware bottleneck that distills noisy, entangled one-step video features into compact world representations. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate strong in-distribution (ID) performance and out-of-distribution (OOD) robustness while maintaining efficient inference. IronMan achieves success rates of 99.0% on LIBERO and 79.4% on RoboTwin clean2clean, outperforming all the evaluated baselines. Under OOD shifts, IronMan achieves a success rate of 79.1% on LIBERO-Plus, exceeding the strongest baseline by 10.4 percentage points. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [79] [SMART: Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Articulated Object Manipulation via Large-Scale Synthetic Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07652)
*Jicong Ao,Shuhan Jiang,Yuling Zhong,Yanwen Liu,Yuhan Gao,Jiangyuan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Shiqiang Zhu,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SMART系统，通过大规模合成演示数据（SMART-Data）提升VLA模型在关节物体操作中的性能，并实现零样本仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能系统需要与关节物体交互，但真实世界演示收集困难，因为涉及精确的接触和约束跟随运动。仿真虽可替代，但现有合成数据覆盖类别有限，且通用合成流程缺乏部件级语义和关节约束设计，阻碍智能体任务生成和高质量演示的可扩展合成。

Method: 提出SMART系统，核心是关节感知的仿真平台SMART-Sim，支持任务生成和高效演示收集；结合智能体任务生成和分布式合成系统，构建SMART-Data，包含超过100万条演示，涵盖44种原子任务类型、5种机器人设置和2507个关节物体。

Result: 在SMART-Data上预训练的VLA模型在仿真基准上表现有竞争力，实现零样本仿真到现实迁移，并在真实关节物体操作任务中展现可扩展性能。

Conclusion: 合成演示可为接触丰富的关节物体操作提供有效且可扩展的监督，提升VLA模型性能。

Abstract: The ability to interact with articulated objects is essential for embodied intelligent systems, but collecting large-scale real-world demonstrations for these interactions remains challenging due to the precise contact and constraint-following motions involved. Although simulation provides a promising alternative, existing synthetic data efforts cover limited articulated-object categories, while general-purpose synthesis pipelines lack explicit designs for part-level semantics and articulation constraints, hindering agentic task generation and scalable synthesis of high-quality articulated-manipulation demonstrations. To bridge this gap, we introduce SMART, a scalable system leveraging large-scale Synthesized Manipulation demonstrations for ARTiculated-object manipulation. At its core, we develop SMART-Sim, a simulation platform with articulation-aware design that enables effective task generation and efficient demonstration collection. Building on SMART-Sim, we apply agentic task generation and design a scalable distributed synthesis system, using them to synthesize SMART-Data, comprising over 1M demonstrations across 44 atomic task types, 5 robot setups, and 2,507 articulated objects. The vision-language-action (VLA) model pretrained on SMART-Data shows competitive performance on simulation benchmarks and achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer and scalable performance in real-world articulated-object manipulation tasks. This highlights the potential of synthetic demonstrations in providing effective and scalable supervision for improving VLA model performance in contact-rich articulated-object manipulation.

</details>


### [80] [Silicon Language: A Robot-Native Knowledge Exchange Framework for Heterogeneous Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07650)
*Yi Liu,Xianglin Meng,Chang Chen,Jingjing Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出机器人原生的知识交换框架 Silicon Language，使机器人能编码、发布、检索、翻译并本地验证知识包，并在玉林模拟矿井等场景进行30天概念验证，显示跨本体能力复用的初步可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人间复用能力仍需大量人工适配与验证：迁移技能往往需要重新工程化接口、重新调参并重新验证安全性。

Method: 将机器人视为自身能力演化的主动主体，构建 Silicon Language 框架：机器人把自身经验编码为知识包并发布，检索同伴知识包，针对自身传感器与执行器进行翻译，并通过独立本地试验进行审查；接收端可用性评估使机器人自行判断外部知识包是否有用，渐进式融合与自动回滚用于降低负迁移风险。系统结合三层基础设施（边缘智能体、Silicon Transfer Protocol, STP、知识中枢）以及受人类学术体系启发的 capability stack。

Result: 在玉林一处地上模拟矿井实验室开展30天概念验证，随后在户外沙路和室内工厂地面试验。两个异构机器人通过操作员辅助的文件拷贝、经 Silicon Language 翻译层中介，编码并翻译了三个跨本体能力；在 Taurus 上记录了 dust-locked following 行为的源端试验。项目记录显示单能力适配时间从数天降至数小时，但作者将其表述为描述性部署记录而非受控测量。

Conclusion: 该部署为跨本体知识交换提供了初步证据；舰队级自主演化以及有无知识包的受控对比仍属未来工作。

Abstract: Reusing a capability across heterogeneous robots still requires substantial human adaptation and verification: transferring a skill often means re-engineering interfaces, retuning parameters, and re-validating safety. We introduce Silicon Language, a robot-native knowledge exchange framework that treats the robot as the active subject of its own capability evolution. In this framework, a robot that wants a capability encodes its own experience into knowledge packets, publishes them, retrieves peer packets, translates them for its own sensors and actuators, and reviews them through independent local trial. A receiver-side usability evaluation procedure lets each robot decide for itself whether an external packet is useful, and progressive blending with automatic rollback is designed to reduce the risk of negative transfer when adopting it. The system combines three infrastructure layers (edge agent, Silicon Transfer Protocol (STP), and knowledge hub) with a capability stack inspired by the human scholarly system. We report a 30-day proof-of-concept deployment at an above-ground simulated-mine laboratory in Yulin, with following trials on an outdoor sand road and an indoor factory floor. Two heterogeneous robots encoded and translated three capabilities across embodiments through operator-assisted file copies mediated by the Silicon Language translation layer; source-side trials of the dust-locked following behavior were recorded on Taurus. Project records indicate that per-capability adaptation time dropped from days to hours; we present these figures as descriptive deployment records rather than controlled measurements. The deployment provides initial evidence for cross-embodiment knowledge exchange; fleet-level autonomous evolution and controlled with/without-packet comparisons remain future work.

</details>


### [81] [OntoPlan: An Ontology-Grounded Scene Representation and Agentic Framework for Scalable Robot Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07649)
*Hyeongwoo Nam,Woongje Cho,Juwon Kim,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出本体驱动的场景表示与OntoPlan智能体框架，解决LLM机器人在大场景长程任务中空间上下文丢失、token开销大、动作前提难满足的问题；在150个任务上成功率达0.89，基线最高0.27，且token减少约5.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的机器人任务规划在开放指令跟随上有前景，但在大环境长程任务中性能下降。文本传递空间信息时LLM难以捕捉空间上下文，且token成本随环境规模增长；直接用LLM生成动作序列也难以满足当前世界状态与动作前提条件。

Method: 构建本体支撑的场景表示，将物体、空间、关系、状态对齐到共享符号词汇表，用于空间推理与任务规划；提出OntoPlan智能体框架，解析指令，选择性检索任务相关信息，形式化目标与约束，并生成可执行计划。

Result: 在覆盖5个室内环境和3种场景规模的150个通用任务上，OntoPlan平均任务成功率为0.89，最强基线为0.27；每任务平均总token为18.1k，比最高效基线少约5.6倍。随场景规模增大，其优势保持，而先前方法成功率下降更明显且token成本更高。对模糊或不可行指令，OntoPlan能通过追问或报告信息不足来恰当响应，而非生成无效计划。

Conclusion: 本体驱动的场景表示与OntoPlan框架能有效提升LLM机器人长程任务规划的成功率、效率和鲁棒性，并在环境规模扩大时保持优势；代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based robot task planning is promising for open-ended instruction following, but degrades on long-horizon tasks in large environments. When spatial information is conveyed to the LLM through text, the model can fail to capture spatial context, and token cost grows with environment size. Generating action sequences directly with an LLM also makes it difficult to satisfy the current world state and action preconditions. We address this with an ontology-grounded scene representation that aligns objects, spaces, relations, and states in a shared symbolic vocabulary for spatial reasoning and task planning, and with OntoPlan, an agentic framework that interprets instructions, selectively retrieves task-relevant information, formalizes goals and constraints, and produces executable plans. Across 150 general tasks spanning five indoor environments and three scene scales, OntoPlan achieves 0.89 average task success, compared with 0.27 for the strongest baseline, while using 18.1k total tokens per task on average, about 5.6$\times$ fewer than the most efficient baseline. These advantages persist as scene scale increases, whereas prior methods degrade more sharply in success and remain far more costly in tokens. OntoPlan also responds appropriately to ambiguous or infeasible instructions by asking follow-up questions or reporting insufficient information rather than committing to invalid plans. Code available at this https URL .

</details>


### [82] [Beyond Task Reward: A Controller-Restriction Protocol for Evaluating Embodiment-Dependent Competence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07629)
*Siyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出用“受限控制能力”评估协同设计机器人形态：在明确声明的低复杂度控制器族、环境、任务与搜索预算下，记录形态保留的任务胜任度。该指标与常规任务奖励只弱相关，且依赖所声明的控制器族；它可作为形态的可报告属性，但必须结合定义它的控制器族来解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同设计通常以完全优化后的“形态-控制器”配对的任务奖励作为唯一评价标准，但单一奖励无法区分那些对控制器依赖程度差异很大的形态。因此需要一个能显式刻画形态在控制器受限时仍能保留多少任务能力的评价量。

Method: 将形态评估定义为：限制其控制器，在明确声明的低复杂度控制器族、环境、任务和搜索预算下测量其保留的任务胜任度。在三个 EvoGym 运动任务上，将该指标与任务奖励及几何描述符比较，并用运行分组交叉验证检验预测性；用 ICC(2,k) 检验优化器重启下的可靠性；通过改变控制器族（如按执行器索引而非位置排序来设定驱动相位）检验指标对族定义的依赖性；最后将其作为第二搜索目标，或用于仅奖励搜索后的最高奖励带内选择。

Result: 在三个 EvoGym 任务中，任务奖励仅解释该受限控制能力方差的 39%、33% 和 10%，几何描述符在运行分组交叉验证下不能预测该指标。测量在优化器重启间可靠（ICC(2,k)=0.956–0.986），但依赖所声明的控制器族：按执行器索引而非位置排序定义驱动相位时，相同形态的排序 Spearman 相关仅为 0.50–0.63，并会逆转奖励匹配的形态对。作为第二搜索目标，在匹配任务奖励下，5 次配对运行中 3 次提升能力，未达到预注册的 4 次标准。作为仅奖励搜索后的选择准则，在 8 次提供选择的运行中均选出不同形态，奖励代价至多 0.10，其中 6/8 在留出控制器族下得分更高。

Conclusion: 受限控制能力是协同设计形态的一种可报告属性，但它只能在定义它的控制器族、任务与环境语境中解释；单独的任务奖励不足以表征形态对控制器的依赖程度。

Abstract: Co-design methods optimize a robot's body and controller jointly and judge the result by one number, the task reward of the fully optimized pair. That number cannot separate morphologies whose competence depends on the controller to very different degrees. We evaluate a morphology by restricting its controller instead, recording the task competence it retains under an explicitly declared, low-complexity controller family, environment, task and search budget. On three EvoGym locomotion tasks, task reward explains only $39\%$, $33\%$ and $10\%$ of the variance in this quantity, and geometric descriptors do not predict it under run-grouped cross-validation. The measurement is reliable across optimizer restarts (ICC$(2,k) = 0.956$--$0.986$) but depends on the declared family: phasing the drive by actuator index instead of position ranks the same morphologies at Spearman $0.50$--$0.63$ and reverses reward-matched pairs. As a second search objective the axis improved competence at matched task reward in $3$ of $5$ paired runs, short of a pre-registered bar of $4$. Used after an ordinary reward-only search instead, to choose within its top task-reward band, it selected a different body in all $8$ runs offering a choice, at a cost of at most $0.10$ reward units, and in $6$ of $8$ that body also scored higher under a held-out family. Restricted-control competence is therefore a reportable property of a co-designed morphology, interpretable only with the controller family that defines it.

</details>


### [83] [TacZero: Training-Free Peg Insertion Using a General-Purpose Vision-Language Model with Tactile Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07621)
*Kazutoshi Tanaka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TacZero：利用预训练通用视觉-语言模型（VLM）融合视觉与触觉观测，直接选择机器人动作，无需额外的触觉/操作训练或任务特定规则；在真实圆柱销插入任务中，有数值触觉输入时成功率为15/20，无触觉输入时为10/20。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人若能从语言指令和感官观测中自主决定动作，就可在无任务特定训练或手工规则的情况下完成新的富接触操作任务。这类任务要求机器人推断物体间接触及由此产生的运动，并据此选择动作；现有方法通常依赖设计估计模型与触觉反馈控制律，或从触觉数据学习物体运动估计、动作结果预测与动作选择。

Method: 提出 TacZero，使用预训练通用 VLM 解释视觉和触觉观测并选择机器人动作，不进行额外触觉或操作训练，也不使用任务特定的接触解释或动作选择规则。系统向 VLM 提供相机图像、机器人状态以及表示为数值或叠加在图像上的向量形式的三轴触觉响应；基于这些观测和交互历史，VLM 生成指定目标末端执行器位置及夹爪开/闭的命令，由底层控制器执行。

Result: 在真实世界圆柱销插入实验中，TacZero 在使用数值触觉输入时20次试验成功15次；不使用触觉输入时20次试验成功10次。

Conclusion: 该研究为使用通用 VLM 进行富接触操作提供了具体起点，并指出了该方向面临的挑战。

Abstract: Robots that autonomously determine their actions from language instructions and sensory observations could perform new contact-rich manipulation tasks without task-specific training or hand-designed rules. To perform these tasks, robots must infer how objects contact one another and move as a result, then select actions. For contact inference and action selection, prior approaches involve designing estimation models and tactile feedback control laws, or learning models for object-motion estimation, action-outcome prediction, and action selection from tactile data. Instead, we propose TacZero, which uses a pretrained general-purpose vision-language model (VLM) to interpret visual and tactile observations and select robot actions without additional tactile or manipulation training or task-specific rules for contact interpretation or action selection. TacZero provides the VLM with camera images, robot state, and three-axis tactile responses represented as numerical values or vectors overlaid on the images. From these observations and interaction history, the VLM generates commands specifying target end-effector positions and gripper opening or closing, which a low-level controller executes. In real-world cylindrical-peg insertion experiments, TacZero succeeded in 15 of 20 trials with numerical tactile input, compared with 10 of 20 without tactile input. This study provides a concrete starting point for further research on contact-rich manipulation using general-purpose VLMs and highlights challenges in pursuing this direction.

</details>


### [84] [Nine Trials to Recover: A Reproducible Benchmark for Repertoire-Free Soft-Robot Damage Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07616)
*Siyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无策略库的软体机器人损伤恢复基准，并用GP-EI参考控制器在九次在线试验中验证其在新机体上的适应优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人损伤后恢复需要可复现、可公平比较的在线适应基准；现有方法常依赖预设策略库，且方法开发与评估混淆，难以衡量学习到的损伤先验及未来适应方法的增益。

Method: 构建无策略库基准：九次在线试验，同形态内配对损伤掩码，保留实测标称回退，分离方法开发与新机体评估。参考控制器GP-EI用三个初始化探针和六个反馈选择rollout调整执行器相位；在两组不相交75机体队列中与随机搜索、Sobol、CEM、CMA-ES等预算比较，并含TuRBO式局部GP；另做确认后固定控制器回放和冻结开发压力测试。

Result: GP-EI在确认集75机体中69个提升最差掩码增益，较四个基线平均最差掩码奖励高0.235-0.310；TuRBO式局部GP最接近，配对置信区间含零。确认后回放平均恢复5.06体素宽度，最差掩码p99几何边缘应变增0.0031，严格无额外需求部署门控保留48.7%平均增益。冻结压力测试物理移除10%占据体素时，GP-EI提升71/75机体，较官方BoTorch TuRBO平均最差掩码增益高0.356。

Conclusion: 该可回放控制器、机体级推断和冻结队列评估为衡量学习损伤先验的附加价值及未来适应方法提供了参考基准。

Abstract: We present a repertoire-free benchmark for soft-robot damage recovery under nine online trials. The protocol pairs damage masks within each morphology, retains a measured nominal fallback, and separates method development from evaluation on new bodies. A Gaussian-process expected-improvement (GP-EI) reference controller adapts actuator phases using three initialization probes and six feedback-selected rollouts. Across two disjoint 75-body cohorts, it outperforms random search, Sobol, CEM, and CMA-ES under equal budgets. GP-EI improves the worst-mask gain on 69/75 confirmation bodies and exceeds these four baselines by 0.235-0.310 mean worst-mask reward. A TuRBO-style local GP is the closest comparator; the paired confidence interval includes zero. Post-confirmation fixed-controller replay finds 5.06 voxel widths of mean recovery together with a 0.0031 increase in worst-mask p99 geometric edge strain; a strict no-added-demand deployment gate retains 48.7% of mean gain.
