Motivation: 现有世界动作模型虽联合学习视觉预测与动作，但视频和动作损失未显式约束动作序列造成的几何后果；同时，经时间注意力后的视觉特征可能包含未来观测，不适合作为辅助预测器唯一的当前视觉输入。

Method: ACG-WAM 引入 ACG-JEPA：从当前观测和中间动作预测多个时间跨度的几何特征，目标为冻结 VGGT 对当前/未来图像对编码的未来 slot。该方法将头相机与腕相机监督施加于时间混合前的共享视觉嵌入，并在推理时移除教师与辅助模块。

Result: 在 50 个 RoboTwin 2.0 任务中，ACG-WAM 在干净场景成功率达 93.46%，随机化最佳成功率为 92.68%，两种设置平均成功率为 93.07%，均为对比方法最优；在真实机器人三个任务上，成功率为 85.00%，部分完成分为 91.67%，分别超过 Motus 10.00 和 9.17 个百分点。

Conclusion: ACG-JEPA 通过动作条件几何联合嵌入预测，增强世界动作模型对动作几何后果的建模能力，且不增加推理开销，在仿真和真实机器人任务上均取得领先表现，并已开源代码。

Abstract: World action models jointly learn visual predictionand robot actions, providing a way to use observations ofscene evolution for policy learning. Their video and actionlosses, however, provide no explicit target for the geometricconsequences of a demonstrated action sequence. Moreover,visual features taken after temporal attention can contain futureobservations, making them unsuitable as the sole current visualinput to an auxiliary predictor. We introduce ACG-WAMand its auxiliary objective, the Action-Conditioned GeometricJoint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (ACG-JEPA), whichpredicts geometric features at several horizons from the currentobservation and intervening actions, using the future slot of afrozen VGGT encoding of each current and future image pairas the target. We apply this supervision from the head and wristcameras to a shared visual embedding before temporal mixing,and remove the teacher and auxiliary modules at this http URL 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, ACG-WAM achieves 93.46%success in clean scenes, with the best randomized success(92.68%) and mean across both settings (93.07%) among thecompared methods; across three tasks on a real robot, itachieves 85.00% success and 91.67% partial completion score,exceeding Motus by 10.00 and 9.17 percentage points, respec-tively. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [123] [Learning Modular Policy for Multi-Floor Object Navigation:A Factorized Framework for Diagnostic Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06958)
*Shichao Zhai,Shuhao Ye,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对多楼层 ObjectNav 中长时程决策与稀疏奖励的挑战，提出模块化诊断框架，并用分层分解策略分析失败因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多楼层 ObjectNav 因长时程决策导致奖励稀疏，学习困难，需要有效策略来诊断多楼层场景中的失败原因。

Method: 设计分层因子化策略，将单一全局策略分解为楼层内探索策略和楼层间切换策略；通过蒸馏 VLM 的探索逻辑学习轻量楼层内策略，为强化学习提供有效初始化；并在理想假设下证明该因子化策略在策略表示层面等价于单一全局策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，感知性能和爬楼梯稳定性是多楼层导航的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 分层因子化策略与 VLM 蒸馏可用于诊断多楼层 ObjectNav，并指出提升感知能力和楼梯攀爬稳定性是改善多楼层导航的关键方向。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation (ObjectNav) in multi-floor scenarios presents a challenge due to sparse rewards caused by long-horizon decision-making. In this paper, we propose a diagnostic study based on a modular framework with an effective learnable policy to analyze failure factors in multi-floor scenarios. To achieve an effective policy for diagnosis, we design the hierarchical factorization policy that deconstructs a single global policy into an intra-floor exploration policy and an inter-floor switching policy. To providing an effective initialization for Reinforcement Learning (RL), the lightweight intra-floor policy is learned by distilling the exploration logic of Visual Language Models (VLMs). Under idealized assumptions, we show that the factorized policy is theoretically equivalent to a single global policy at the policy-representation level. Experiment results indicate that perception performance and stair climbing stability are the primary bottlenecks in multi-floor navigation.

</details>


### [124] [ROMA: LLM System for Real-World Object-Centric Multi-Sensory Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06955)
*Ruoxuan Feng,Yutong Chen,Ruihua Song,Huan Yang,Zhongyuan Wang,Guocai Yao,Di Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的主动多感官感知系统ROMA，通过推理-交互-反馈闭环主动获取缺失证据，并构建数据集ROMI-2K与评测基准ROMA Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过主动交互理解物理世界，当感官证据不足时会决定缺失信息、获取方式及何时足够；现有机器人多感官系统仅被动融合感官输入，缺乏主动获取缺失证据的能力。

Method: ROMA集成视觉、音频、触觉与力觉，构建推理-交互-反馈闭环：LLM识别缺失证据，确定目标物体、交互动作与模态，由物理接口执行并采集多感官反馈。构建ROMI-2K数据集（近2000物体、6种原子交互、同步感官反馈），设计两阶段训练框架对齐多模态并训练LLM评估证据充分性、选择信息性交互、推理反馈；将主动感知建模为感知链，并建立ROMA Bench评测单属性、长程多属性及意图驱动的主动感知。

Result: 实验表明ROMA能主动获取缺失证据，解决现有方法难以处理的复杂长链多感官感知任务。

Conclusion: 为主动多感官具身智能体奠定感知基础。

Abstract: Humans inherently understand the physical world through an active process. When sensory evidence is insufficient to infer physical properties, we naturally interact with the environment by deciding what information is missing, how to acquire it, and when sufficient evidence has been obtained. In stark contrast, existing multi-sensory robot systems mainly integrate sensory inputs rather than actively acquiring missing evidence through interactions. In this work, we introduce ROMA, an LLM-based system for Real-World Object-Centric Multi-Sensory Active Perception. ROMA integrates vision, audio, tactile, and force sensing into a reasoning-interaction-feedback loop. The model identifies missing evidence and determines the target objects, interactions, and modalities, while a physical interface executes the selected interactions and collects the multi-sensory feedback. To support this capability, we construct ROMI-2K, a large-scale real-world multi-sensory object interaction dataset covering nearly 2,000 objects and 6 atomic interactions with synchronized sensory feedback. Building on these data, we develop a two-stage training framework that aligns sensory modalities and equips the LLM to assess evidence sufficiency, select informative interactions, and reason over the multi-sensory feedback. We further characterize active perception as perception chains, where acquired evidence guides subsequent interactions and reasoning, and establish ROMA Bench to evaluate single-attribute, long-horizon multi-attribute, and intent-driven active perception. Experiments show that ROMA can actively acquire missing evidence and solve complex, long-chain multi-sensory perception tasks that existing methods struggle to handle, laying a strong perceptual foundation for active multi-sensory embodied agents.

</details>


### [125] [Physical Twins: Accelerating and Enabling Robot Learning with Phantom Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06929)
*Elizabeth Peiros,Derek Chen,Lucas Yager,Edward Lee,Kevin Wang,Zoe Samuels,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进的 phantom 物理孪生设备，可复现肩部球窝运动及肩胛、前伸/后缩运动，用于人机物理交互（pHRI）算法的真实世界强化学习式测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改善人机物理交互对物理治疗、搜救和远程医疗具有重要意义，但直接在人类身上开展实验面临安全、IRB 审批、参与者招募、可重复性、可扩展性和参与者多样性等限制。

Method: 设计改进的 phantom 设备作为物理孪生体，除肩关节球窝运动外，还渲染肩胛运动与前伸/后缩运动；该设备支持面向物理交互算法的真实世界 RL 型测试。

Result: 实验表明该设备能够渲染多种 3D 关节限位，并演示 Franka Panda 机械臂感知关节限位，作为手臂与设备交互，用于 ADLs（日常生活活动）和 pHRI 任务。

Conclusion: 该物理孪生设备可在避免人类实验限制的同时，为 pHRI 算法提供可重复、可扩展的真实世界测试平台。

Abstract: Improvements in human-robot physical interaction (pHRI) can have major implications for physical therapy, search and rescue, and telemedicine. However, a major challenge concerns human constraints and safety in human-robot physical experiments. Concerns about human studies also include repeatability, scalability, and participant diversity. To conduct such experiments, an IRB and willing human participants are required. In this work, we present an improved phantom device, a physical twin, that enables real-world RL-type testing for physically interactive algorithms. The new device not only replicates the ball-and-socket motion of the shoulder but also renders scapular and protraction/retraction motions. The experiments showcase the device's ability to render multiple 3D joint limits and demonstrate a Franka Panda arm sensing limits and interacting with the device as an arm for ADLs (activities of daily living) and pHRI tasks.

</details>


### [126] [SWAP: Stepwise Action Policy Routing for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06926)
*Mousumi Das,Aditeya Prajapati,Abrar Anwar,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SWAP 将多个 VLA 策略的动态组合建模为离线强化学习路由问题，在每个决策步根据当前观测选择最合适的策略，从而提升机器人操作成功率并缩短成功轨迹的动作步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) 模型的机器人操作系统通常仅使用单个 VLA 执行任务，但单个 VLA 在不同任务状态和环境中表现不佳，因此需要一种能够在执行过程中动态组合多个 VLA 策略的方法。

Method: 提出 StepWise Action Policy Routing (SWAP) 框架，将策略路由形式化为离线强化学习问题，学习一个路由 critic，在每一决策步根据当前观测选择最合适的策略；使机器人能够在线切换新策略，而非在整个 episode 中固定使用单一策略。

Result: 在真实世界 DROID 操作任务和 LIBERO 仿真实验中评估 SWAP。相比固定策略执行和路由基线，SWAP 在真实世界任务成功率上取得最高 33% 的绝对提升，并将成功轨迹的机器人动作步长减少 28.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，逐步动态路由多个 VLA 策略优于固定策略与现有路由基线，能够提升机器人操作任务的成功率与执行效率。

Abstract: Robot manipulation systems using Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model backbones typically use just one VLA for task execution. However, individual VLAs do not perform well across different task states and environments. We introduce a framework for dynamically composing multiple VLA policies during execution: StepWise Action Policy Routing (SWAP). SWAP formulates policy routing as an offline reinforcement learning problem, learning a routing critic that selects the most appropriate policy at each decision step given the current observation. SWAP enables robots to select new policies to execute online rather than committing to a single policy for the duration of an episode. We evaluate SWAP on both real-world DROID manipulation tasks and LIBERO simulation experiments. SWAP improves over fixed-policy execution and routing baselines, giving absolute improvements in real-world task success up to 33% while reducing successful trajectory robot action step length by 28.3%.

</details>


### [127] [Does a Learned Corrector Beat a Simple Retreat? Evidence from a Frozen VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06921)
*Chenchao Sheng,Zhuang Jiang,Liuhaichen Yang,Ningwei Bai,Zezhi Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在冻结的VLA策略π0.5上，用配对安慰剂与简单回退基线评估运行时恢复：仅beat_block_hammer有+13.5pp净增益，其他三个RoboTwin任务无可检测增益；简单固定触发/脚本回退与学习恢复相当，需区分真实改进与基策略随机波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署运行时恢复前，必须验证干预是否超越普通run-to-run波动，以及复杂学习纠正是否比简单动作更有价值。

Method: 在四个RoboTwin任务上评估冻结π0.5。每个测试seed配对有/无纠正的rollout，并加入同seed基策略重跑作为安慰剂；使用seed-cluster区间和预设比较规则评估净增益。还比较固定时间触发+脚本回到早期关节配置、暂停、常量动作等控制基线。

Result: 3,888个配对episode中，完整pipeline在beat_block_hammer成功率提升+13.5pp（95%区间[+9.4,+17.7]），其他三任务在部署权重下无可检测增益。响应任务失败基episode中，普通重跑43.2%成功，纠正后63.5%，说明许多名义救援来自基策略自身波动。固定时间触发+脚本回退在两轮中产生净增益，且与学习pipeline无检测差异，但预设等价标准未一致满足；暂停和常量动作控制无相当增益。

Conclusion: 该benchmark上是否干预强烈依赖任务；需要配对安慰剂和简单回退基线，才能确认学习纠正的真实贡献。

Abstract: Before deploying runtime recovery for a frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policy, one must establish that an intervention improves success beyond ordinary run-to-run variation and that its complexity adds value over a simple action. We evaluate these questions on frozen $\pi_{0.5}$ across four RoboTwin tasks. For each test seed, we pair rollouts with and without correction and include a same-seed base-policy re-run as a placebo. Seed-cluster intervals and prespecified comparison rules assess net gains against stochastic outcome changes. Across 3,888 paired episodes, the full pipeline raises success on beat_allowbreak block_allowbreak hammer by $+13.5$\,pp (95\% interval $[+9.4,+17.7]$), with no detectable gain on the other three tasks at the deployed weight. Among failed base episodes on the responsive task, $43.2\%$ succeed on a plain re-run, compared with $63.5\%$ after correction; many nominal rescues therefore reflect the base policy's own variability. A fixed-time trigger and scripted return to an earlier joint configuration produce a net gain with no detected difference from the learned pipeline across two rounds, although our prespecified equivalence criterion is not met consistently. Pausing and a constant-action control do not yield comparable gains. On this benchmark, the decision to intervene depends strongly on the task, and a paired placebo plus a simple retreat baseline are needed to establish what learned correction contributes.

</details>


### [128] [Geometric Coherence via Weighted Matching for 3D Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Reach-Avoid Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06882)
*Prajwal Vijay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究3D异构多智能体到达-规避博弈中的分配质量，指出仅按基数匹配会导致“Geometric Sprawl”失败模式，并提出以HJI拦截值为次级权重的基数优先加权序列匹配方法，在基准上显著减少超时并提升捕获与拦截高度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在几何结构明显的场景中，最大基数分配往往不唯一，其中部分分配会产生空间不连贯配对和低效追捕轨迹；仅优化匹配数量不足以保证分配质量。

Method: 基于Yan等人的逃避空间框架，提出基数优先的加权序列匹配：以Hamilton-Jacobi-Isaacs拦截值 z_I(s,j) 作为次级分配权重，每一序列阶段用最小费用最大流求解；无权重基线使用同一求解器但去掉权重项。

Result: 在包含7个族、5种初始化变体的确定性35场景基准上，诊断性静止未匹配设置中，加权方法解决了15个几何压力测试实例中的13个，而这些实例使无权重基线反复超时；混合鞍点设置中，平均捕获数从3.20提升到3.91（+22%），平均拦截高度从3.87提升到5.36（+40%），并报告更低路径曲折度和角努力代理值。

Conclusion: 结果表明，以HJI拦截值为权重的基数优先序列匹配能缓解几何结构场景下的Geometric Sprawl，改善追捕轨迹平滑性与拦截性能；作者发布模拟器与基准套件以支持可复现评估。

Abstract: We study assignment quality in 3D heterogeneous multi-agent reach-avoid games and identify a recurring failure mode of cardinality-only matching in geometrically structured scenarios, which we term \emph{Geometric Sprawl}. In these cases, multiple maximum-cardinality assignments are available, but some induce spatially incoherent pairings and inefficient pursuit trajectories. Building on the evasion-space framework of Yan et al.~\cite{yan2022}, we introduce a cardinality-first weighted sequential matching method in which the Hamilton--Jacobi--Isaacs interception value $z_I(s,j)$ is used as a secondary assignment weight. Each sequential stage is solved with a min-cost max-flow backend, while the unweighted baseline uses the same solver with the weight term removed. We evaluate both methods on a deterministic 35-scenario benchmark (7 families & 5 initialization variants) under two regimes: a diagnostic stationary-unmatched setting and a hybrid saddle-point setting with goal-directed unmatched evaders. On this benchmark, the weighted method resolves 13 of 15 geometric stress-test instances that cause repeated timeouts for the unweighted baseline in the diagnostic regime, and under the hybrid regime improves mean captures from $3.20$ to $3.91$ (22\% increase) and mean interception height from $3.87$ to $5.36$ (40\% increase). We also report lower path tortuosity and lower angular-effort proxy values, suggesting smoother pursuit trajectories in this first-order simulation model. We release the simulator and benchmark suite at \href{ this https URL }{ this http URL } to support reproducible evaluation of assignment strategies for 3D reach-avoid games.

</details>


### [129] [RMRRT: Riemannian Barrier Metric RRT for Inequality-Aware Steering on Equality Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06863)
*Minhyeong Kang,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Riemannian Barrier Metric RRT (RMRRT)，将等式与不等式约束统一到单一几何度量中，用于高维机器人采样规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统采样规划中，等式约束通常通过投影处理，不等式约束则依赖碰撞检测等二值有效性检查，二者分离导致探索效率低。

Method: RMRRT 先由不等式敏感障碍项构建环境障碍度量，再通过等式约束相关的 G-正交投影诱导切空间度量；该度量一致用于 steering 与最近邻选择，使探索远离不等式边界并保持一阶等式一致性；用基于 signed distance 的几何代理不等式实例化度量，硬可行性仍由标准有效性检查保证。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境多种约束操作任务中达到 100% 成功率，并较代表性约束规划基线减少规划时间；消融表明该度量减少拒绝样本、缩短路径长度，提升探索质量。

Conclusion: 统一几何度量能有效耦合等式与不等式约束，提升约束采样规划的探索效率与成功率，代码与实验视频已公开。

Abstract: This paper presents a motion planning framework that unifies equality and inequality constraints within a single geometric formulation for sampling-based planning in high-dimensional robotic systems. In conventional sampling-based planners, equality constraints are typically enforced through projection, whereas inequality constraints are handled separately through binary validity checks such as collision testing, often leading to inefficient exploration. To address this limitation, we propose Riemannian Barrier Metric RRT (RMRRT), which constructs a unified local geometry for planning on equality-constrained manifolds. RMRRT first builds an ambient barrier metric from inequality-sensitive barrier terms and then induces a tangent-space metric via a (G)-orthogonal projection associated with the equality constraints. The resulting tangent-space metric is used consistently in both steering and nearest-neighbor selection, biasing exploration away from nearby inequality boundaries while preserving first-order equality consistency. In this work, the metric is instantiated from signed-distance-based geometric proxy inequalities to provide collision-informative tangent-space directions; hard feasibility is enforced separately through standard validity checks. Experimental results show that RMRRT achieves a 100% success rate across diverse constrained manipulation tasks in both simulation and real-world settings, while reducing planning time relative to representative constrained planning baselines. Ablation studies further demonstrate that the proposed metric improves exploration quality by reducing rejected samples and shortening path length. Experiment videos and source code are available at: this https URL

</details>

### [130] [VeriFine: Scaling Verification for Self-Improvement in Embodied Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08761)
*Zewei Zhou,Rachel Luo,Yulong Cao,Chaowei Xiao,Chensheng Peng,Boyi Li,Thomas Tian,Zheng Lian,Yan Wang,Jiaqi Ma,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Wenhao Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 VeriFine，一个通过策略、训练课程与评判器协同演化来扩展验证的智能体框架，实现具身推理中策略与评判器的持续自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我改进策略不断暴露新的失败模式，固定评判器会限制优化反馈和有效训练样本发现；具身推理更要求可靠评估空间 grounding、因果推理与安全决策，使验证成为关键瓶颈。

Method: VeriFine 包含策略改进循环：使用 rubric 评判器诊断重复失败、构建自适应课程并优化策略。当进展停滞且验证成为瓶颈时，评判器改进循环选择性查询人类对信息丰富失败案例的指导，并通过 coactive calibration 精炼评判器，即人机解决分歧并收敛到物理推理的客观 rubric；更新后的评判器指导下一阶段数据选择与策略优化。

Result: 在驾驶与机器人导航任务上，跨强化学习与监督微调，策略和评判器能力均实现持续自我改进。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，扩展验证能够支持策略失败模式演化下的持续自我改进。

Abstract: Self-improving policies continually expose new failure patterns, changing what their judges must be able to verify. However, current fixed judges constrain both optimization feedback and the discovery of useful training examples, limiting further self-improvement. This challenge is even more acute in embodied reasoning, where reliable evaluation must account for spatial grounding, causal reasoning, and safety-aware decision-making. We introduce VeriFine, an agent harness framework that scales verification through the co-evolution of the policy, training curriculum, and judge. The Policy Improvement Loop uses a rubric judge to diagnose recurring failures, construct an adaptive curriculum, and optimize the policy. When progress plateaus and verification becomes a bottleneck, the Judge Improvement Loop selectively queries human guidance on informative failure cases and refines the judge through coactive calibration, in which humans and agents resolve disagreements and converge toward the objective rubric of physical reasoning. The revised judge then guides the next stage of data selection and policy optimization. Experiments on driving and robot navigation tasks demonstrate continuous self-improvement in both policy and judge capability across reinforcement and supervised fine-tuning. These results show how scaling verification supports continuous self-improvement as policy failure patterns evolve.

</details>


### [131] [Sim-to-Real Transfer of Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments Using an Ackermann-Steered Mobile Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07192)
*Chalindu Abeywansa,Sahan Gunasekara,Devindi De Silva,Seniru Dissanayake,Ranga Rodrigo,Peshala Jayasekara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该工作将一种面向连续环境、无需导航图、全景视图和完美定位的视觉语言导航（VLN）方法从仿真迁移到真实机器人，通过跨模态注意力、线性光度调整和少量真实数据微调，在离线专用硬件上实现自然语言驱动机器人导航，并用SPL和nDTW验证其鲁棒性与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLN模型通常依赖导航图、360度全景视图和完美定位，这些假设在真实场景中难以满足，限制了VLN从仿真到现实世界的部署。因此，作者旨在实现一种无需上述假设、可在连续真实环境中运行的VLN系统，提升人机交互导航的实用性。

Method: 系统将视觉输入与语言指令对齐到共享嵌入空间，采用基于跨模态注意力（CMA）的架构。模型先在仿真环境中的现有数据集上训练，再使用自建Ackermann转向机器人（配备相机和LiDAR）采集的真实数据进行微调。训练中结合线性光度调整，并仅用有限数量的episode进行微调，最终在专用硬件上离线运行。

Result: 实验使用SPL（Success weighted by Path Length）和nDTW（Normalized Dynamic Time Warping）指标评估。结果表明，模型能够成功适应真实环境，实现有效导航，并展现出较好的鲁棒性和适应性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，通过CMA架构和仿真到真实的微调策略，VLN系统可以在不依赖导航图、全景视图和完美定位的情况下迁移到真实机器人，并支持离线部署，为自然语言驱动的实际机器人导航提供可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) enables robots to navigate through environments using natural language instructions, making human-robot interaction intuitive. Traditional VLN models often rely on navigation graphs, 360-degree views, and perfect localization which pose significant challenges when adapting these models to real-world settings. This work addresses these limitations by performing a simulation-to-real domain shift of a VLN approach that operates in continuous environments without requiring navigation graphs or panoramic views. The proposed system integrates vision-language models that align visual inputs and linguistic instructions within a shared embedding space, facilitating natural language-driven navigation. We employ a Cross-Modal Attention (CMA) based architecture trained on an existing dataset in a simulated environment and fine-tune it using real-world data collected from a custom-built Ackermann-steered robot equipped with a camera and a LiDAR sensor. By utilising linear photometric adjustments and fine-tuning on a limited number of episodes, our model successfully adapts to real-world environments, achieving effective navigation while running offline on dedicated hardware. Experimental results, evaluated using Success weighted by Path Length (SPL) and Normalized Dynamic Time Warping (nDTW) metrics, demonstrate the robustness and adaptability of our approach. Keywords: Vision-Language Navigation, Cross-Modal Attention, Natural Language Instructions, Sim-to-Real Transfer, Autonomous Navigation, Ackermann-steering.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [MRPilot: Supervising and Intervening LLM-Based Multi-Robot Teams through Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07477)
*Xiaoran Yang,Xun Qian,Yang Zhan,Nathan Tran,Ziyi Liu,Qiao Jin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了混合现实系统MRPilot，用四个监督与干预阶段帮助非专家用户检查、审查、监控和修复基于大语言模型的多机器人系统任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽支持用户用自然语言指挥异构多机器人系统，但任务解释、机器人分配与协调过程难以被检查和修改，导致透明性、可控性和信任不足。

Method: 基于12名非专家用户的形成性研究，设计MRPilot：将机器人团队计划与执行状态表示为结构化承诺，并在同步的场景视图与总览视图中共享；围绕四个阶段支持用户：Forming（消解歧义指代）、Reviewing（执行前审查计划）、Following（监控分布式执行）、Repairing（出现问题时进行机器人级或团队级修改）。

Result: 在20名参与者、虚拟现实模拟家庭环境中的被试内研究中，与使用相同LLM规划器和机器人能力的传统LLM对话界面相比，MRPilot降低了工作负荷，并提高了情境意识、透明度、信任和感知控制。

Conclusion: 研究为基于LLM的多机器人系统提供了多尺度干预、自适应监督和校准依赖方面的设计启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) let users direct heterogeneous multi-robot systems (MRS) through natural language, but make task interpretation, robot assignment, and coordination difficult to inspect and change. Based on a formative study with 12 non-expert users, we developed MRPilot, a mixed reality system organized around four stages of supervision and intervention. MRPilot represents robot-team plans and execution states as structured commitments shared across synchronized situated and overview views. Across four stages, it helps users resolve ambiguous references (Forming), review plans before execution (Reviewing), monitor distributed execution (Following), and make robot-level or team-level changes when problems arise (Repairing). In a within-subjects study with 20 participants in a virtual reality-simulated home, MRPilot reduced workload, increased situational awareness, transparency, trust, and perceived control compared with a conventional LLM-based conversational interface using the same LLM planner and robot capabilities. We provide design implications for multi-scale intervention, adaptive supervision, and calibrated reliance in LLM-based MRS.

</details>


### [133] [Educating future engineers about LLMs: A scalable workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07027)
*R. Zhang,J. C. F. de Winter,T. Dicke,D. Dodou,Y. B. Eisma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出一个可扩展的游戏化工作坊，让工程硕士生通过移动网页界面与LLM协作完成导航规划任务，并在Boston Dynamics Spot机器人上演示。226名学生参与后，问卷显示学习效果和参与度均较高，提示策略也随LLM类型和任务复杂度而变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型日益融入工程工作流，学生需要获得动手经验，以学习如何批判性地与LLM协作。

Method: 设计面向工程硕士的10场工作坊，共226名学生使用移动网页界面，为非推理型和推理型LLM编写提示，解决复杂度递增的网格导航任务；系统返回机器人可执行计划、轨迹可视化和自动评分，并最终进行Spot机器人现场演示。

Result: 课后问卷中，81.5%的学生报告有显著学习收获，91.0%报告高参与度；提示分析表明，学生从为非推理型LLM提供逐步指令，转向为复杂问题解决任务提供更高层次指导。

Conclusion: 此类交互式“仿真到现实”环境可用于教授负责任使用LLM所需的验证与协作技能。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into engineering workflows, students require hands-on experience to learn how to collaborate with them critically. This paper presents a scalable gamified workshop designed for engineering Master's students to practice human-AI collaboration in navigation planning. Using a mobile web interface across 10 workshop sessions, a total of 226 students wrote prompts for a non-reasoning and a reasoning LLM to solve grid-based navigation tasks of increasing complexity. The system returned robot-executable plans, trajectory visualizations, and automated scoring, culminating in a live demonstration on a Boston Dynamics Spot robot. In a post-workshop questionnaire, 81.5% reported substantial learning and 91.0% reported high engagement. Analysis of the submitted prompts revealed that students changed their strategies from step-by-step instructions for the non-reasoning LLM toward providing higher-level guidance for complex problem-solving tasks. We conclude that such interactive simulation-to-reality environments are viable for teaching the verification and collaboration skills necessary for responsible LLM use in engineering. Code is available at: this https URL

</details>
